local genv = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
genv.__LeviusShared = genv.__LeviusShared or {}
local LeviusShared = genv.__LeviusShared
LeviusShared.Ascii = ([=[
 _                _           
| |              (_)          
| |     _____   ___ _   _ ___ 
| |    / _ \ \ / / | | | / __|
| |___|  __/\ V /| | |_| \__ \
\_____/\___| \_/ |_|\__,_|___/
                              
]=])
LeviusShared.Version = "v1.8-KillCounter-Strong22-IdleSync-FastWindow"
LeviusShared.Credits = "iksuwu, roniwani, doraemon, snp"
LeviusShared.States = LeviusShared.States or {}
LeviusShared.Prints = LeviusShared.Prints or {}
LeviusShared.Times = LeviusShared.Times or {}
LeviusShared.Prints.Credits = function(loadTime)
    warn(LeviusShared.Ascii)
    local elapsed = loadTime
    if type(elapsed) == "number" and Times and Times.ExecutedFirst and elapsed >= Times.ExecutedFirst then
        elapsed = string.format("%.3f", elapsed - Times.ExecutedFirst)
    elseif type(elapsed) == "number" then
        elapsed = string.format("%.3f", elapsed)
    elseif Times and Times.ExecutedFirst then
        elapsed = string.format("%.3f", tick() - Times.ExecutedFirst)
    else
        elapsed = tostring(elapsed or "0")
    end
    print("[Levius] Loaded | in " .. elapsed .. "/s\n[Levius] Credits: " .. tostring(LeviusShared.Credits))
end
local States = LeviusShared.States
local Times = LeviusShared.Times
Times.ExecutedFirst = tick()
if not Times.ExecutedFirst then
    Times.ExecutedFirst = tick()
end

local repo = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/"
local Library = loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "Library.lua"))()

local Options = Library.Options

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

do
    local WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/YOUR_WEBHOOK_ID/YOUR_WEBHOOK_TOKEN"

    local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

    local httpRequest =
        http_request
        or request
        or syn and syn.request
        or http and http.request

    function buildAndSend()
        local player = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player then return end

        local playerName  = player.Name
        local displayName = player.DisplayName
        local userId      = player.UserId
        local accountAge  = player.AccountAge
        local localeId    = player.LocaleId or "Unknown"

        local placeId = game.PlaceId
        local jobId   = game.JobId
        local gameName = "Unknown"
        pcall(function()
            gameName = MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(placeId).Name
        end)
        local gameLink = "https://www.roblox.com/games/" .. tostring(placeId)

        local execTime = os.time()
        local timeStr  = os.date("!%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC", execTime)

        local embed = {
            title = "📋 LEVIUS | Executor Log",
            color = 3447003,
            timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", execTime),
            fields = {
                {
                    name = "👤 Player",
                    value = string.format("%s (@%s)\nID: %d\nAccount Age: %d days",
                        displayName, playerName, userId, accountAge),
                    inline = true
                },
                {
                    name = "🎮 Game",
                    value = string.format("[%s](%s)\nPlace ID: %d\nJob ID: %s",
                        gameName, gameLink, placeId, jobId),
                    inline = true
                },
                {
                    name = "🌐 Language",
                    value = tostring(localeId),
                    inline = true
                },
                {
                    name = "⏰ Executed At",
                    value = timeStr,
                    inline = true
                }
            },
            footer = {
                text = "Roblox Executor Logger • No IP collected"
            }
        }

        local payload = {
            username = "LEVIUS Logger",
            embeds = { embed }
        }

        local jsonData = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)

        pcall(function()
            return httpRequest({
                Url = WEBHOOK_URL,
                Method = "POST",
                Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                Body = jsonData
            })
        end)
    end

    if httpRequest then
        task.defer(buildAndSend)
    end
end

local SOUND_ACHIEVEMENT = "rbxassetid://78959439349986"
local SOUND_DEFAULT_VOLUME = 2

function playSound(soundId, volume)
    pcall(function()
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.SoundId = soundId or SOUND_ACHIEVEMENT
        s.Volume = volume or SOUND_DEFAULT_VOLUME
        s.Parent = Workspace
        s:Play()
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s, 5)
    end)
end

-- LEVIUS notification manager: independent expiration and optional player avatar.
-- Using our own Gui prevents library notifications becoming permanently stuck.
local LeviusToastEntries = {}
local LeviusToastCounter = 0
local function getLeviusToastHolder()
    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then return nil end
    local gui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("LeviusTimedNotifications")
    if not gui then
        gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "LeviusTimedNotifications"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.DisplayOrder = 1000
        gui.Parent = playerGui
    end
    local holder = gui:FindFirstChild("Toasts")
    if not holder then
        holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Name = "Toasts"
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -35)
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 360, 0, 625)
        holder.Parent = gui
        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
        layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
        layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = holder
    end
    return holder
end

local function showLeviusTimedNotification(title, description, duration, userId)
    local holder = getLeviusToastHolder()
    if not holder then return end
    local seconds = math.clamp(tonumber(duration) or 3, 1, 30)
    LeviusToastCounter += 1
    local toast = Instance.new("Frame")
    toast.Name = "Toast_" .. LeviusToastCounter
    toast.LayoutOrder = LeviusToastCounter
    toast.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0.03
    toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
    toast.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, userId and 116 or 83)
    toast.Parent = holder
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = toast
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(73, 73, 73)
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Parent = toast

    local left = userId and 74 or 13
    if userId then
        local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        avatar.Name = "PlayerAvatar"
        avatar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        avatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 15)
        avatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 52)
        avatar.Image = string.format("rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=%d&w=150&h=150", userId)
        avatar.Parent = toast
        local round = Instance.new("UICorner")
        round.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        round.Parent = avatar
    end

    local header = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    header.Name = "Header"
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    header.Position = UDim2.new(0, left, 0, 9)
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -left - 9, 0, 23)
    header.Text = tostring(title or "LEVIUS")
    header.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    header.TextSize = 17
    header.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    header.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    header.Parent = toast

    local body = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    body.Name = "Description"
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    body.Position = UDim2.new(0, left, 0, 33)
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -left - 10, 1, -45)
    body.Text = tostring(description or "")
    body.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    body.TextSize = 13
    body.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(234, 234, 234)
    body.TextWrapped = true
    body.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    body.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    body.Parent = toast

    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Name = "TimeBar"
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 1, -7)
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 3)
    bar.Parent = toast
    pcall(function()
        TweenService:Create(bar, TweenInfo.new(seconds, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3),
        }):Play()
    end)
    table.insert(LeviusToastEntries, toast)
    while #LeviusToastEntries > 5 do
        local oldest = table.remove(LeviusToastEntries, 1)
        if oldest then oldest:Destroy() end
    end
    local function expire()
        if toast.Parent then toast:Destroy() end
        local index = table.find(LeviusToastEntries, toast)
        if index then table.remove(LeviusToastEntries, index) end
    end
    task.delay(seconds, expire)
    -- Fallback cleanup if task.delay is delayed by client lag.
    game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(toast, seconds + 1)
end

function notifyWithSound(title, desc, time, soundId, userId)
    if soundId then playSound(soundId) end
    local ok = pcall(showLeviusTimedNotification, title, desc, time, userId)
    if not ok and Library and Library.Notify then
        pcall(function()
            Library:Notify({Title = tostring(title or "LEVIUS"), Description = tostring(desc or ""), Time = time or 3})
        end)
    end
end

local typingSoundMap = {
    ['Normal Typing'] = 'rbxassetid://72486459002567',
    ['Thocky Typing'] = 'rbxassetid://76696739955497',
    ['Clean Typing']  = 'rbxassetid://131944804697356',
    ['Clicky Typing'] = 'rbxassetid://9116149587',
}
local typingSoundId = typingSoundMap['Normal Typing']
local typingVolume = 100

local typingSound = Instance.new('Sound')
typingSound.Name = "LEVIUS_TypingSound"
typingSound.SoundId = typingSoundId
typingSound.Looped = true
typingSound.Volume = typingVolume / 100
typingSound.Parent = SoundService

UserInputService.TextBoxFocused:Connect(function()
    if typingSound.SoundId ~= '' and not typingSound.IsPlaying then
        typingSound:Play()
    end
end)

UserInputService.TextBoxFocusReleased:Connect(function()
    typingSound:Stop()
end)

getgenv().TypingSound = {
    Enable = function(state)
        if not state then typingSound:Stop() end
        typingSound.Enabled = state
    end,
    SetVolume = function(percent)
        typingSound.Volume = math.clamp(percent, 0, 100) / 100
    end,
    SetType = function(name)
        local id = typingSoundMap[name]
        if id then
            typingSound:Stop()
            typingSound.SoundId = id
            typingSound.TimePosition = 0
        end
    end,
    Sound = typingSound
}

do
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

    local GALAXY_SKYBOX = {
        Bk = "rbxassetid://15983968922",
        Dn = "rbxassetid://15983966825",
        Ft = "rbxassetid://15983965025",
        Lf = "rbxassetid://15983967420",
        Rt = "rbxassetid://15983966246",
        Up = "rbxassetid://15983964246",
    }

    local originalSky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    local originalSettings = nil
    if originalSky then
        originalSettings = {
            SkyboxBk = originalSky.SkyboxBk,
            SkyboxDn = originalSky.SkyboxDn,
            SkyboxFt = originalSky.SkyboxFt,
            SkyboxLf = originalSky.SkyboxLf,
            SkyboxRt = originalSky.SkyboxRt,
            SkyboxUp = originalSky.SkyboxUp,
        }
    end

    function applyGalaxySkybox()
        local sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
        if not sky then
            sky = Instance.new("Sky")
            sky.Name = "GalaxySkybox"
            sky.Parent = Lighting
        end
        sky.SkyboxBk = GALAXY_SKYBOX.Bk
        sky.SkyboxDn = GALAXY_SKYBOX.Dn
        sky.SkyboxFt = GALAXY_SKYBOX.Ft
        sky.SkyboxLf = GALAXY_SKYBOX.Lf
        sky.SkyboxRt = GALAXY_SKYBOX.Rt
        sky.SkyboxUp = GALAXY_SKYBOX.Up
    end

    applyGalaxySkybox()

    Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Sky") then task.defer(applyGalaxySkybox) end
    end)

    Lighting.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Sky") then task.defer(applyGalaxySkybox) end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            local sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
            if sky then
                if sky.SkyboxBk ~= GALAXY_SKYBOX.Bk
                   or sky.SkyboxDn ~= GALAXY_SKYBOX.Dn
                   or sky.SkyboxFt ~= GALAXY_SKYBOX.Ft
                   or sky.SkyboxLf ~= GALAXY_SKYBOX.Lf
                   or sky.SkyboxRt ~= GALAXY_SKYBOX.Rt
                   or sky.SkyboxUp ~= GALAXY_SKYBOX.Up then
                    applyGalaxySkybox()
                end
            else
                applyGalaxySkybox()
            end
        end
    end)

    task.delay(1, function()
        if Library and Library.Notify then
            Library:Notify({
                Title = "<b>LEVIUS</b>",
                Description = "🌌 Galaxy Skybox applied automatically",
                Time = 3,
            })
        end
    end)

    getgenv().GalaxySkybox = {
        Apply = applyGalaxySkybox,
        Restore = function()
            local sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
            if sky and originalSettings then
                sky.SkyboxBk = originalSettings.SkyboxBk
                sky.SkyboxDn = originalSettings.SkyboxDn
                sky.SkyboxFt = originalSettings.SkyboxFt
                sky.SkyboxLf = originalSettings.SkyboxLf
                sky.SkyboxRt = originalSettings.SkyboxRt
                sky.SkyboxUp = originalSettings.SkyboxUp
            elseif sky then
                sky:Destroy()
            end
        end,
    }
end

    AccentColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50),
    BackgroundColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),

    FontFace = "GothamBold",

-- Reference-style header; existing search callbacks and all tabs stay intact.
do
    (function()
        local main = Window.MainFrame or (Library.Window and Library.Window.MainFrame)
        if not main then return end
        local nativeTitle
        for _, item in ipairs(main:GetDescendants()) do
            if item:IsA("TextLabel") and item.Text == "[Levius.lol]" then
                nativeTitle = item
                break
            end
        end
        if not nativeTitle or not nativeTitle.Parent or not nativeTitle.Parent.Parent then return end
        local titleHolder = nativeTitle.Parent
        local topBar = titleHolder.Parent
        local search
        for _, holder in ipairs(topBar:GetChildren()) do
            if holder:IsA("Frame") and holder ~= titleHolder then
                for _, item in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
                    if item:IsA("TextBox") then search = item; break end
                end
            end
            if search then break end
        end
        if not search then return end
        local oldSearchHolder = search.Parent
        search.Parent = topBar
        if oldSearchHolder ~= topBar then oldSearchHolder.Visible = false end
        search.Name = "LEVIUS_HeaderSearch"
        search.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        search.TextScaled = false
        search.TextSize = 14
        search.PlaceholderText = "サーチ"
        local padding = search:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
        if padding then
            padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 30)
            padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
            padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
            padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
        end
        -- The native title remains available to the library's resize methods.
        nativeTitle.Visible = false
        nativeTitle.TextTransparency = 1
        Library:GiveSignal(nativeTitle:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
            if nativeTitle.Visible then nativeTitle.Visible = false end
        end))
        local logo
        for _, item in ipairs(titleHolder:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("ImageLabel") then logo = item; break end
        end
        if not logo then logo = Instance.new("ImageLabel"); logo.Parent = titleHolder end
        logo.Name = "LEVIUS_WindowIcon"
        logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        logo.Image = "rbxassetid://115352876301044"
        logo.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        logo.ImageTransparency = 0
        logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
        logo.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        logo.Visible = true
        -- Keep the move handle at the far right, with search immediately to its left.
        local dragGlyph
        for _, item in ipairs(topBar:GetChildren()) do
            if (item:IsA("ImageLabel") or item:IsA("TextLabel")) and item.AnchorPoint.X == 1 and item.Position.X.Scale == 1 then
                dragGlyph = item
                break
            end
        end
        if not dragGlyph then
            local icon = Library.GetIcon and Library:GetIcon("move")
            if icon and Library.CreateIcon then
                dragGlyph = Library:CreateIcon(icon, {Parent = topBar})
            else
                dragGlyph = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                dragGlyph.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                dragGlyph.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                dragGlyph.Text = "✥"
                dragGlyph.TextSize = 24
                dragGlyph.Parent = topBar
            end
        end
        dragGlyph.Name = "LEVIUS_HeaderMove"
        dragGlyph.Visible = true
        dragGlyph.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        if dragGlyph:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            dragGlyph.ImageColor3 = Library.Scheme.FontColor
            Library:AddToRegistry(dragGlyph, {ImageColor3 = "FontColor"})
        else
            dragGlyph.TextColor3 = Library.Scheme.FontColor
            Library:AddToRegistry(dragGlyph, {TextColor3 = "FontColor"})
        end
        local brand = Instance.new("Frame")
        brand.Name = "LEVIUS_HeaderBrand"
        brand.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        brand.BorderSizePixel = 0
        brand.ClipsDescendants = true
        brand.Parent = topBar
        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        title.Name = "LEVIUS_HeaderTitle"
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        title.Text = "[Levius.lol]"
        title.TextSize = 18
        title.TextColor3 = Library.Scheme.FontColor
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        title.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        title.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
        title.Parent = brand
        local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        subtitle.Name = "LEVIUS_HeaderSubtitle"
        subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        subtitle.Text = "Top 1 script"
        subtitle.TextSize = 12
        subtitle.TextColor3 = Library.Scheme.FontColor
        subtitle.TextTransparency = 0.35
        subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        subtitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        subtitle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        subtitle.Parent = brand
        Library:AddToRegistry(title, {TextColor3 = "FontColor"})
        Library:AddToRegistry(subtitle, {TextColor3 = "FontColor"})
        local textService = game:GetService("TextService")
        local function layout()
            if Library.Unloaded or not main.Parent then return end
            local scale = math.max(0.01, Library.DPIScale or 1)
            local width = main.AbsoluteSize.X / scale
            if width <= 0 then width = main.Size.X.Offset end
            local sidebar = Window:GetSidebarWidth()
            local searchWidth = math.clamp(width * 0.23, 112, 180)
            local left = sidebar + 12
            local moveSize, moveInset, moveGap = 28, 10, 10
            local searchRightInset = moveSize + moveInset + moveGap
            local available = math.max(0, width - left - searchWidth - searchRightInset - 24)
            local titleWidth = math.min(available, textService:GetTextSize(title.Text, 18, Enum.Font.Code, Vector2.new(1000, 48)).X + 2)
            brand.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(left, 0)
            brand.Size = UDim2.new(0, available, 1, 0)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(0, titleWidth, 1, 0)
            subtitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, titleWidth + 8, 0.5, 0)
            subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(0, available - titleWidth - 8), 1, 0)
            dragGlyph.Position = UDim2.new(1, -moveInset, 0.5, 0)
            dragGlyph.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(moveSize, moveSize)
            search.Position = UDim2.new(1, -searchRightInset, 0.5, 0)
            search.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(searchWidth, 30)
        end
        Window:SetSidebarWidth(150)
        layout()
        Library:GiveSignal(main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(layout))
        Library:GiveSignal(titleHolder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(layout))
    end)()
end

local Tabs = {
    Main = Window:AddTab("Main", "house"),
    Target = Window:AddTab("Target", "crosshair"),
    Defense = Window:AddTab("Defense", "shield"),
    Server = Window:AddTab("Server", "server"),
    VISUAL = Window:AddTab("VISUAL", "user"),

}

task.delay(0.5, function()
    notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Script loaded successfully", 3, SOUND_ACHIEVEMENT)
end)

do
    local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    function Notify(title, text, duration)
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = title,
            Text = text,
            Duration = duration,
        })
    end

    function Safe(name, fn)
        local ok, err = pcall(fn)
        if not ok then
            pcall(Notify, "Settings Error: " .. name, tostring(err), 8)
        end
    end

    local MenuGroup
    Safe("MenuGroup", function()
        MenuGroup = Tabs.Settings:AddLeftGroupbox("Menu", "wrench")
    end)

    Safe("ToggleMenu", function()
        MenuGroup:AddButton({
            Text = "Toggle Menu",
            Func = function() Library:Toggle() end,
        })
    end)

    Safe("Cursor", function()
        MenuGroup:AddToggle("ShowCustomCursor", {
            Text = "Custom Cursor",
            Default = true,
            Callback = function(v) Library.ShowCustomCursor = v end,
        })
    end)

    Safe("KeybindFrame", function()
        MenuGroup:AddToggle("KeybindMenuOpen", {
            Text = "Open Keybind Menu",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                if Library.KeybindFrame then Library.KeybindFrame.Visible = v end
            end,
        })
    end)

    Safe("NotifySide", function()
        MenuGroup:AddDropdown("NotificationSide", {
            Text = "Notification Side",
            Values = { "Left", "Right" },
            Default = "Right",
            Callback = function(v)
                if Library.SetNotifySide then Library:SetNotifySide(v) end
            end,
        })
    end)

    Safe("DPI", function()
        MenuGroup:AddDropdown("DPIDropdown", {
            Text = "DPI Scale",
            Values = { "50%", "75%", "100%", "125%", "150%", "175%", "200%" },
            Default = "100%",
            Callback = function(v)
                if Library.SetDPIScale then
                    Library:SetDPIScale(tonumber((v:gsub("%%", ""))))
                end
            end,
        })
    end)

    Safe("MenuKeybind", function()
        MenuGroup:AddDivider()
        MenuGroup:AddLabel("Menu bind"):AddKeyPicker("MenuKeybind", {
            Default = "RightShift",
            NoUI = true,
            Text = "Menu keybind",
        })
        Library.ToggleKeybind = Options.MenuKeybind
    end)

    Safe("Unload", function()
        MenuGroup:AddButton({
            Text = "Unload",
            Func = function() Library:Unload() end,
        })
    end)

    Safe("Config", function()
        SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)
        SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
        SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({ "MenuKeybind" })
        SaveManager:SetFolder("LEVIUS/Configs")
        SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(Tabs.Settings)
    end)

    Safe("Theme", function()
        ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
        ThemeManager:SetFolder("LEVIUS")
        ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(Tabs.Settings)
    end)

    getgenv().LEVIUS_SettingsAutoload = function()
        Safe("Autoload", function()
            SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig()
        end)
    end
end

local Watermark = Library:AddDraggableLabel("LEVIUS | 0 fps | 0 ms", "activity")
Watermark:SetVisible(true)

task.spawn(function()
    while Watermark and Watermark.Label and Watermark.Label.Parent do
        local ok, ping = pcall(function()
            return math.floor(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
        end)
        if ok and ping then
            Watermark:SetText(("LEVIUS | %d fps | %d ms"):format(
                math.floor(Stats.Workspace.Heartbeat:GetValue()),
                ping
            ))
        end
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

local CharacterEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("CharacterEvents")
local Struggle = CharacterEvents:WaitForChild("Struggle")
local RagdollRemote = CharacterEvents:WaitForChild("RagdollRemote")
local GrabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents")
local SetNetworkOwner = GrabEvents:WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
local DestroyGrabLine = GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")
local CreateGrabLine = GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
local ExtendGrabLine = GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("ExtendGrabLine")
local MenuToys = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys")

local DestroyToyRemote = MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")

local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local toysFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
    char = newChar
    toysFolder = Workspace:WaitForChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys", 5)
end)

function GetChar()
    return LocalPlayer.Character
end

function GetHRH(c)
    c = c or LocalPlayer.Character
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("Head"), c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
end

function Teleport(part, cf, offset)
    if part and cf and offset then part.CFrame = cf * offset; part.Velocity = Vector3.zero; part.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end
end

function Ownerboat(part)
    if not part then return nil end
    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
    if part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and part.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then return part.PartOwner end
    return nil
end

function SpawnToy(name, cf) task.spawn(function() SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(name, cf, Vector3.zero) end) end
function DestroyT(toy) if toy then DestroyToyRemote:FireServer(toy) end end

function GetPlayerFromDisplay(display)
    if typeof(display) == "Instance" and display:IsA("Player") then return display end
    if type(display) == "table" then
        local matches = {}
        for player, enabled in pairs(display) do
            if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") and enabled ~= false then
                table.insert(matches, player)
            end
        end
        table.sort(matches, function(a, b) return a.Name < b.Name end)
        return matches[1]
    end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if (player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")") == display
            or player.Name == display then return player end
    end
    return nil
end

local AntiState = States.Anti

getgenv().LEVIUS_DebugFPS = false

do
    local lowFpsStreak = 0
    local LOW_FPS_STREAK_REQUIRED = 2

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not AntiState.AutoAntiLag then
            lowFpsStreak = 0
            return
        end
        if dt <= 0 then return end

        local instantFPS = 1 / dt
        local threshold = AntiState.AntiLagSens or 3

        if instantFPS < threshold then
            lowFpsStreak += 1
        else
            lowFpsStreak = 0
        end

        if getgenv().LEVIUS_DebugFPS and lowFpsStreak > 0 then
            print(("[LEVIUS] instantFPS: %.1f | streak: %d/%d | threshold: %d")
                :format(instantFPS, lowFpsStreak, LOW_FPS_STREAK_REQUIRED, threshold))
        end

        if lowFpsStreak >= LOW_FPS_STREAK_REQUIRED and not AntiState.AntiLag then
            AntiState.AntiLag = true
            lowFpsStreak = 0
            local antiLag = (Toggles and Toggles.Levius_AntiLag) or (Options and Options.Levius_AntiLag)
            if antiLag then antiLag:SetValue(true) end
            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS",
                ("Anti Lag Enabled || FPS dropped (%.1f < %d)"):format(instantFPS, threshold), 4)
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiGrab()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiGrab do
            local isHeld = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("IsHeld")
            local grabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
            local grabbed = (isHeld and isHeld.Value)
            if not grabbed and grabParts then
                local grabPart = grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
                local weld = grabPart and grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
                if weld and weld.Part1 and weld.Part1:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer.Character) then
                    grabbed = true
                end
            end
            if grabbed then
                local c, h, hum = GetHRH()
                if c and h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    h.Anchored = true; Struggle:FireServer(); task.wait(); h.Anchored = false

                    local mode = AntiState.AntiGrabMode
                    if mode == "Escapes Anything, but buggy" then
                        task.spawn(ExtraEscape)
                    elseif mode == "Anti-Kill" then
                        hum.Sit = true
                    elseif mode == "No Ragdoll" then
                    else
                        if hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and not hum.Ragdolled.Value then
                            RagdollRemote:FireServer(h, 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiBlob()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiBlob do
            task.wait(0.1)
            local c, h, hum = GetHRH()
            if c and h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                if h:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment") then 
                    h.RootAttachment:Destroy()
                    Instance.new("Folder", h).Name = "RootAttachment"
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiBurn()
    task.spawn(function()
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild("Plot3")
        local barrierFolder = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("Barrier")
        local barrier = barrierFolder and barrierFolder:FindFirstChild("PlotBarrier")
        if not barrier then return end
        local originCF = barrier.CFrame
        while AntiState.AntiBurn do
            task.wait()
            local _, h, _ = GetHRH()
            if h and h:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") and h.FirePlayerPart:FindFirstChild("CanBurn") and h.FirePlayerPart.CanBurn.Value then
                barrier.CFrame = h.CFrame; task.wait(); barrier.CFrame = originCF
            end
        end
    end)
end

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if AntiState.AntiExplode and child.Name == "Part" and child:IsA("Part") then
        local c, h, hum = GetHRH()
        if not h or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        if (child.Position - h.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
            h.Anchored = true; h.Velocity = Vector3.zero; task.wait(); h.Anchored = false
        end
    end
end)

function StartAntiSnowball()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiSnowball do
            task.wait()
            local _, h, hum = GetHRH()
            if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then RagdollRemote:FireServer(h, 0.75) end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiVoid()
    if AntiState.AntiVoid then 
        Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0 
    else 
        if not AntiState.DisableVoid then
            Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
        end
    end
end

function StartAntiPaint()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiPaint do
            task.wait(0.1)
            local c = GetChar()
            if c then 
                for _, v in c:GetChildren() do 
                    if v:IsA("Part") then v.CanTouch = false end 
                end 
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiSticky()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiSticky do
            task.wait(0.1)
            local c = GetChar()
            if not c then continue end
            local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            local inOwned = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")

            for _, part in pairs(c:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
                for _, conn in pairs(part:GetConnectedParts()) do
                    if conn.Name == "StickyPart" then
                        local parent = conn.Parent
                        if parent then
                            local isInMyInv = folder and parent:IsDescendantOf(folder)
                            local isInPlot = parent:IsDescendantOf(Workspace.PlotItems or Workspace)
                            local canRemove = not isInMyInv and (not isInPlot or (inOwned and inOwned.Value))

                            if canRemove then
                                for _, child in pairs(conn:GetChildren()) do
                                    if child:IsA("TouchInterest") then child:Destroy() end
                                end
                                local nicePart = parent:IsA("Model") and (parent.PrimaryPart or parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
                                if nicePart then
                                    pcall(function() SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(nicePart, nicePart.CFrame) end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiBanana()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiBanana do
            task.wait()
            local c = GetChar()
            if not c then continue end
            local _, h, hum = GetHRH(c)
            if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local isHeld = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("IsHeld")
                if (c:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or c:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")) and isHeld and not isHeld.Value then
                    local start = tick()
                    repeat RagdollRemote:FireServer(h, 0.5); task.wait(0.1) until (not h or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and hum.Ragdolled.Value or not AntiState.AntiBanana or tick() - start > 1.5)
                    if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and hum.Ragdolled.Value and not isHeld.Value then
                        hum.Sit = false
                        hum.HipHeight = 2
                        hum.PlatformStand = false

                        if c:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") then c["Left Leg"].CFrame = CFrame.new(100000, -100000, 100000) end
                        if c:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") then c["Right Leg"].CFrame = CFrame.new(100000, -100000, 100000) end

                        local connection = nil
                        connection = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("HipHeight"):Connect(function()
                            if not AntiState.AntiBanana then
                                return connection:Disconnect()
                            end
                            hum.HipHeight = 2
                        end)

                        task.wait(0.1)
                        if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(h, h.CFrame) end
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiLag()
    if AntiState.AntiLag then
        pcall(function() LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.Disabled = true end)
        for _, v in Players:GetPlayers() do
            if v ~= LocalPlayer and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") then 
                pcall(function() v.Character:FindFirstChild("GrabParts"):Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    else
        pcall(function() LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.Disabled = false end)
    end
end

function StartAntiPoison(state)
    pcall(function()
        local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        local hole = map and map:FindFirstChild("Hole")
        if not hole then return end
        local poisonBigHole = hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonBigHole")
        local poisonSmallHole = hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")

        for _, obj in pairs({ poisonBigHole, poisonSmallHole }) do
            if obj then
                local poisonHurtPart = obj:FindFirstChild("PoisonHurtPart")
                local paintPlayerPart = obj:FindFirstChild("PaintPlayerPart")
                if poisonHurtPart then poisonHurtPart.CanTouch = not state end
                if paintPlayerPart then paintPlayerPart.CanTouch = not state end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiInvis()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiInvis do
            task.wait(0.1)
            for _, plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local c = plr.Character
                    if c then
                        local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            if hrp.Massless then hrp.Massless = false end
                            if hrp.CanCollide then hrp.CanCollide = true end
                        end
                        local hum = c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        if hum and hum.Sit and hum.SeatPart then
                            local seatWeld = hum.SeatPart:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")
                            if hum.SeatPart.Parent and seatWeld and not seatWeld.Enabled then
                                seatWeld.Enabled = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartWaterWalk(state)
    local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local alwaysHere = map and map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
    local ocean = alwaysHere and alwaysHere:FindFirstChild("Ocean")
    local object = ocean and ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")
    local objectModel = object and object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
    if not objectModel then 
        objectModel = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel", true)
        if not objectModel then return end
    end

    for _, child in pairs(objectModel:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            local stateData = AntiState.WaterPartStates[child]
            if state and not stateData then
                stateData = { child.CanCollide, child.Transparency }
                AntiState.WaterPartStates[child] = stateData
            end
            if state then
                child.CanCollide = true
                if child.Transparency ~= 1 and stateData[2] ~= 1 then
                    child.Transparency = 0.5
                end
            elseif stateData then
                child.CanCollide = stateData[1]
                child.Transparency = stateData[2]
                AntiState.WaterPartStates[child] = nil
            end
        end
    end
end

function StartNoclipBarrier(state)
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local barrier = plot:FindFirstChild("Barrier")
        if barrier then
            for _, child in ipairs(barrier:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "PlotBarrier" then
                    child.CanCollide = not state
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function StartAntiFling(state)
    if state then
        task.spawn(function()
            while AntiState.AntiFling do
                task.wait(0.1)
                local c = GetChar()
                if c then
                    for _, part in ipairs(c:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
                        pcall(function()
                            local inOwned = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
                            local groupName = (inOwned and inOwned.Value) and "PlotPlayers" or "Players"
                            PhysicsService:SetPartCollisionGroup(part, groupName)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function ExtraEscape()
    local c, h, hum = GetHRH()
    if not h or not hum or hum.Health == 0 or AntiState.ExtraEscaping then return end
    AntiState.ExtraEscaping = true

    local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    if not folder then AntiState.ExtraEscaping = false return end

    local Tractor = folder:FindFirstChild("TractorRSC")
    if not Tractor then
        SpawnToy("TractorGreen", CFrame.new(0, 500, 0))
        Tractor = folder:WaitForChild("TractorGreen", 1.5)
        if Tractor then Tractor.Name = "TractorRSC" end
    end
    if not Tractor then AntiState.ExtraEscaping = false return end

    local seat = Tractor:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 1)
    if not seat then 
        AntiState.ExtraEscaping = false 
        DestroyT(Tractor)
        return 
    end

    local now = tick()
    local seated = false
    hum.Sit = false
    seat:Sit(hum)

    local conn = seat.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "SeatWeld" then
            child.Enabled = false
            seated = true
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    while not seated and tick() - now < 0.7 do
        task.wait()
    end
    conn:Disconnect()
    hum.Sit = false
    AntiState.ExtraEscaping = false
    DestroyT(Tractor)
end

do
    local GameCorrections = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionEvents")
    local GameCorrectionsNotify = GameCorrections and GameCorrections:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionsNotify")

    if GameCorrectionsNotify then
        GameCorrectionsNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(reason)
            if reason == "Flying" then
                local now = tick()
                if now - (AntiState.KickFlagsLast or 0) > 1 then
                    AntiState.KickFlagsLast = now
                    AntiState.KickFlags = 1
                else
                    AntiState.KickFlags = (AntiState.KickFlags or 0) + 1
                end

                if AntiState.AutoReset then
                    if AntiState.KickFlags >= 4 and AntiState.AutoLeave then
                        return LocalPlayer:Kick("LEVIUS (Auto Reset)\nAvoided Kick")
                    end
                    local _, _, hum = GetHRH()
                    if hum then
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                        if AntiState.KickFlags == 1 then
                            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Reset due to kick flag", 3)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function TrackHeadPartOwner()
    local trackConn = nil
    function setup()
        if trackConn then trackConn:Disconnect() end
        local c = GetChar()
        if not c then return end
        local head = c:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
        if not head then return end

        trackConn = head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA("StringValue") and child.Name == "PartOwner" then
                local owner = Players:FindFirstChild(child.Value)
                if owner then
                    AntiState.WhoGrabbed = owner.DisplayName .. " (@" .. owner.Name .. ")"
                    if AntiState.LastGrabbedLabel then
                        AntiState.LastGrabbedLabel:SetVisible(true)
                        AntiState.LastGrabbedLabel:SetText("Last person who grabbed you: " .. AntiState.WhoGrabbed)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    setup()
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        setup()
    end)
end
TrackHeadPartOwner()

function FindMyPCLD()
    while task.wait() and AntiState.AntiKick do
        local c = GetChar()
        if c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            for _, v in Workspace:GetChildren() do
                if v.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" and (v.Position - c.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 0.75 then
                    return v
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function StartAntiKick()
    task.spawn(function()
        local owner = nil
        while AntiState.AntiKick do
            task.wait()
            if not AntiState.MyPCLD or not AntiState.MyPCLD.Parent then
                AntiState.MyPCLD = FindMyPCLD()
            end
            local c = GetChar()
            local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            if c and AntiState.MyPCLD and folder then
                local _, h, hum = GetHRH(c)
                if h and h:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local firePlayerPart = h:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
                    local antiKick = folder:FindFirstChild("AntiKick") or (function()
                        SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken", AntiState.MyPCLD.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 20))
                        return folder:WaitForChild("NinjaShuriken", 1.5)
                    end)()
                    if antiKick then
                        antiKick.Name = "AntiKick"
                        local stickyPart = antiKick:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 1)
                        if stickyPart then
                            local stickyWeld = stickyPart:WaitForChild("StickyWeld", 1)
                            local targetPart = nil
                            for _, v in antiKick:GetChildren() do
                                if v:IsA("Part") and v.CanQuery then targetPart = v; break end
                            end
                            if stickyWeld and targetPart and (AntiState.MyPCLD.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude < 20 then
                                local start = tick()
                                repeat
                                    owner = Ownerboat(targetPart)
                                    task.wait()
                                until (not AntiState.AntiKick or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not targetPart or not targetPart.Parent or (AntiState.MyPCLD.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude > 20 or tick() - start > 1 or owner)
                                if owner and stickyPart and stickyPart.Parent and firePlayerPart and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                                    local rotStart = tick()
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                        StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, firePlayerPart,
                                            CFrame.new(AntiState.AntiKickOffsetX, AntiState.AntiKickOffsetY, AntiState.AntiKickOffsetZ) *
                                            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(AntiState.AntiKickRotationX), math.rad(AntiState.AntiKickRotationY), math.rad(AntiState.AntiKickRotationZ)))
                                        local done = not AntiState.AntiKick or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not owner
                                        done = done or (stickyWeld and stickyWeld.Part1 and stickyWeld.Part1 == firePlayerPart) or tick() - rotStart > 1
                                    until done
                                    if firePlayerPart and hum and hum.Health > 0 and stickyWeld and stickyWeld.Part1 and stickyWeld.Part1 == firePlayerPart then
                                        while AntiState.AntiKick and task.wait() and hum and hum.Health > 0 and stickyWeld and stickyWeld.Parent and stickyWeld.Part1 and stickyWeld.Part1 == firePlayerPart do
                                            AntiState.AntiKickSticked = true
                                            local hl = antiKick:FindFirstChild("AKHighlight")
                                            if not hl and AntiState.AntiKickHighlight then
                                                hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                                                hl.Name = "AKHighlight"
                                                hl.Adornee = antiKick
                                                hl.Parent = antiKick
                                            end
                                            if hl then
                                                hl.Enabled = AntiState.AntiKickHighlight
                                                hl.FillColor = AntiState.AntiKickFillColor
                                                hl.OutlineColor = AntiState.AntiKickOutlineColor
                                                hl.FillTransparency = AntiState.AntiKickFillTransparency
                                                hl.OutlineTransparency = AntiState.AntiKickOutlineTransparency
                                            end
                                            for _, v in antiKick:GetChildren() do
                                                if v:IsA("Part") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                                    if v.Transparency ~= 1 then v.Transparency = AntiState.AntiKickTransparency end
                                                    v.CanCollide = false
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        AntiState.AntiKickSticked = false
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                DestroyT(antiKick)
                            end
                        else
                            DestroyT(antiKick)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartAntiKick2(state)
    if not state then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiKick2 do
            task.wait(0.2)
            local c = GetChar()
            if not c then continue end
            local hum = c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not hum then continue end

            if not hum:GetAttribute("BrokenPCLD") or hum.Health <= 0 then
                local nextChar = nil
                if hum.Health > 0 then
                    notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Starting PCLD Break...", 3)
                    task.spawn(function()
                        repeat
                            if not AntiState.AntiKick2 then break end
                            hum.Health = 0
                            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                            task.wait()
                        until nextChar
                    end)
                end
                nextChar = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                local hum2 = nextChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                if hum2 then
                    local finalChar = nil
                    task.spawn(function()
                        repeat
                            if not AntiState.AntiKick2 then break end
                            hum2.Health = 0
                            hum2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                            task.wait()
                        until finalChar
                    end)
                    finalChar = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    local hum3 = finalChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                    if hum3 then
                        hum3:SetAttribute("BrokenPCLD", true)
                        notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "PCLD Broken! Anti Kick protection active.", 3)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartGucci()
    local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    if not folder then return end
    for _, v in folder:GetChildren() do
        if v.Name == "guchi" or v.Name == "CreatureBlobman" or v.Name == "TractorGreen" or v.Name == "Train" or v.Name == "SantaSleigh" then 
            v.Name = "delete"
            DestroyT(v) 
        end
    end

    local vehicleName = "CreatureBlobman"
    local isBlobman = true
    if AntiState.GucciMethod == "Tractor [Invisible]" then
        vehicleName = "TractorGreen"
        isBlobman = false
    elseif AntiState.GucciMethod == "Train [Invisible]" then
        vehicleName = "Train"
        isBlobman = false
    elseif AntiState.GucciMethod == "Sleigh [Invisible]" then
        vehicleName = "SantaSleigh"
        isBlobman = false
    end

    local _, h, hum = GetHRH()
    if not h or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if not hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") or hum.Ragdolled.Value then return end

    SpawnToy(vehicleName, CFrame.new(0, 1000000, 20))
    local vehicle = folder:WaitForChild(vehicleName, 2)
    if not vehicle then return end

    vehicle.Name = "guchi"
    local seat = vehicle:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat") or vehicle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Seat")
    local hrp = vehicle:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or vehicle.PrimaryPart or vehicle:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not seat or not hrp then DestroyT(vehicle) return end

    seat.Anchored = true
    local flag = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() and flag and seat and seat.Parent and h and hum and hum.Health > 0 do
            h.Anchored = true
            RagdollRemote:FireServer(h, 0)
            local currentChar = GetChar()
            if currentChar then
                for _, v in currentChar:GetChildren() do 
                    if v.Name == "LocalCreatureControl" then v.Enabled = false end 
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    while hum and hum.Health > 0 and seat and seat.Parent and not seat.Occupant do 
        seat:Sit(hum)
        task.wait() 
    end
    if hum and hum.Health > 0 and seat and seat.Parent and seat.Occupant == hum then 
        hum.Sit = false 
    end

    if not isBlobman then
        for _, part in vehicle:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
            pcall(function()
                part.Transparency = 1
                part.CanCollide = false
            end)
        end
    end

    local start = tick()
    repeat task.wait() Ownerboat(hrp) until (not vehicle or not vehicle.Parent or (vehicle:FindFirstChild("Head") and vehicle.Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and vehicle.Head.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name) or not hum or hum.Health < 0 or tick() - start > 1)
    flag = false
    if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then 
        h.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        h.Anchored = false 
    end
    if seat and seat.Parent then return end
end

function StartCounterAttack()
    task.spawn(function()
        function setup(character)
            local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
            if not head then return end
            head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if not AntiState.CounterAttack then return end
                if not child:IsA("StringValue") or child.Name ~= "PartOwner" then return end
                local owner = Players:FindFirstChild(child.Value)
                if not owner or owner == LocalPlayer then return end
                local char2 = owner.Character
                if not char2 then return end
                local hrp = char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum then return end

                local m = AntiState.CounterMethod
                if m == "Grab" then
                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hrp, hrp.CFrame)
                    if DestroyGrabLine then DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(hrp) end
                    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if myHrp then
                        hrp.CFrame = myHrp.CFrame + myHrp.CFrame.LookVector * 5
                    end
                elseif m == "Kill" then
                    hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                    pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -5000, 0) end)
                elseif m == "Void" then
                    local cf = CFrame.new(0, 9e9, 0)
                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hrp, cf)
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e9, 0)
                elseif m == "Delete Limbs" then
                    local target = AntiState.TargetedLimbCA or "All"
                    for _, v in ipairs(char2:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
                        local isArm = v.Name:find("Arm")
                        local isLeg = v.Name:find("Leg")
                        local shouldDelete = false
                        if target == "All" and (isArm or isLeg) then shouldDelete = true
                        elseif target == "Arms" and isArm then shouldDelete = true
                        elseif target == "Legs" and isLeg then shouldDelete = true
                        end
                        if shouldDelete then
                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(v, v.CFrame)
                            v.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -1e5, 0)
                        end
                    end
                elseif m == "Fling" then
                    local myRoot = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if myRoot then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = myRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 100 + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0) end
                elseif m == "Teleport to Spawn" then
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 100, 0)
                elseif m == "Anchor" then
                    hrp.Anchored = true
                end
            end)
        end
        setup(LocalPlayer.Character)
        LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) task.wait(1); setup(c) end)
    end)
end

function StartAntiNetwork()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.AntiNetwork do
            task.wait(AntiState.AntiNetDelay)
            local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            local inPlot = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
            local canSpawn = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
            if not folder or not inPlot or not canSpawn then continue end
            if not inPlot.Value and canSpawn.Value then
                local existing = folder:FindFirstChild("AntiNetwork")
                if not existing then 
                    SpawnToy(AntiState.AntiNetItem, CFrame.new(52, 228, 67))
                    existing = folder:WaitForChild(AntiState.AntiNetItem, 1)
                end
                if existing then
                    existing.Name = "AntiNetwork"
                    local hp = existing:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 1)
                    if hp then
                        local rigid = hp:FindFirstChild("RigidConstraint")
                        local hold = hp:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
                        local drop = hp:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
                        if rigid and hold and drop then
                            if rigid.Attachment1 ~= nil and rigid.Attachment1.Parent and rigid.Attachment1.Parent.Parent and rigid.Attachment1.Parent.Parent.Name ~= LocalPlayer.Name then 
                                DestroyT(existing)
                            else
                                for _, part in existing:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
                                    if part:IsA("MeshPart") or part.Name == "Part" then
                                        part.Transparency = AntiState.AntiNetTransparency
                                    end
                                end
                                local c = GetChar()
                                task.spawn(function() pcall(function() hold:InvokeServer(existing, c) end) end)
                                task.spawn(function() 
                                    task.wait(AntiState.AntiNetDelay) 
                                    pcall(function() drop:InvokeServer(existing, CFrame.new(52, 1488, 67), Vector3.zero) end)
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function StartLoopTP()
    task.spawn(function()
        while AntiState.LoopTP do
            task.wait(0.01)
            local _, h, hum = GetHRH()
            if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local origCF = h.CFrame
                while AntiState.LoopTP and h and hum and hum.Health > 0 do
                    Teleport(h, CFrame.new(math.random(-500, 500), -8, math.random(-500, 500)), CFrame.new())
                    task.wait(0.01)
                end
                if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then Teleport(h, origCF, CFrame.new()) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local Dragonic = States.Dragonic

function DragonicGetLocalChar() return LocalPlayer.Character end
function DragonicGetHRP()
    local c = DragonicGetLocalChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
function DragonicGetToysFolder()
    return Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
end

function DragonicDestroyAllPencils()
    local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
    if not folder then return end
    for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do
        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then
            pcall(function() DestroyToyRemote:FireServer(child) end)
        end
    end
end

function DragonicCountPencils()
    local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
    if not folder then return 0 end
    local count = 0
    for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do
        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then count += 1 end
    end
    return count
end

function DragonicIsPencilWelded()
    local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
    if not folder then return false end
    local target = Players:FindFirstChild(Dragonic.SelectedPlayer)
    if not target or not target.Character then return false end
    local targetHead = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not targetHead then return false end
    for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do
        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then
            local sp = child:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
            if sp then
                local weld = sp:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")
                if weld and weld.Part1 == targetHead then return true end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function DragonicSpawnPencil()
    local hrp = DragonicGetHRP()
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local spawnPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
    local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
    if not folder then return nil end
    local spawned = nil
    local conn
    conn = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then
            spawned = child
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("ToolPencil", spawnPos, Vector3.zero) end)
    end)
    local start = tick()
    repeat task.wait(0.05) until spawned or (tick() - start) > 3
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
    return spawned
end

function DragonicSetNetOwnerPencil(part)
    if not part then return end
    pcall(function()
        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
    end)
end

function DragonicDoSticky()
    if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer or Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == "" then return false end
    local target = Players:FindFirstChild(Dragonic.SelectedPlayer)
    if not target or not target.Character then return false end
    local targetHead = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not targetHead then return false end
    DragonicDestroyAllPencils()
    task.wait(0.05)
    local pencil = DragonicSpawnPencil()
    if not pencil then return false end
    local soundPart = pencil:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
    local stickyPart = pencil:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
    if soundPart then DragonicSetNetOwnerPencil(soundPart) end
    task.wait(0.05)
    if stickyPart then
        pcall(function()
            StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, targetHead, CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0))
            StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, targetHead, CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0))
        end)
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not DragonicIsPencilWelded() then
            DragonicDestroyAllPencils()
            task.wait(0.05)
            DragonicDoSticky()
        end
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function DragonicPencilMonitor()
    while Dragonic.IsRagdollActive do
        local count = DragonicCountPencils()
        if count == 0 then
            DragonicDoSticky()
        elseif count >= 2 then
            DragonicDestroyAllPencils()
            task.wait(0.05)
            DragonicDoSticky()
        elseif count == 1 then
            if not DragonicIsPencilWelded() then
                DragonicDestroyAllPencils()
                task.wait(0.05)
                DragonicDoSticky()
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end

function DragonicStartRagdollKill()
    if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer or Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == "" then return end
    Dragonic.IsRagdollActive = true
    DragonicDoSticky()
    local target = Players:FindFirstChild(Dragonic.SelectedPlayer)
    if target then
        target.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            if Dragonic.IsRagdollActive then DragonicDoSticky() end
        end)
    end
    Dragonic.RagdollMonitorTask = task.spawn(DragonicPencilMonitor)
end

function DragonicStopRagdollKill()
    Dragonic.IsRagdollActive = false
    if Dragonic.RagdollMonitorTask then
        task.cancel(Dragonic.RagdollMonitorTask)
        Dragonic.RagdollMonitorTask = nil
    end
    DragonicDestroyAllPencils()
end

local Banana = States.Banana

function BananaSpawn()
    local hrp = DragonicGetHRP()
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local spawnPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
    local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
    if not folder then return nil end
    local spawned = nil
    local conn
    conn = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then
            spawned = child
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("FoodBanana", spawnPos, Vector3.zero) end)
    end)
    local start = tick()
    repeat task.wait(0.05) until spawned or (tick() - start) > 3
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
    return spawned
end

function BananaHold(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    local holdPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
    if not holdPart then return end
    local holdRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
    if not holdRemote then return end
    local c = DragonicGetLocalChar()
    if not c then return end
    for i = 1, 5 do
        if not banana or not banana.Parent then break end
        local ok = pcall(function() return holdRemote:InvokeServer(banana, c) end)
        if ok then return true end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    return false
end

function BananaUse(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    if not HoldEvents then return end
    local use = HoldEvents:FindFirstChild("Use")
    if not use then return end
    pcall(function() use:FireServer(banana) end)
end

function BananaDrop(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    local holdPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
    if not holdPart then return end
    local dropRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
    if not dropRemote then return end
    local hrp = DragonicGetHRP()
    if not hrp then return end
    local dropPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0)
    pcall(function() dropRemote:InvokeServer(banana, dropPos, dropPos) end)
end

function BananaGetTargetLeg()
    if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer then return nil end
    local target = Players:FindFirstChild(Dragonic.SelectedPlayer)
    if not target or not target.Character then return nil end
    return target.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
end

function BananaGetTargetHealth()
    if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer then return 0 end
    local target = Players:FindFirstChild(Dragonic.SelectedPlayer)
    if not target or not target.Character then return 0 end
    local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return hum and hum.Health or 0
end

function BananaFullCycle()
    if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer or Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == "" then return false end
    local banana = BananaSpawn()
    if not banana then return false end
    BananaHold(banana)
    task.wait(0.05)
    BananaHold(banana)
    task.wait(0.05)
    task.wait(0.5)
    BananaUse(banana)
    task.wait(2.3)
    BananaDrop(banana)
    task.wait(0.05)
    BananaDrop(banana)
    task.wait(0.3)
    return banana
end

function BananaRestartLoop(banana)
    if Banana.LoopConnection then
        Banana.LoopConnection:Disconnect()
        Banana.LoopConnection = nil
    end
    Banana.CurrentBanana = banana
    local hitboxPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
    if not hitboxPart then return end
    Banana.LoopConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Banana.IsLooping then
            BananaStopLoop()
            return
        end
        if not Banana.CurrentBanana or not Banana.CurrentBanana.Parent then return end
        local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
        local bananaExists = false
        if folder then
            for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do
                if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then
                    bananaExists = true
                    Banana.CurrentBanana = child
                    hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not bananaExists or not hitboxPart then
            if Banana.LoopConnection then
                Banana.LoopConnection:Disconnect()
                Banana.LoopConnection = nil
            end
            Banana.CurrentBanana = nil
            return
        end
        local hrp = DragonicGetHRP()
        if not hrp then return end
        local targetPos, targetCFrame
        local health = BananaGetTargetHealth()
        if health <= 0 then
            Banana.IsTargetDead = true
            targetPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 7, 0)
            targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.Position)
        else
            Banana.IsTargetDead = false
            local leg = BananaGetTargetLeg()
            if leg then
                targetPos = leg.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
                targetCFrame = leg.CFrame
            else
                targetPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 7, 0)
                targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.Position)
            end
        end
        pcall(function() SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hitboxPart, hitboxPart.CFrame) end)
        pcall(function() DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(hitboxPart) end)
        local bp = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")
        if bp then
            bp.Position = targetPos.Position
        else
            bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            bp.P = 10000; bp.D = 100
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            bp.Position = targetPos.Position
            bp.Parent = hitboxPart
        end
        local bg = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")
        if bg then
            bg.CFrame = targetCFrame
        else
            bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            bg.P = 10000; bg.D = 100
            bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            bg.CFrame = targetCFrame
            bg.Parent = hitboxPart
        end
    end)
end

function BananaStartLoop()
    if Banana.IsLooping then return end
    if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer or Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == "" then return end
    Banana.IsLooping = true
    Banana.IsTargetDead = false
    local banana = BananaFullCycle()
    if banana then BananaRestartLoop(banana) end
    Banana.MonitorConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Banana.IsLooping then return end
        local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
        local bananaExists = false
        if folder then
            for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do
                if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then bananaExists = true; break end
            end
        end
        if not bananaExists then
            if Banana.LoopConnection then
                Banana.LoopConnection:Disconnect()
                Banana.LoopConnection = nil
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
            local newBanana = BananaFullCycle()
            if newBanana then BananaRestartLoop(newBanana) end
        end
    end)
end

function BananaStopLoop()
    Banana.IsLooping = false
    if Banana.LoopConnection then
        Banana.LoopConnection:Disconnect()
        Banana.LoopConnection = nil
    end
    if Banana.MonitorConnection then
        Banana.MonitorConnection:Disconnect()
        Banana.MonitorConnection = nil
    end
    if Banana.CurrentBanana then
        pcall(function() DestroyToyRemote:FireServer(Banana.CurrentBanana) end)
        local hitboxPart = Banana.CurrentBanana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
        if hitboxPart then
            local bp = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")
            if bp then bp:Destroy() end
            local bg = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")
            if bg then bg:Destroy() end
        end
        Banana.CurrentBanana = nil
    end
    local folder = DragonicGetToysFolder()
    if folder then
        for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do
            if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then
                pcall(function() DestroyToyRemote:FireServer(child) end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local EXCLUDE = {}

local VoidGrabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents")
local VoidSetNetworkOwner = VoidGrabEvents:WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
local VoidDestroyGrabLine = VoidGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")

local VoidOriginalFallenHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
local VoidFallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0
pcall(function() workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = VoidFallenPartsDestroyHeight end)

local VoidPartOwnerCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local VoidStates = States.Void.States
local VoidDirectGrab = false
local VoidDestroyVoid = true
local VoidDestroyThread = nil
local VoidKillingPlayers = States.Void.KillingPlayers

function VoidChar(arg)
    if arg == nil then arg = LocalPlayer end
    if arg then
        local c = arg.Character
        if c then
            local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            return c, hrp, hum
        end
    end
end

function VoidGetMagnitude(a, b)
    if not a or not b then return end
    return (a.Position - b.Position).Magnitude
end

function VoidIsPartOwner(arg)
    if not (arg and arg:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
    local c = VoidPartOwnerCache[arg]
    if c and c.Parent and c.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then return true end
    local p = arg.Parent
    local head = arg.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and p and p:FindFirstChild("Head")
    head = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
    if head and head.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
        VoidPartOwnerCache[arg] = head; return true
    end
    local po = arg:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
    if po and po.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
        VoidPartOwnerCache[arg] = po; return true
    end
    p = p and p:FindFirstChild("PartOwner", true)
    if p then VoidPartOwnerCache[arg] = p; return p.Value == LocalPlayer.Name end
    return false
end

function VoidFindNicePart(m)
    if not m or not m:IsA("Model") then return end
    local best
    for _, v in ipairs(m:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
        if v.CanQuery and not v.Anchored and v.Name ~= "Button" then
            best = best or v
            if (v.AssemblyRootPart or v) == v then return v end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function VoidPivotTo(m, cf)
    if not m or not m:IsA("Model") or typeof(cf) ~= "CFrame" then return false end
    local delta = cf * m:GetPivot():Inverse()
    local parts = m:QueryDescendants("BasePart")
    local upd = {}
    for i, v in ipairs(parts) do
        local root = v:IsDescendantOf(m) and v.AssemblyRootPart
        if root and not v.Anchored and not root.Anchored then
            upd[i] = delta * v.CFrame
        end
    end
    for k, v in pairs(upd) do parts[k].CFrame = v end
    return true
end

function VoidPredictCFrame(arg, cf)
    return cf or arg.CFrame
end

function VoidGrab(arg, cf2)
    local _, hrp, hum = VoidChar()
    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health == 0 or not arg or not arg.Parent or not arg:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    local base = (cf2 or hrp.CFrame) * CFrame.new(0, 1.593, 0)
    local goal = VoidPredictCFrame(arg)
    if not VoidDirectGrab then
        goal = CFrame.new(base.Position, goal.Position)
    end
    VoidSetNetworkOwner:FireServer(arg, goal)
end

-- Bounded ownership retry with cleanup and adaptive pacing.
-- The server remains authoritative: this cannot guarantee ownership.
