function VoidSNO(arg, timeout, cond, burstCount, strongOwnership, fastUntil)
    local ch, hrp, hum = VoidChar()
    local head = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local rag = hum and hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
    if not (head and rag and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0) then return false end
    if not arg or not arg:IsA("BasePart") or not arg.Parent then return false end
    local spawnPart = workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
    if not spawnPart or VoidGetMagnitude(arg, spawnPart) > 10000000 then return false end
    if VoidIsPartOwner(arg) then return true end
    if VoidStates.Owning or (cond and not cond()) then return false end

    local far = (hrp.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude >= 31
    local restoreRoot, restoreHead = hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame
    local pinFrame = restoreRoot
    local renderName = "LEVIUS_VoidSNO_" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
    local heartbeatConnection
    local simulationConnection
    local postSimulationConnection
    local pinned = false
    local visibleRestored = true
    local ownerWake = nil
    local ownerWaiting = false
    local ownerConnections = {}
    local ownerWakeHook = nil
    local function cleanup()
        if ownerWakeHook and LeviusShared.LoopKillWakeActiveOwnership == ownerWakeHook then
            LeviusShared.LoopKillWakeActiveOwnership = nil
        end
        ownerWaiting = false
        if simulationConnection then simulationConnection:Disconnect(); simulationConnection = nil end
        if postSimulationConnection then postSimulationConnection:Disconnect(); postSimulationConnection = nil end
        if heartbeatConnection then heartbeatConnection:Disconnect(); heartbeatConnection = nil end
        for _, connection in ipairs(ownerConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        if ownerWake then ownerWake:Destroy(); ownerWake = nil end
        if pinned then
            pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(renderName) end)
            if head.Parent and hrp.Parent then
                pcall(function() head.CFrame, hrp.CFrame = restoreHead, restoreRoot end)
            end
            pinned = false
        end
    end

    VoidStates.Owning = true
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if strongOwnership then
            local canWakeNetwork = type(sethiddenproperty) == "function"
            if far then
                pinFrame = VoidPredictCFrame(arg) - Vector3.new(0, 22, 0)
                pinned = true
                RunService:BindToRenderStep(renderName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                    if hrp.Parent and head.Parent then
                        hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame = restoreRoot, restoreHead
                        visibleRestored = true
                    end
                end)
            end
            local function acquisitionCurrent()
                return hrp.Parent and head.Parent and arg.Parent and hum.Health > 0
                    and LocalPlayer.Character == ch and (not cond or cond())
                    and not VoidIsPartOwner(arg)
            end
            local function wakeNetwork()
                if acquisitionCurrent() and canWakeNetwork then
                    local awake = pcall(sethiddenproperty, hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                    if not awake then canWakeNetwork = false end
                end
            end
            local function syncAcquisition()
                if not acquisitionCurrent() then return end
                if far then
                    -- Capture normal physics/movement before pinning for replication.
                    -- Heartbeat must never overwrite this with the hidden position.
                    if visibleRestored then
                        restoreRoot, restoreHead = hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame
                        visibleRestored = false
                    end
                    hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame = pinFrame, pinFrame
                end
                wakeNetwork()
            end
            -- Let walking/standing physics run at the visible location, then
            -- publish the acquisition position before the next network flush.
            -- Unsupported sleep APIs are disabled after one failure per attempt.
            simulationConnection = RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
                if not acquisitionCurrent() then return end
                -- Physics may tick without a render frame (low FPS/background).
                if far and not visibleRestored then
                    hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame = restoreRoot, restoreHead
                    visibleRestored = true
                end
                wakeNetwork()
            end)
            postSimulationConnection = RunService.PostSimulation:Connect(syncAcquisition)
            heartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(syncAcquisition)
            syncAcquisition()
        else
            if far then
                pinned = true
                RunService:BindToRenderStep(renderName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                    if hrp.Parent and head.Parent then
                        hrp.CFrame = restoreRoot
                        head.CFrame = restoreHead
                    end
                end)
                heartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if hrp.Parent and head.Parent then
                        restoreRoot, restoreHead = hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame
                        hrp.CFrame, head.CFrame = pinFrame, pinFrame
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        timeout = math.clamp(tonumber(timeout) or 0.95, 0.1, 1.5)
        burstCount = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(burstCount) or 4), 1, strongOwnership and 22 or 6)
        -- GetNetworkPing reports one-way latency in seconds. Allow roughly
        -- one round trip for replication, bounded to keep retries responsive.
        local pingOK, ping = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() end)
        ping = pingOK and tonumber(ping) or 0.05
        if not ping or ping ~= ping or ping < 0 or ping == math.huge then ping = 0.05 end
        local retryInterval = strongOwnership
            and math.clamp(ping, 0.03, 0.12)
            or math.clamp(ping * 2, 0.04, 0.15)
        local deadline = os.clock() + timeout
        local nextSend = 0
        local attempt = 0
        if strongOwnership then
            -- Replicated ownership can become ready between physics frames.
            -- Keep Heartbeat as the fallback for retries, cancellation and timeout.
            ownerWake = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
            local watchedOwners = {}
            local function wakeOwner()
                if ownerWaiting and ownerWake then ownerWake:Fire() end
            end
            ownerWakeHook = wakeOwner
            LeviusShared.LoopKillWakeActiveOwnership = wakeOwner
            local function watchOwner(value)
                if value.Name == "PartOwner" and value:IsA("StringValue") and not watchedOwners[value] then
                    watchedOwners[value] = true
                    table.insert(ownerConnections, value:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(wakeOwner))
                    wakeOwner()
                end
            end
            local container = arg.Parent
            table.insert(ownerConnections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(wakeOwner))
            table.insert(ownerConnections, arg.AncestryChanged:Connect(wakeOwner))
            if container then
                table.insert(ownerConnections, container.DescendantAdded:Connect(watchOwner))
                for _, value in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do watchOwner(value) end
            end
        end
        while arg.Parent and not VoidIsPartOwner(arg)
            and hum.Health > 0 and hrp.Parent
            and (not strongOwnership or LocalPlayer.Character == ch)
            and (not cond or cond()) and os.clock() < deadline do
            if far then pinFrame = VoidPredictCFrame(arg) - Vector3.new(0, 22, 0) end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now >= nextSend then
                attempt += 1
                -- Strong target mode retains pressure after the initial burst.
                -- Ownership, cancellation and deadline checks still bound every send.
                local count
                if strongOwnership then
                    count = attempt == 1 and burstCount
                        or math.min(burstCount, 18 + math.floor((attempt - 2) / 2))
                else
                    count = attempt == 1 and burstCount
                        or math.min(burstCount, 1 + math.floor((attempt - 2) / 3))
                end
                for _ = 1, count do
                    if not arg.Parent or hum.Health <= 0 or not hrp.Parent
                        or (strongOwnership and LocalPlayer.Character ~= ch)
                        or os.clock() >= deadline or VoidIsPartOwner(arg)
                        or (cond and not cond()) then break end
                    VoidGrab(arg, far and pinFrame or nil)
                end
                local sentAt = os.clock()
                -- A single shared 100 ms window per newly observed character.
                -- The rest of the bounded attempt keeps its normal ping-based pace.
                local interval = retryInterval
                if strongOwnership and fastUntil and sentAt < fastUntil then
                    interval = math.min(interval, 0.02)
                end
                nextSend = sentAt + interval
            end
            if VoidIsPartOwner(arg) then break end
            if ownerWake then
                ownerWaiting = true
                ownerWake.Event:Wait()
                ownerWaiting = false
            else
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
        end
    end)
    cleanup()
    VoidStates.Owning = false
    -- Wake the selected respawn as soon as the previous ownership attempt exits.
    if LeviusShared.LoopKillOwnershipReady then
        task.defer(LeviusShared.LoopKillOwnershipReady)
    end
    if not ok then warn("[LEVIUS Network] " .. tostring(err)) end
    return arg.Parent ~= nil and VoidIsPartOwner(arg) == true
end

function VoidRNO(part)
    if VoidDestroyGrabLine and part then
        pcall(function() VoidDestroyGrabLine:FireServer(part) end)
    end
end

function VoidDestroyObject(model, part, strongOwnership, cond, fastUntil)
    if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then return end
    part = part or VoidFindNicePart(model)
    if not part or part.Anchored or not VoidSNO(part, strongOwnership and 1.5 or 1.35, cond, strongOwnership and 22 or 6, strongOwnership, fastUntil) then return end

    if cond and not cond() then return false end
    workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = VoidFallenPartsDestroyHeight
    VoidPivotTo(model, CFrame.new(0, -11111, 0))

    if VoidDestroyThread then task.cancel(VoidDestroyThread) end
    VoidDestroyThread = task.delay(
        math.clamp(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() + 0.05, 0.08, 0.25),
        function()
            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -10000
            task.wait()
            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = VoidDestroyVoid and (0/0) or -100
            VoidDestroyThread = nil
        end
    )
    -- Post-kill release only: this is not a delay before moving the target.
    task.delay(0.5, VoidRNO, part)
    return true
end

function VoidKill(player, cond, onSuccess, fastUntil)
    local ch, hrp, hum = VoidChar(player)
    local spawn = workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
    if not (ch and hrp and hum and spawn) or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    if VoidGetMagnitude(spawn, hrp) >= 15000 or VoidKillingPlayers[ch] then return false end
    if cond and not cond() then return false end
    local function isCurrent()
        return player.Parent ~= nil and player.Character == ch and ch.Parent ~= nil
            and hrp.Parent ~= nil and hum.Parent ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
            and (not cond or cond())
    end

    -- Keep the in-flight lock for the entire ownership attempt, not a fixed
    -- 0.15 seconds. This prevents overlapping remote/network requests.
    VoidKillingPlayers[ch] = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            -- Do not mark the target dead locally before ownership succeeds:
            -- an unsuccessful attempt must remain eligible for a retry.
            if isCurrent() and VoidDestroyObject(ch, hrp, true, isCurrent, fastUntil) then
                -- Counting must never block or delay the kill itself.
                if onSuccess then
                    local counted, countError = pcall(onSuccess, player, ch)
                    if not counted then warn("[LEVIUS Kill Counter] " .. tostring(countError)) end
                end
                hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
            end
        end)
        if not ok then warn("[LEVIUS LoopKill] " .. tostring(err)) end
        VoidKillingPlayers[ch] = nil
        if LeviusShared.LoopKillOwnershipReady then
            task.defer(LeviusShared.LoopKillOwnershipReady)
        end
    end)
    return true
end

local excludeSet = {}
for _, v in ipairs(EXCLUDE) do
    excludeSet[v] = true
end

function isExcluded(p)
    return excludeSet[p.Name] or excludeSet[p.UserId]
end

local VoidLoopKillActive = false

local LagState = States.Lag

function StartLineLag()
    if LagState.LineThread then task.cancel(LagState.LineThread); LagState.LineThread = nil end
    LagState.Line = true
    LagState.LineThread = task.spawn(function()
        while LagState.Line do
            for i = 1, LagState.LinesAmount do
                CreateGrabLine:FireServer(Workspace.SpawnLocation, CFrame.new(LagState.LinePosX, 0, LagState.LinePosZ))
            end
            task.wait(0.001)
        end
    end)
end

function StopLineLag()
    LagState.Line = false
    if LagState.LineThread then task.cancel(LagState.LineThread); LagState.LineThread = nil end
end

function StartPacketLag()
    if LagState.PacketThread then task.cancel(LagState.PacketThread); LagState.PacketThread = nil end
    LagState.Packet = true
    LagState.PacketThread = task.spawn(function()
        while LagState.Packet do
            local payload = LagState.PacketPrefix .. string.rep("0", LagState.PacketMultipler - #LagState.PacketPrefix)
            if LagState.AntiDetect then
                ExtendGrabLine:FireServer({ pitux = { payload } })
            else
                ExtendGrabLine:FireServer(payload)
            end
            task.wait(LagState.PacketInterval)
        end
    end)
end

function StopPacketLag()
    LagState.Packet = false
    if LagState.PacketThread then task.cancel(LagState.PacketThread); LagState.PacketThread = nil end
end

function SendOnePacket()
    local payload = LagState.PacketPrefix .. string.rep("0", LagState.PacketMultipler - #LagState.PacketPrefix)
    if LagState.AntiDetect then
        ExtendGrabLine:FireServer({ pitux = { payload } })
    else
        ExtendGrabLine:FireServer(payload)
    end
end

local KickAllState = States.KickAll

function getAllOtherPlayers()
    local list = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
            if KickAllState.Whitelist then
                local isFriend = false
                pcall(function() isFriend = LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) end)
                if isFriend then continue end
            end
            table.insert(list, plr)
        end
    end
    return list
end

function startLineLagSpam()
    if KickAllState.LineSpam then return end
    KickAllState.LineSpam = true
    task.spawn(function()
        if not CreateGrabLine then return end
        while KickAllState.LineSpam do
            local sp = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation") or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Spawn") or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            if sp then
                local rx, rz = math.random(-9e9, 9e9), math.random(-9e9, 9e9)
                for _, pos in pairs({
                    CFrame.new(rx, 0, rz), CFrame.new(-rx, 0, -rz),
                    CFrame.new(rx, 0, -rz), CFrame.new(-rx, 0, rz),
                }) do
                    pcall(function() CreateGrabLine:FireServer(sp, pos) end)
                end
            end
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
end

function stopLineLagSpam() KickAllState.LineSpam = false end

function ExecuteKickAllNoBlob()
    if KickAllState.Active then
        notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Kick all already running...", 2)
        return
    end
    KickAllState.Active = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local height = (KickAllState.Height == "Heaven") and 1e9 or 25
        local radius = (KickAllState.Height == "Heaven") and 40 or 18
        startLineLagSpam()
        task.wait(1)
        local players = getAllOtherPlayers()
        if #players == 0 then
            stopLineLagSpam(); KickAllState.Active = false
            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Kick all: No players found!", 3)
            return
        end
        local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myHrp then
            stopLineLagSpam(); KickAllState.Active = false
            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Kick all: Character missing!", 3)
            return
        end
        local playerData = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(players) do
            local hrp = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then table.insert(playerData, { player = plr, hrp = hrp }) end
        end
        if #playerData == 0 then
            stopLineLagSpam(); KickAllState.Active = false
            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Kick all: No valid targets!", 3)
            return
        end
        for _, data in ipairs(playerData) do
            pcall(function()
                myHrp.CFrame = data.hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 5)
                myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
            task.wait(0.2)
            pcall(function() SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(data.hrp, data.hrp.CFrame) end)
            task.wait()
        end

        local angleStep = (math.pi * 2) / #playerData
        for idx, data in ipairs(playerData) do
            local angle = (idx - 1) * angleStep
            local x = math.cos(angle) * radius
            local z = math.sin(angle) * radius
            local spawnPos = Vector3.new(x, height, z)

            pcall(function()
                data.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(spawnPos)
                data.hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)

            local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9); bp.P = 50000000
            bp.Position = spawnPos; bp.Parent = data.hrp
            task.delay(2, function() pcall(function() bp:Destroy() end) end)
            task.wait()
        end
        for i = 1, 8 do
            for _, data in ipairs(playerData) do
                pcall(function()
                    if CreateGrabLine then CreateGrabLine:FireServer(data.hrp, CFrame.new(0, 1e9, 0)) end
                    task.wait()
                    if DestroyGrabLine then DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(data.hrp) end
                end)
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
        stopLineLagSpam(); KickAllState.Active = false
        notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Kick all (No blob) completed!", 3)
    end)
end

local KickNotifyState = States.KickNotify

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if not KickNotifyState.Enabled or child.Name ~= "BlackHoleKick" then return end
    for _, v in Players:GetPlayers() do
        if v ~= LocalPlayer and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            and v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored
            and v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Name ~= "Kicked" then
            local lastGrabber = "\nLast Grabber: Unknown"
            local head = v.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local partOwner = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
            if partOwner then
                local owner = Players:FindFirstChild(partOwner.Value)
                if owner then
                    lastGrabber = "\nLast Grabber: " .. owner.DisplayName .. " [@" .. owner.Name .. "]"
                end
            end
            v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Name = "Kicked"
            KickNotifyState.KickCount += 1
            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS",
                v.DisplayName .. " [@" .. v.Name .. "] Kicked!" .. lastGrabber .. "\nTotal Kicks: " .. KickNotifyState.KickCount,
                KickNotifyState.Time, KickNotifyState.SoundId, v.UserId)
        end
    end
end)

local MainAvatarGroup = Tabs.Main:AddLeftGroupbox("Welcome", "chess-queen")
local thumbnail = string.format("rbxthumb://type=AvatarBust&id=%d&w=180&h=180", LocalPlayer.UserId)
pcall(function()
    local img = MainAvatarGroup:AddImage("MyIcon", {
        Image = thumbnail, Height = 180, Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Transparency = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
    })
    task.spawn(function()
        local holder = img and img.Holder
        local image = holder and holder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel", true)
        if not image then return end
        for attempt = 1, 6 do
            if not image.Parent then return end
            local ok, content, ready = pcall(function()
                return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size180x180)
            end)
            if ok and ready and type(content) == "string" and content ~= "" then
                image.Image = content; img.Image = content; return
            end
            task.wait(math.min(attempt * 0.5, 2))
            if image.IsLoaded then return end
        end
        if image.Parent then image.Image = thumbnail; img.Image = thumbnail end
    end)
end)

local hour = tonumber(os.date("%H")) or 12
local greeting = "Night"
if hour >= 5 and hour < 12 then greeting = "Morning"
elseif hour >= 12 and hour < 17 then greeting = "Afternoon"
elseif hour >= 17 and hour < 21 then greeting = "Evening"
end
MainAvatarGroup:AddLabel({ Text = ("%s, <b>%s</b>"):format(greeting, LocalPlayer.DisplayName), DoesWrap = true })
MainAvatarGroup:AddDivider()
MainAvatarGroup:AddLabel({ Text = "Welcome to <b><font color=\"#fffb00\">LEVIUS</font></b>", DoesWrap = true })
MainAvatarGroup:AddLabel({ Text = "Private Edition", DoesWrap = true })

local MainStatsGroup = Tabs.Main:AddRightGroupbox("Session Stats", "chart-no-axes-combined")
MainStatsGroup:AddLabel("CurrentTime", { Text = "Current time: --:--", DoesWrap = true })
MainStatsGroup:AddLabel("SessionTime", { Text = "Session: 0 seconds", DoesWrap = true })
MainStatsGroup:AddLabel("KickCount", { Text = "Kicked players: 0", DoesWrap = true })

task.spawn(function()
    local sessionStart = tick()
    while true do
        pcall(function()
            Library.Labels.CurrentTime:SetText("Current time: <b>" .. os.date("%H:%M") .. "</b>")
            Library.Labels.SessionTime:SetText("Session: <b>" .. math.floor(tick() - sessionStart) .. " seconds</b>")
            Library.Labels.KickCount:SetText("Kicked players: <b>" .. KickNotifyState.KickCount .. "</b>")
        end)
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

local MainServerGroup = Tabs.Main:AddRightGroupbox("Server", "server")
local AutoRejoinEnabled = false
local AutoRejoinJobId = game.JobId

MainServerGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Rejoin",
    Func = function()
        notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Rejoining server...", 2)
        local placeId = game.PlaceId
        local jobId = game.JobId
        if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
            LocalPlayer:Kick("Rejoining...")
            task.wait(0.25)
            TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer)
        else
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, jobId, LocalPlayer)
        end
    end,
})

MainServerGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Server Hop",
    Func = function()
        notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Searching for a new server...", 2)
        task.spawn(function()
            local placeId = game.PlaceId
            local jobId = game.JobId
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                local url = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"):format(placeId)
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(url))
            end)
            if not ok or not result or not result.data then
                notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Failed to fetch servers!", 3); return
            end
            local candidates = {}
            for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
                if server.id ~= jobId and server.playing < server.maxPlayers and server.playing > 0 then
                    table.insert(candidates, server)
                end
            end
            if #candidates == 0 then
                notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "No available servers found!", 3); return
            end
            local target = candidates[math.random(1, #candidates)]
            notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", ("Hopping to server (%d/%d)..."):format(target.playing, target.maxPlayers), 2)
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, target.id, LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end)
    end,
})

MainServerGroup:AddToggle("AutoRejoin", {
    Text = "Auto Rejoin",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v) AutoRejoinEnabled = v end,
})

game:GetService("GuiService").ErrorMessageChanged:Connect(function(msg)
    if AutoRejoinEnabled and msg and msg ~= "" then
        local lower = tostring(msg):lower()
        if lower:find("kicked") or lower:find("disconnected") or lower:find("error") then
            task.wait(1)
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, AutoRejoinJobId, LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end
    end
end)

-- Shared switches for attack-linked actions. These run only when Grab Kick
-- or Loop Kill (Target Only) is enabled; no separate attack toggle is needed.
LeviusShared.GucciAttack = {
    StealAntiKick = true,
    StealHoldables = true,
    BreakGucci = true,
    Mode = "Break",
}

-- The group must be created alongside the other Target groups, not at EOF.
do
    local controller = {
        Flags = {
            DestroyFood = false,
            RemoveGucci = false,
            RemoveInvisibility = false,
            DeleteSeatables = false,
        },
    }
    LeviusShared.GucciBreaker = controller

    function controller.GetTarget()
        local name = States.Dragonic.SelectedPlayer
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return nil end
        local player = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
        return player ~= LocalPlayer and player or nil
    end

    function controller.ApplyEnabled()
        local player = controller.GetTarget()
        if not player or not controller.Apply then return end
        for _, key in ipairs({"DestroyFood", "RemoveGucci", "RemoveInvisibility", "DeleteSeatables"}) do
            if controller.Flags[key] then controller.Apply(key, player) end
        end
    end
    LeviusShared.GucciBreakerTargetChanged = function()
        -- A changed dropdown must rebind every enabled selected-target feature.
        controller.ApplyEnabled()
        local bridge = LeviusShared.ResonanceTarget
        if bridge and type(bridge.OnTargetChanged) == "function" then
            bridge.OnTargetChanged()
        end
    end

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local group = Tabs.Target:AddRightGroupbox("Gucci Breaker / steal", "shield")
        local function addToggle(id, label, key)
            group:AddToggle(id, {
                Text = label,
                Default = false,
                Callback = function(enabled)
                    controller.Flags[key] = enabled == true
                    if controller.EnsureLoop then controller.EnsureLoop() end
                    if enabled and controller.Apply then
                        controller.Apply(key, controller.GetTarget())
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        addToggle("LeviusGB_DestroyFood", "Destroy Food", "DestroyFood")
        addToggle("LeviusGB_RemoveGucci", "Remove Gucci", "RemoveGucci")
        addToggle("LeviusGB_RemoveInvisibility", "Remove Invisibility", "RemoveInvisibility")
        addToggle("LeviusGB_DeleteSeatables", "Delete Seatables", "DeleteSeatables")

        -- Separate manual Gucci Breaker controls from attack-linked toy controls.
        group:AddDivider()
        group:AddLabel("Attack-linked Target Toys")
        local linked = LeviusShared.GucciAttack
        group:AddToggle("LeviusAttackStealAntiKick", {
            Text = "Steal Anti Kick", Default = true,
            Callback = function(value) linked.StealAntiKick = value == true end,
        })
        group:AddDivider()
        group:AddDropdown("LeviusAttackStealMode", {
            Text = "Steal Mode", Values = {"Hold", "Break", "Delete"},
            Default = "Break", Multi = false,
            Callback = function(value) linked.Mode = value end,
        })
        group:AddToggle("LeviusAttackStealHoldables", {
            Text = "Steal Holdables", Default = true,
            Callback = function(value) linked.StealHoldables = value == true end,
        })
        group:AddDivider()
        group:AddToggle("LeviusAttackBreakGucci", {
            Text = "Break Gucci", Default = true,
            Callback = function(value) linked.BreakGucci = value == true end,
        })

    end)
    if not ok then warn("[LEVIUS Gucci Breaker/UI] " .. tostring(err)) end
end

local NoBlobGroup = Tabs.Target:AddLeftGroupbox("No Blobman", "crosshair")

-- Resonance-style searchable multi-player target picker.
-- LEVIUS single-target features use the first selected player;
-- Grab Kick processes all selected players.
local LeviusGrabKickTargets = {}
local function SelectedGrabKickPlayers()
    local list = {}
    for player, enabled in pairs(LeviusGrabKickTargets) do
        if enabled and typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player")
            and player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Parent == Players then
            table.insert(list, player)
        end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.Name < b.Name end)
    return list
end
LeviusShared.GetAttackTargets = SelectedGrabKickPlayers
local TargetDropdown = NoBlobGroup:AddDropdown("SelectedTarget", {
    SpecialType = "Player",
    Default = nil,
    ExcludeLocalPlayer = true,
    Multi = true,
    Searchable = true,
    Text = "Targets",
    FormatListValue = function(player)
        if player == LocalPlayer then return "You" end
        return player.DisplayName ~= player.Name
            and string.format("%s (@%s)", player.DisplayName, player.Name)
            or player.Name
    end,
    FormatDisplayValue = function(player)
        if player == LocalPlayer then return "You" end
        return player.DisplayName ~= player.Name
            and string.format("%s (@%s)", player.DisplayName, player.Name)
            or player.Name
    end,
    EnablePlayerImages = true,
    Callback = function(value)
        table.clear(LeviusGrabKickTargets)
        if typeof(value) == "Instance" and value:IsA("Player") then
            LeviusGrabKickTargets[value] = true
        elseif type(value) == "table" then
            for player, enabled in pairs(value) do
                if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") then
                    LeviusGrabKickTargets[player] = enabled ~= false
                elseif typeof(enabled) == "Instance" and enabled:IsA("Player") then
                    LeviusGrabKickTargets[enabled] = true
                end
            end
        end
        local first = SelectedGrabKickPlayers()[1]
        Dragonic.SelectedPlayer = first and first.Name or nil
        if LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter then LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter.SetTarget(first) end
        if LeviusShared.GucciBreakerTargetChanged then
            task.defer(LeviusShared.GucciBreakerTargetChanged)
        end
        if LeviusShared.LoopKillTargetChanged then
            task.defer(LeviusShared.LoopKillTargetChanged)
        end
    end,
})

-- Count a credited Loop Kill only when that selected character respawns.
-- A function scope keeps the large script below Luau's local/register limits.
do
    (function()
        local previous = LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter
        if previous and previous.Cleanup then previous.Cleanup() end
        local label = NoBlobGroup:AddLabel("LEVIUS_LoopKillRespawnCount", {
            Text = "Loop Kill (Void) Count: 0",
            DoesWrap = true,
        })
        local counter = {Count = 0, Target = nil, Character = nil, Token = {}}
        local connections = {}
        local pendingCharacter = nil
        local function disconnect()
            for key, connection in pairs(connections) do
                connection:Disconnect()
                connections[key] = nil
            end
        end
        local function render()
            if label and label.SetText then
                label:SetText("Loop Kill (Void) Count: " .. tostring(counter.Count))
            end
        end
        function counter.Cleanup()
            disconnect()
            counter.Target, counter.Character = nil, nil
            counter.Token = {}
            pendingCharacter = nil
        end
        function counter.SetTarget(player)
            if player and (player == LocalPlayer or player.Parent ~= Players or isExcluded(player)) then player = nil end
            if player == counter.Target then return end
            disconnect()
            counter.Target = player
            counter.Character = player and player.Character or nil
            counter.Count = 0
            counter.Token = {}
            pendingCharacter = nil
            render()
            if not player then return end
            local token = counter.Token
            local function observeRespawn(character)
                if not character or counter.Token ~= token or counter.Target ~= player then return end
                if character == counter.Character then return end
                if pendingCharacter and pendingCharacter == counter.Character then
                    counter.Count = counter.Count + 1
                    pendingCharacter = nil
                    render()
                else
                    pendingCharacter = nil
                end
                counter.Character = character
            end
            connections.respawn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(observeRespawn)
            connections.characterProperty = player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
                observeRespawn(player.Character)
            end)
            connections.removed = player.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if parent ~= Players and counter.Token == token then counter.SetTarget(nil) end
            end)
        end
        function counter.TokenFor(player)
            if player and counter.Target == player then return counter.Token end
            return nil
        end
        function counter.Record(token, player, character)
            if player and character and token and token == counter.Token and counter.Target == player
                and counter.Character == character and player.Character == character then
                pendingCharacter = character
            end
        end
        LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter = counter
        counter.SetTarget(GetPlayerFromDisplay(TargetDropdown.Value))
    end)()
end

-- =====================================================
-- LEVIUS Target / Resonance methods bridge.
-- The real Resonance implementation is initialized in the Defense block below.
-- All Target UI callbacks use this shared bridge; no Nil'e toy loop remains.
-- =====================================================
do
    local bridge = LeviusShared.ResonanceTarget or {}
    LeviusShared.ResonanceTarget = bridge
    local enabled = false
    local generation = 0
    local previouslyActive = {}

    local function cleanupInactive()
        local current = {}
        for _, player in ipairs(SelectedGrabKickPlayers()) do current[player] = true end
        for player in pairs(previouslyActive) do
            if not enabled or not current[player] then
                if type(bridge.Cleanup) == "function" then
                    pcall(bridge.Cleanup, player)
                end
                previouslyActive[player] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    bridge.OnTargetChanged = function()
        cleanupInactive()
    end
    local function setGrabKick(value)
        enabled = value == true
        generation += 1
        local mine = generation
        if not enabled then
            cleanupInactive()
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            while enabled and mine == generation do
                local targets = SelectedGrabKickPlayers()
                cleanupInactive()
                for index, player in ipairs(targets) do
                    if not enabled or mine ~= generation then break end
                    if type(bridge.KickPass) == "function" then
                        local ok, result = pcall(bridge.KickPass, player, index, #targets)
                        if ok and result == true then previouslyActive[player] = true end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)
    end
    NoBlobGroup:AddToggle("GrabKick", {
        Text = "Grab Kick", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Height 15 | Loop Ragdoll ON | Remove Anti-Kick ON | Faster but worse",
        Callback = setGrabKick,
    })
end

-- Event-driven Loop Kill: cancel obsolete attempts and react to respawn readiness.
-- All new variables live in this scope, avoiding extra top-level local registers.
do
    local config = States.LoopKill
    config.Interval = config.Interval or 0.01 -- interval controlled by slider

    local connections = config.Connections
    local activeThreads = config.ActiveThreads
    local generation = 0
    local targetRevision = 0
    local worker = nil
    local observedPlayer = nil
    local observedCharacter = nil
    local nextAttempt = 0
    local enabled = false
    local warmup = nil
    local warmupAttempted = nil
    local respawnFastDeadline = nil

    local function disconnectTracked()
        for key, connection in pairs(connections) do
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
            connections[key] = nil
        end
    end

    local function wakeActiveOwnership()
        local wake = LeviusShared.LoopKillWakeActiveOwnership
        if wake then wake() end
    end

    local function reset()
        generation += 1
        warmup, warmupAttempted = nil, nil
        respawnFastDeadline = nil
        wakeActiveOwnership()
        if worker then pcall(task.cancel, worker); worker = nil end
        disconnectTracked()
        for key, threads in pairs(activeThreads) do
            if type(threads) == "table" then
                for _, thread in ipairs(threads) do pcall(task.cancel, thread) end
            end
            activeThreads[key] = nil
        end
        observedPlayer, observedCharacter = nil, nil
        nextAttempt = 0
    end

    local function selectedTarget()
        local player = GetPlayerFromDisplay(TargetDropdown.Value)
        if player and player ~= LocalPlayer and not isExcluded(player) then
            return player
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function attemptNow(force)
        if not enabled then return end
        local player = selectedTarget()
        local character = player and player.Character
        if not (character and character == observedCharacter and character.Parent) then return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not (root and root:IsA("BasePart") and root.Parent and not root.Anchored) then return end
        -- Acquire a newly replicated root while the target Humanoid is still
        -- streaming in. One warmup per root; it shares the normal ownership lock.
        if not humanoid then
            if warmup or warmupAttempted == root or VoidStates.Owning or VoidKillingPlayers[character] then return end
            if not respawnFastDeadline then respawnFastDeadline = os.clock() + 0.1 end
            local token = {generation = generation, revision = targetRevision, fastUntil = respawnFastDeadline}
            warmup, warmupAttempted = token, root
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, err = pcall(VoidSNO, root, 1.5, function()
                    local currentHumanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    return enabled and generation == token.generation and targetRevision == token.revision
                        and selectedTarget() == player and player.Character == character
                        and character.Parent ~= nil and root.Parent ~= nil and not root.Anchored
                        and (character:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) == root
                        and (not currentHumanoid or currentHumanoid.Health > 0)
                end, 22, true, token.fastUntil)
                if warmup == token then warmup = nil end
                if not ok then warn("[LEVIUS LoopKill/Ready] " .. tostring(err)) end
                task.defer(function() if enabled then attemptNow(false) end end)
            end)
            return
        end
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        -- A busy acquisition must not consume the new character's retry interval.
        if VoidKillingPlayers[character] or VoidStates.Owning then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if not force and now < nextAttempt then return end
        if not respawnFastDeadline then respawnFastDeadline = now + 0.1 end
        local fastUntil = respawnFastDeadline
        -- Set the next retry time before scheduling the asynchronous attempt.
        -- VoidKill also owns a per-character in-flight lock.
        nextAttempt = now + math.max(0.01, config.Interval or 0.05)
        local attemptGeneration, attemptRevision = generation, targetRevision
        local counter = LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter
        local countToken = counter and counter.TokenFor(player)
        local ok, result = pcall(VoidKill, player, function()
            return enabled and generation == attemptGeneration and targetRevision == attemptRevision
                and selectedTarget() == player and player.Character == character
                and character.Parent ~= nil and humanoid.Health > 0
                and root.Parent ~= nil and not root.Anchored
                and (character:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) == root
                and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") == humanoid
        end, function(killedPlayer, killedCharacter)
            if counter then counter.Record(countToken, killedPlayer, killedCharacter) end
        end, fastUntil)
        if not ok then warn("[LEVIUS LoopKill] " .. tostring(result)) end
    end

    LeviusShared.LoopKillOwnershipReady = function()
        if enabled then attemptNow(false) end
    end

    local function observeCharacter(character)
        targetRevision += 1
        observedCharacter = character
        nextAttempt = 0
        warmup, warmupAttempted = nil, nil
        respawnFastDeadline = nil
        -- Invalidate the old condition before waking its suspended acquisition.
        wakeActiveOwnership()
        if connections.characterChildren then
            connections.characterChildren:Disconnect()
            connections.characterChildren = nil
        end
        if connections.characterHealth then
            connections.characterHealth:Disconnect()
            connections.characterHealth = nil
        end
        if connections.characterAncestry then
            connections.characterAncestry:Disconnect()
            connections.characterAncestry = nil
        end
        if connections.characterRootAnchored then
            connections.characterRootAnchored:Disconnect()
            connections.characterRootAnchored = nil
        end
        if character then
            local function watchRoot()
                if connections.characterRootAnchored then
                    connections.characterRootAnchored:Disconnect()
                    connections.characterRootAnchored = nil
                end
                local root = character:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
                    connections.characterRootAnchored = root:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
                        wakeActiveOwnership()
                        if not root.Anchored then attemptNow(true) end
                    end)
                end
            end
            watchRoot()
            -- React synchronously when the required parts become usable.
            local function watchHumanoid(humanoid)
                if connections.characterHealth then
                    connections.characterHealth:Disconnect()
                    connections.characterHealth = nil
                end
                if humanoid and humanoid:IsA("Humanoid") then
                    connections.characterHealth = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
                        wakeActiveOwnership()
                        if health > 0 then attemptNow(true) end
                    end)
                end
            end
            watchHumanoid(character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
            connections.characterChildren = character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("Humanoid") then watchHumanoid(child) end
                if child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or child.Name == "realHrp" then watchRoot() end
                if child:IsA("Humanoid") or child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or child.Name == "realHrp" then
                    wakeActiveOwnership()
                    attemptNow(true)
                end
            end)
            -- Some games replicate character readiness through property changes.
            -- This supplements CharacterAdded without running an unbounded busy loop.
            connections.characterAncestry = character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if parent then attemptNow(true) end
            end)
            attemptNow(true)
        end
    end

    local function observePlayer(player)
        if player == observedPlayer then return end
        targetRevision += 1
        warmup, warmupAttempted = nil, nil
        respawnFastDeadline = nil
        wakeActiveOwnership()
        disconnectTracked()
        observedPlayer = player
        observedCharacter = nil
        nextAttempt = 0
        if player then
            connections.respawn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
                if enabled and selectedTarget() == player then
                    if character ~= observedCharacter then observeCharacter(character) end
                    -- Try immediately if the replicated character is already ready.
                    -- Otherwise ChildAdded and HealthChanged will trigger the first attempt.
                    attemptNow(true)
                end
            end)
            connections.characterProperty = player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
                if not enabled or selectedTarget() ~= player then return end
                local character = player.Character
                if character ~= observedCharacter then
                    observeCharacter(character)
                else
                    wakeActiveOwnership()
                    attemptNow(true)
                end
            end)
            connections.removed = player.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then observePlayer(nil) end
            end)
            observeCharacter(player.Character)
        end
    end

    -- Start the first valid attempt synchronously from the toggle callback.
    local function start()
        reset()
        enabled = true
        local currentGeneration = generation
        observePlayer(selectedTarget())
        attemptNow(true)
        worker = task.spawn(function()
            while enabled and currentGeneration == generation do
                local player = selectedTarget()
                if player ~= observedPlayer then
                    observePlayer(player)
                elseif player and player.Character ~= observedCharacter then
                    observeCharacter(player.Character)
                end
                attemptNow(false)
                -- Heartbeat still limits actual timing; this is a scheduling target.
                task.wait(math.max(0.01, math.min(0.03, config.Interval or 0.05)))
            end
        end)
    end

    LeviusShared.LoopKillTargetChanged = function()
        if not enabled then return end
        observePlayer(selectedTarget())
        attemptNow(true)
    end

    NoBlobGroup:AddSlider("LoopKillInterval", {
        Text = "Loop Kill Interval",
        Default = config.Interval,
        Min = 0.01,
        Max = 1,
        Rounding = 2,
        Compact = true,
        Callback = function(value)
            config.Interval = value
        end,
    })

    NoBlobGroup:AddToggle("LoopKill", {
        Text = "Loop Kill (Void)", Default = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            enabled = value
            VoidLoopKillActive = value
            if value then start() else reset() end
        end,
    })
end

local PenKillActive = false
local PenKillThread = nil
local PenKillRespawnConn = nil

NoBlobGroup:AddToggle("PenKill", {
    Text = "Pen Kill",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        PenKillActive = v
        if v then
            local target = GetPlayerFromDisplay(TargetDropdown.Value)
            if not target then
                notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Select a target first!", 2)
                local penKill = (Toggles and Toggles.PenKill) or (Options and Options.PenKill)
                if penKill then penKill:SetValue(false) end
                return
            end

            if PenKillRespawnConn then PenKillRespawnConn:Disconnect() end
            PenKillRespawnConn = target.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if PenKillActive then
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    local tHead = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if tHead then
                        local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                        if folder then
                            for _, obj in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                                if obj.Name == "ToolPencil" then
                                    pcall(function() DestroyT(obj) end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        local _, root = GetHRH()
                        if not root then return end
                        local spawnPos = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
                        folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                        if not folder then return end

                        local newPencil = nil
                        local conn = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                            if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then
                                newPencil = child
                                conn:Disconnect()
                            end
                        end)

                        task.spawn(function()
                            pcall(function() SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("ToolPencil", spawnPos, Vector3.zero) end)
                        end)

                        local startTime = tick()
                        repeat task.wait(0.05) until newPencil or (tick() - startTime) > 3
                        if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
                        if not newPencil then return end

                        local soundPart = newPencil:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                        local stickyPart = newPencil:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")

                        if soundPart then
                            pcall(function()
                                for _ = 1, 5 do SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame) end
                            end)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        if stickyPart then
                            pcall(function()
                                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, tHead, CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0))
                                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, tHead, CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0))
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            if PenKillThread then task.cancel(PenKillThread) end
            PenKillThread = task.spawn(function()
                while PenKillActive do
                    local target = GetPlayerFromDisplay(TargetDropdown.Value)
                    local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                    local count = 0
                    if folder then
                        for _, obj in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                            if obj.Name == "ToolPencil" then count = count + 1 end
                        end
                    end

                    local isWelded = false
                    if folder and target and target.Character then
                        local tHead = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if tHead then
                            for _, obj in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                                if obj.Name == "ToolPencil" then
                                    local sPart = obj:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                                    if sPart then
                                        local weld = sPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")
                                        if weld and weld.Part1 == tHead then
                                            isWelded = true
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if count == 0 or count >= 2 or (count == 1 and not isWelded) then
                        if folder then
                            for _, obj in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                                if obj.Name == "ToolPencil" then
                                    pcall(function() DestroyT(obj) end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait(0.05)

                        if target and target.Character then
                            local tHead = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                            local _, root = GetHRH()
                            if tHead and root then
                                local spawnPos = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
                                folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                                if folder then
                                    local newPencil = nil
                                    local conn = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                                        if child.Name == "ToolPencil" then                                            newPencil = child
                                            conn:Disconnect()
                                        end
                                    end)

                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        pcall(function() SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("ToolPencil", spawnPos, Vector3.zero) end)
                                    end)

                                    local startTime = tick()
                                    repeat task.wait(0.05) until newPencil or (tick() - startTime) > 3
                                    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end

                                    if newPencil then
                                        local soundPart = newPencil:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                        local stickyPart = newPencil:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")

                                        if soundPart then
                                            pcall(function()
                                                for _ = 1, 5 do SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame) end
                                            end)
                                        end

                                        task.wait(0.05)

                                        if stickyPart then
                                            pcall(function()
                                                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, tHead, CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0))
                                                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, tHead, CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0))
                                            end)
                                            task.wait(0.5)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        else
            if PenKillThread then
                task.cancel(PenKillThread)
                PenKillThread = nil
            end
            if PenKillRespawnConn then
                PenKillRespawnConn:Disconnect()
                PenKillRespawnConn = nil
            end
            local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            if folder then
                for _, obj in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if obj.Name == "ToolPencil" then
                        pcall(function() DestroyT(obj) end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})

NoBlobGroup:AddToggle("RagdollKill", {
    Text = "Ragdoll Kill",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer or Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == "" then
                notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Select a target first!", 2); return
            end
            DragonicStartRagdollKill()
        else
            DragonicStopRagdollKill()
        end
    end,
})
NoBlobGroup:AddToggle("LoopBanana", {
    Text = "Loop Banana",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            if not Dragonic.SelectedPlayer or Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == "" then
                notifyWithSound("LEVIUS", "Select a target first!", 2); return
            end
            BananaStopLoop(); BananaStartLoop()
        else
            BananaStopLoop()
        end
    end,
})

do
    local HighlightBox = Tabs.Target:AddRightGroupbox("Target Highlight", "crosshair")

    local highlightScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    highlightScreenGui.Name = "LeviusTargetHighlight"
    highlightScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    highlightScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true

    local getCoreGui = (get_hidden_gui or gethui)
    local coreGui = getCoreGui and getCoreGui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    pcall(function()
        local old = (coreGui and coreGui:FindFirstChild("LeviusTargetHighlight"))
            or (LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("LeviusTargetHighlight"))
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end)

    local successCore = pcall(function() highlightScreenGui.Parent = coreGui end)
    if not successCore or not highlightScreenGui.Parent then
        pcall(function() highlightScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end)
    end

    local highlightImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    highlightImage.Name = "HighlightImage"
    highlightImage.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    highlightImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    highlightImage.Image = "rbxassetid://124632728813211"
    highlightImage.Visible = false
    highlightImage.Parent = highlightScreenGui

    local highlightGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    highlightGradient.Parent = highlightImage

    HighlightBox:AddToggle("HighlightEnabled", {
        Text    = "Enabled",
        Default = false,
    }):AddColorPicker("HighlightColor1", {
        Default = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        Title   = "Color 1",
    }):AddColorPicker("HighlightColor2", {
        Default = Color3.fromRGB(215, 168, 255),
        Title   = "Color 2",
    })

    HighlightBox:AddDropdown("HighlightTargetMode", {
        Text    = "Target Mode",
        Values  = { "Selected Target", "Closest Player" },
        Default = "Selected Target",
        Multi   = false,
    })

    HighlightBox:AddDropdown("HighlightImageSelect", {
        Text    = "Highlight Image",
        Values  = { "Corner", "Triangle", "Star" },
        Default = "Corner",
        Callback = function(val)
            local ids = {
                Corner   = "rbxassetid://124632728813211",
                Triangle = "rbxassetid://121941578628282",
                Star     = "rbxassetid://102878460627329",
            }
            if highlightImage and ids[val] then
                highlightImage.Image = ids[val]
            end
        end
    })

    HighlightBox:AddSlider("HighlightLerp", {
        Text     = "Lerp",
        Default  = 0.2,
        Min      = 0.01,
        Max      = 1,
        Rounding = 2,
    })

    HighlightBox:AddSlider("HighlightRotateSpeed", {
        Text     = "Rotate Speed",
        Default  = 5,
        Min      = 0,
        Max      = 20,
        Rounding = 1,
    })

    HighlightBox:AddSlider("HighlightPulseSpeed", {
        Text     = "Pulse Speed",
        Default  = 5,
        Min      = 0,
        Max      = 20,
        Rounding = 1,
    })

    HighlightBox:AddSlider("HighlightMinSize", {
        Text     = "Min Size",
        Default  = 50,
        Min      = 10,
        Max      = 200,
        Rounding = 0,
    })

    HighlightBox:AddSlider("HighlightMaxSize", {
        Text     = "Max Size",
        Default  = 80,
        Min      = 10,
        Max      = 300,
        Rounding = 0,
    })

    local currentHighlightPos = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    local highlightTargetStr = nil

    function getClosestOtherPlayer()
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myRoot then return nil end

        local closest = nil
        local shortestDist = math.huge
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local dist = (hrp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < shortestDist then
                        shortestDist = dist
                        closest = plr
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return closest
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait()

            local isEnabled = false
            if Toggles and Toggles.HighlightEnabled then
                isEnabled = Toggles.HighlightEnabled.Value
            elseif Options and Options.HighlightEnabled then
                isEnabled = Options.HighlightEnabled.Value
            end

            if not isEnabled then
                if highlightImage.Visible then highlightImage.Visible = false end
                continue
            end

            local targetPlayer = nil
            local mode = Options.HighlightTargetMode and Options.HighlightTargetMode.Value or "Selected Target"

            if mode == "Selected Target" then
                targetPlayer = GetPlayerFromDisplay(TargetDropdown.Value)
                if not targetPlayer and Dragonic.SelectedPlayer then
                    targetPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(Dragonic.SelectedPlayer)
                end
            elseif mode == "Closest Player" then
                targetPlayer = getClosestOtherPlayer()
            end

            local targetChar = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character
            local hrp = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if camera then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                    if onScreen then
                        local targetVector = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
                        local targetName = targetPlayer.Name

                        if highlightTargetStr ~= targetName or not highlightImage.Visible then
                            if not highlightImage.Visible then
                                currentHighlightPos = targetVector
                            end
                            highlightTargetStr = targetName
                        end

                        highlightImage.Visible = true

                        local lerpFactor = Options.HighlightLerp and Options.HighlightLerp.Value or 0.2
                        currentHighlightPos = currentHighlightPos:Lerp(targetVector, lerpFactor)
                        highlightImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, currentHighlightPos.X, 0, currentHighlightPos.Y)

                        local minSize = Options.HighlightMinSize and Options.HighlightMinSize.Value or 50
                        local maxSize = Options.HighlightMaxSize and Options.HighlightMaxSize.Value or 80
                        local pulseSpeed = Options.HighlightPulseSpeed and Options.HighlightPulseSpeed.Value or 5
                        local sine = (math.sin(tick() * pulseSpeed) + 1) / 2
                        local currentSize = minSize + (maxSize - minSize) * sine
                        highlightImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, currentSize, 0, currentSize)

                        local rotSpeed = Options.HighlightRotateSpeed and Options.HighlightRotateSpeed.Value or 5
                        highlightImage.Rotation = highlightImage.Rotation + rotSpeed

                        local c1 = Options.HighlightColor1 and Options.HighlightColor1.Value or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                        local c2 = Options.HighlightColor2 and Options.HighlightColor2.Value or Color3.fromRGB(215, 168, 255)
                        highlightGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c1),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c2)
                        }
                    else
                        highlightImage.Visible = false
                    end
                else
                    highlightImage.Visible = false
                end
            else
                highlightImage.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

local AKill = (function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local antiGrabActive = false
    local antiGrabRenderConn = nil
    local antiGrabCharConn = nil

    local GRAB_ANIMATION_ID = "rbxassetid://7047322890"

    local STOMP_DURATION       = 1.2
    local SELF_ANCHOR_STRENGTH = 3
    local stompActive          = false
    local stompEndTime         = 0
    local stompCooldownUntil   = 0
    local stompConnectionStep  = nil
    local stompConnectionBeat  = nil
    local stompSaved           = {}
    local stompSavedParts      = {}
    local stompSavedJoints     = {}
    local stompFixedCFrame     = nil

    local TORSO_NAMES = { "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Chest" }

    local BLOBKILL_DURATION    = 1.2
    local blobkillBurstActive  = false
    local blobkillBurstTask    = nil
    local blobkillBurstEndTime = 0

    local RAGDOLL_DURATION    = 1.2
    local ragdollBurstActive  = false
    local ragdollBurstTask    = nil
    local ragdollBurstEndTime = 0

    local watchedParts = {}
    local partOwnerConns = {}
    local characterWatchConnections = {}

    local StartStomp, EndStomp, StopGrabAnimation, ApplyStompFrame

    function watchPart(part)
        if watchedParts[part] then return end
        if not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        if not part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then return end
        watchedParts[part] = true
        local owner = part.PartOwner
        partOwnerConns[part] = owner:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
            if not antiGrabActive then return end
            if owner.Value ~= "" then
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if char then
                    StartStomp(char)
                    pcall(function()
                        ReplicatedStorage.CharacterEvents.Struggle:FireServer(LocalPlayer)
                        local rootPart = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if rootPart then
                            ReplicatedStorage.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote:FireServer(rootPart, 0.00000000001)
                        end
                    end)
                    StopGrabAnimation(char)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function unwatchPart(part)
        if partOwnerConns[part] then partOwnerConns[part]:Disconnect(); partOwnerConns[part] = nil end
        watchedParts[part] = nil
    end

    function setupCharacterWatch(character)
        if not character then return end
        for _, d in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do watchPart(d) end
        characterWatchConnections.added = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if antiGrabActive then watchPart(d) end
        end)
        characterWatchConnections.removing = character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(d) unwatchPart(d) end)
    end

    function teardownCharacterWatch()
        for key, connection in pairs(characterWatchConnections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
            characterWatchConnections[key] = nil
        end
        for part in pairs(watchedParts) do unwatchPart(part) end
        watchedParts = {}
        partOwnerConns = {}
    end

    function IsGrabbed(character)
        if not character then return false end
        for _, part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and part.PartOwner.Value ~= "" then return true, part end
        end
        return false, nil
    end

    function IsRagdolled(character)
        if not character then return false end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return false end
        local state = humanoid:GetState()
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    StopGrabAnimation = function(character)
        if not character then return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end
        for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == GRAB_ANIMATION_ID then track:Stop() end
        end
    end

    function GetTorsoParts(character)
        local parts = {}
        if not character then return parts end
        for _, name in ipairs(TORSO_NAMES) do
            local p = character:FindFirstChild(name)
            if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(parts, p) end
        end
        return parts
    end

    function AntiBlobmanKillStep()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        hum.Sit = true
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            local lookVec = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + Vector3.new(lookVec.X, 0, lookVec.Z))
        end
    end

    function TriggerAntiBlobmanKillBurst()
        blobkillBurstEndTime = tick() + BLOBKILL_DURATION
        if blobkillBurstActive then return end
        blobkillBurstActive = true
        blobkillBurstTask = task.spawn(function()
            while blobkillBurstActive do
                pcall(AntiBlobmanKillStep)
                if tick() >= blobkillBurstEndTime then blobkillBurstActive = false; break end
                task.wait()
            end
            blobkillBurstTask = nil
        end)
    end

    function StopAntiBlobmanKillBurst()
        blobkillBurstActive = false
        if blobkillBurstTask then task.cancel(blobkillBurstTask); blobkillBurstTask = nil end
    end

    function RagdollBurstStep()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local rp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not rp or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll)
        hum.PlatformStand = true
        rp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        rp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end

    function TriggerRagdollBurst()
        ragdollBurstEndTime = tick() + RAGDOLL_DURATION
        if ragdollBurstActive then return end
        ragdollBurstActive = true
        ragdollBurstTask = task.spawn(function()
            while ragdollBurstActive do
                pcall(RagdollBurstStep)
                if tick() >= ragdollBurstEndTime then ragdollBurstActive = false; break end
                task.wait()
            end
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false; hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
            ragdollBurstTask = nil
        end)
    end

    function StopRagdollBurst()
        ragdollBurstActive = false
        if ragdollBurstTask then task.cancel(ragdollBurstTask); ragdollBurstTask = nil end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
    end

    ApplyStompFrame = function()
        if not stompActive then return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local rp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not rp then return end
        local parts = GetTorsoParts(char)
        for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
            p.Anchored = true
            p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            pcall(function() p.Velocity = Vector3.zero; p.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
        end
        if stompFixedCFrame then
            if SELF_ANCHOR_STRENGTH >= 2 then
                rp.CFrame = stompFixedCFrame
            else
                local t = math.clamp(SELF_ANCHOR_STRENGTH * 0.5, 0, 1)
                rp.CFrame = rp.CFrame:Lerp(stompFixedCFrame, t)
            end
        end
        rp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        rp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        pcall(function() rp.Velocity = Vector3.zero; rp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
        local rootJoint = char:FindFirstChild("RootJoint")
        if rootJoint and stompSavedJoints[rootJoint] then
            local saved = stompSavedJoints[rootJoint]
            pcall(function() rootJoint.C0 = saved.C0; rootJoint.C1 = saved.C1 end)
        end
        hum.PlatformStand = true
    end

    EndStomp = function()
        if not stompActive then return end
        stompActive = false
        stompFixedCFrame = nil
        if stompConnectionStep then stompConnectionStep:Disconnect(); stompConnectionStep = nil end
        if stompConnectionBeat then stompConnectionBeat:Disconnect(); stompConnectionBeat = nil end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum.WalkSpeed = stompSaved.WalkSpeed or 16
            hum.JumpPower = stompSaved.JumpPower or 50
            hum.JumpHeight = stompSaved.JumpHeight or 7.2
            hum.AutoRotate = stompSaved.AutoRotate ~= false
            hum.PlatformStand = stompSaved.PlatformStand or false
        end
        if char then
            local parts = GetTorsoParts(char)
            for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
                local saved = stompSavedParts[p]
                p.Anchored = saved and saved.Anchored or false
            end
            local rj = char:FindFirstChild("RootJoint")
            if rj and stompSavedJoints[rj] then
                local s = stompSavedJoints[rj]
                pcall(function() rj.C0 = s.C0; rj.C1 = s.C1 end)
            end
        end
        stompSavedParts = {}
        stompSavedJoints = {}
        stompCooldownUntil = tick() + 0.15
    end

    StartStomp = function(character)
        if not character then return end
        if stompActive then return end
        if tick() < stompCooldownUntil then return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoid or not root then return end
        stompActive = true
        stompEndTime = tick() + STOMP_DURATION
        TriggerAntiBlobmanKillBurst()
        TriggerRagdollBurst()
        stompSaved = {
            WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed, JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower,
            JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight, AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate,
            PlatformStand = humanoid.PlatformStand,
        }
        stompSavedParts = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(GetTorsoParts(character)) do
            stompSavedParts[p] = { Anchored = p.Anchored, CFrame = p.CFrame }
            p.Anchored = true
            p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
