    local list = actionQueues[key]
    if not list then
        list = {}
        actionQueues[key] = list
    end

    if not table.find(list, player) then
        table.insert(list, player)
    end

    if actionQueueRunning[key] then
        return true
    end

    actionQueueRunning[key] = true

    task.spawn(function()
        while #list > 0 do
            local item = table.remove(list, 1)
            if item and item.Parent then
                local ok, err = pcall(callback, item)
                if not ok then
                    warn(
                        ("[Resonance Target/queue:%s] %s"):format(
                            tostring(key),
                            tostring(err)
                        )
                    )
                end
                task.wait()
            end
        end
        actionQueueRunning[key] = nil
    end)

    return true
end

function H.clearAllQueues(toggleKey)
    if toggleKey then
        loopGenerations[toggleKey] = (loopGenerations[toggleKey] or 0) + 1
    else
        for key in pairs(loopGenerations) do
            loopGenerations[key] = loopGenerations[key] + 1
        end
    end

    for _, list in pairs(actionQueues) do
        table.clear(list)
    end

    table.clear(state.NetworkKickQueue)
end

function H.GetTargets(isServer, selectedPlayers)
    local result = {}
    if isServer then
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and not state.Whitelisted[player] then
                table.insert(result, player)
            end
        end
        return result
    end

    for player, enabled in pairs(selectedPlayers or {}) do
        local resolved = nil
        local selected = enabled ~= false

        if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") then
            resolved = player
        elseif type(player) == "string" then
            resolved = Players:FindFirstChild(player)
        elseif typeof(enabled) == "Instance" and enabled:IsA("Player") then
            resolved = enabled
            selected = true
        elseif type(enabled) == "string" then
            resolved = Players:FindFirstChild(enabled)
            selected = true
        end

        if resolved
            and selected
            and resolved ~= LocalPlayer
            and not state.Whitelisted[resolved]
        then
            table.insert(result, resolved)
        end
    end
    return result
end

function H.ProcessPlayer(player, loopCall, isServer, isBlob)
    if isBlob then
        return
    end

    if not player or player == LocalPlayer or state.Whitelisted[player] then
        return
    end

    local methods = state.TargetMethods

    if methodSelected(methods, "Kick") then
        if not loopCall or isServer then
            H.NetworkKick(player, loopCall, isServer)
        end
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Kill") then
        if loopCall then
            H.queue("Kill", H.kill, player)
        else
            H.kill(player)
        end
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Void") then
        if loopCall then
            H.queue("Void", H.void, player)
        else
            H.void(player)
        end
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Remove Gucci") then
        if loopCall then
            H.queue("Remove Gucci", H.RemoveGucci, player)
        else
            H.RemoveGucci(player)
        end
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Destroy Food") then
        if loopCall then
            H.queue("Destroy Food", H.EatAntiInput, player)
        else
            H.EatAntiInput(player)
        end
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Remove Invisibility") then
        H.RemoveInvisibility(player)
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Delete Seatables") then
        if loopCall then
            H.queue("Delete Seatables", H.DeleteSeatables, player)
        else
            H.DeleteSeatables(player)
        end
    end

    if methodSelected(methods, "Bring") then
        local _, targetRoot, targetHumanoid = H.charp(player)
        local _, localRoot = H.charp()
        if targetRoot
            and localRoot
            and targetHumanoid
            and targetHumanoid.Health ~= 0
            and H.sno(targetRoot)
        then
            H.teleport(
                targetRoot,
                localRoot,
                CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
            )
            task.delay(0.2, H.unsno, targetRoot)
        end
    end
end

local function runSelectedNetworkKickLoop()
    while task.wait() do
        local loopEnabled =
            runtimeFlags.LoopApplyMethodT == true

        local kickEnabled =
            methodSelected(
                state.TargetMethods,
                "Kick"
            )

        local inPlotObject =
            LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

        local inPlot =
            inPlotObject
            and inPlotObject.Value == true

        for player in pairs(selectedKickActive) do
            if not player
                or not player.Parent
                or not loopEnabled
                or not kickEnabled
                or inPlot
                or state.SelectedPlayers[player] ~= true
            then
                pcall(
                    cleanupNetworkKickPlayer,
                    player
                )
                selectedKickActive[player] = nil
            end
        end

        if loopEnabled
            and kickEnabled
            and not inPlot
        then
            local _, localRoot, localHumanoid =
                H.charp()

            if localRoot
                and localHumanoid
                and localHumanoid.Health > 0
            then
                local batch = {}

                for player, enabled in pairs(
                    state.SelectedPlayers
                ) do
                    if enabled == true
                        and typeof(player) == "Instance"
                        and player:IsA("Player")
                        and player.Parent
                    then
                        table.insert(
                            batch,
                            player
                        )
                        selectedKickActive[player] = true
                    end
                end

                local count = #batch

                if count > 0 then
                    for index, player in ipairs(batch) do
                        if runtimeFlags.LoopApplyMethodT ~= true then
                            break
                        end

                        local currentInPlot =
                            LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

                        if currentInPlot
                            and currentInPlot.Value == true
                        then
                            break
                        end

                        local _, currentRoot, currentHumanoid =
                            H.charp()

                        if not currentRoot
                            or not currentHumanoid
                            or currentHumanoid.Health <= 0
                        then
                            break
                        end

                        pcall(
                            performNetworkKickPass,
                            player,
                            index,
                            count,
                            true
                        )
                    end

                    if settings.NKDelayMode
                        == "Slower but better"
                    then
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function safeProcessPlayer(player, loopCall, isServer, isBlob)
    local ok, result = pcall(
        H.ProcessPlayer,
        player,
        loopCall,
        isServer,
        isBlob
    )

    if not ok then
        local key = typeof(player) == "Instance" and player.Name or tostring(player)
        local now = tick()
        local last = targetErrorTimes[key] or 0
        if now - last >= 1 then
            targetErrorTimes[key] = now
            warn(
                ("[Resonance Target] %s: %s"):format(
                    key,
                    tostring(result)
                )
            )
        end
        return false, result
    end

    return true, result
end

local function validateTargetRuntime()
    refreshRemotes()

    local missing = {}
    if not Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation") then
        table.insert(missing, "workspace.SpawnLocation")
    end
    if not Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") then
        table.insert(missing, "workspace.Plots")
    end
    if not Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems") then
        table.insert(missing, "workspace.PlotItems")
    end
    if not remotes.sno then
        table.insert(missing, "GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner")
    end
    if not remotes.dgl then
        table.insert(missing, "GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine")
    end

    if #missing > 0 then
        warn("[Resonance Target/self-check] missing: " .. table.concat(missing, ", "))
        return false, missing
    end

    return true, missing
end

H.ValidateTargetRuntime = validateTargetRuntime

task.spawn(runNetworkKickWorker)
task.spawn(runSelectedNetworkKickLoop)
task.defer(validateTargetRuntime)

local function syncSelectedPlayers(value)
    table.clear(state.SelectedPlayers)
    if typeof(value) == "Instance" and value:IsA("Player") then
        state.SelectedPlayers[value] = true
    elseif type(value) == "table" then
        for player, enabled in pairs(value) do
            if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") then
                state.SelectedPlayers[player] = enabled ~= false
            elseif typeof(enabled) == "Instance" and enabled:IsA("Player") then
                state.SelectedPlayers[enabled] = true
            end
        end
    end
end

local function createPlayerDropdown(groupbox, index, values, multi, addKeypicker, callback)
    if not groupbox or not values then
        notify("Something went wrong, tell the owner about this notification.")
        return
    end

    addKeypicker = addKeypicker ~= false
    multi = multi ~= false

    local record = {
        Dropdown = nil,
        Values = values,
        Selected = {},
        Index = index,
    }

    local dropdown = groupbox:AddDropdown(index .. "Dropdown", {
        SpecialType = "Player",
        Default = nil,
        ExcludeLocalPlayer = true,
        Multi = multi,
        Searchable = true,
        Text = "Targets",
        FormatListValue = H.FormatPlayer,
        FormatDisplayValue = H.FormatPlayer,
        EnablePlayerImages = true,
        Callback = function(value)

            local snapshot = {}
            if multi and type(value) == "table" then
                for player, enabled in pairs(value) do
                    snapshot[player] = enabled
                end
            elseif value then
                snapshot[value] = true
            end

            table.clear(values)
            table.clear(record.Selected)

            for player, enabled in pairs(snapshot) do
                local resolved = nil
                local selected = enabled ~= false

                if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") then
                    resolved = player
                elseif type(player) == "string" then
                    resolved = Players:FindFirstChild(player)
                elseif typeof(enabled) == "Instance" and enabled:IsA("Player") then
                    resolved = enabled
                    selected = true
                elseif type(enabled) == "string" then
                    resolved = Players:FindFirstChild(enabled)
                    selected = true
                end

                if resolved and resolved:IsA("Player") then
                    values[resolved] = selected
                    record.Selected[resolved] = selected
                end
            end

            if callback then
                callback(value)
            end
        end,
    })

    record.Dropdown = dropdown
    table.insert(state.PlayerDropdowns, record)

    local function togglePlayer(player)
        if not player then
            return
        end
        if multi then
            local newValue = {}
            for existingPlayer, enabled in pairs(dropdown.Value or {}) do
                if existingPlayer ~= player then
                    newValue[existingPlayer] = enabled
                end
            end
            if dropdown.Value and dropdown.Value[player] then
                notify(("Deselected %* in %*!"):format(player.Name, index))
            else
                newValue[player] = true
                notify(("Selected %* in %*!"):format(player.Name, index))
            end
            dropdown:SetValue(newValue)
        else
            dropdown:SetValue(player)
            notify(("Selected %* in %*!"):format(player.Name, index))
        end
    end

    if addKeypicker then
        local label = groupbox:AddLabel("Add To List")
        label:AddKeyPicker(index .. "DropdownST", {
            Text = "Add To List",
            Mode = "Press",
            NoUI = true,
            Callback = function()
                local target = Mouse.Target
                if not target or not target.Parent then
                    return
                end
                local player = Players:FindFirstChild(target.Parent.Name)
                if player and player:IsA("Player") then
                    togglePlayer(player)
                end
            end,
        })
    end

    return dropdown
end

local function processLoop(toggleKey, isServer, selectedPlayers, generation)
    while runtimeFlags[toggleKey]
        and loopGenerations[toggleKey] == generation
    do
        for _, player in ipairs(H.GetTargets(isServer, selectedPlayers)) do
            if not runtimeFlags[toggleKey]
                or loopGenerations[toggleKey] ~= generation
            then
                break
            end
            safeProcessPlayer(player, true, isServer, false)
        end
        task.wait()
    end
end

local function createLoopToggle(groupbox, id, toggleKey, isServer, isBlob, selectedPlayers)
    local label = isServer and "Server" or "Selected"
    local targetDescription = isServer and "all" or "selected"
    groupbox:AddToggle(id, {
        Text = label,
        Default = false,
        Tooltip = targetDescription .. " players",
        Callback = function(enabled)
            runtimeFlags[toggleKey] = enabled
            loopGenerations[toggleKey] = (loopGenerations[toggleKey] or 0) + 1
            local generation = loopGenerations[toggleKey]

            if enabled then
                task.spawn(
                    processLoop,
                    toggleKey,
                    isServer,
                    selectedPlayers,
                    generation
                )
            else
                H.clearAllQueues(toggleKey)
            end
        end,
    })
end

local function createApplyButton(groupbox, isServer, isBlob, selectedPlayers)
    local label = isServer and "Server" or "Selected"
    groupbox:AddButton({
        Text = label,
        Func = function()
            local targets = H.GetTargets(isServer, selectedPlayers)
            if #targets == 0 then
                notify("No valid targets selected.", "Target")
                return
            end

            for _, player in ipairs(targets) do
                safeProcessPlayer(player, false, isServer, isBlob)
            end
        end,
    })
end

local function handleCounterAttackChild(child, localRoot)
    if not child:IsA("StringValue") or child.Name ~= "PartOwner" then
        return
    end

    local player = Players:FindFirstChild(child.Value)
    if not player then
        return
    end

    state.WhoGrabbed = H.FormatPlayer(player)
    if not runtimeFlags.CounterAttack or state.Whitelisted[player] then
        return
    end

    local _, targetRoot = H.charp(player)
    if settings.CounterMethod == "Kill" then
        H.kill(player)
    elseif settings.CounterMethod == "Void" then
        H.void(player)
    elseif settings.CounterMethod == "Grab" then
        H.grab(targetRoot)
    elseif settings.CounterMethod == "Delete Limbs" then
        H.breaklimb(player, settings.TargetedLimbCA)
    elseif settings.CounterMethod == "Fling" then
        if targetRoot and localRoot and H.sno(targetRoot) then
            targetRoot.Velocity = localRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 100 + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)
        end
    elseif settings.CounterMethod == "Teleport to Spawn" then
        if targetRoot and H.sno(targetRoot) then
            H.teleport(targetRoot, CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
        end
    end
end

local function bindCounterAttack(character)
    local _, rootPart = H.charp()
    local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then
        return
    end
    if activeConnections.CounterAttack then
        activeConnections.CounterAttack:Disconnect()
    end
    activeConnections.CounterAttack = head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        handleCounterAttackChild(child, rootPart)
    end)
end

if LocalPlayer.Character then
    task.defer(bindCounterAttack, LocalPlayer.Character)
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    task.wait()
    bindCounterAttack(character)
end)

local function getMap()
    return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
end

local function getInOwnedPlotValue()
    local value = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot", true)
    if value and value:IsA("BoolValue") then
        return value.Value
    end
    return false
end

local function isLocallySpawnedToy(instance)
    local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    return folder and instance:IsDescendantOf(folder) or false
end

local function isGrabbed()
    return IsHeld.Value == true
end

local antiGrabConnection = nil
local antiGrabSavedCFrame = nil

local function stopAntiGrab()
    if antiGrabConnection then
        antiGrabConnection:Disconnect()
        antiGrabConnection = nil
    end

    state.States["Anti Grab"] = false

    local _, _, humanoid = H.charp()

    if humanoid then
        if settings.AGType == "Anti-Kill" then
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                if humanoid.Parent then
                    humanoid.Sit = false
                end
            end)
        end

        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
    end
end

local function startAntiGrabIfNeeded()
    if not runtimeFlags.AntiGrab
        or not IsHeld.Value
        or state.States["Anti Grab"]
    then
        return
    end

    refreshRemotes()

    if remotes.Struggle then
        fireRemote(remotes.Struggle)
    end

    local character, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp()
    local torso =
        character
        and character:FindFirstChild("Torso")

    local neck =
        torso
        and torso:FindFirstChild("Neck")

    if not humanoid or not torso then
        return
    end

    humanoid.RequiresNeck = false
    H.resetVelocity(rootPart or torso)

    antiGrabSavedCFrame = torso.CFrame
    state.States["Anti Grab"] = true

    local buggy =
        settings.AGType
        == "Escapes Anything, but buggy"

    local antiKill =
        settings.AGType
        == "Anti-Kill"

    local noRagdoll =
        settings.AGType
        == "No Ragdoll"

    if buggy then
        task.spawn(H.ExtraEscape)
    end

    local lastExtraEscape = tick()

    antiGrabConnection =
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not runtimeFlags.AntiGrab or not IsHeld.Value
                or LocalPlayer.Character ~= character
                or not humanoid.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                stopAntiGrab()
                return
            end
            if remotes.Struggle then
                fireRemote(remotes.Struggle)
            end

            if rootPart
                and not buggy
                and not noRagdoll
                and remotes.RagdollRemote
            then
                fireRemote(
                    remotes.RagdollRemote,
                    rootPart,
                    antiKill and 0.11 or 0
                )
            end

            if buggy then
                local now = tick()

                if now - lastExtraEscape > 2 then
                    task.spawn(H.ExtraEscape)
                    lastExtraEscape = tick()
                end
            end

            local moveDirection =
                humanoid.MoveDirection

            local speed = 16

            if humanoid.WalkSpeed > 16 then
                speed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
            end

            if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                antiGrabSavedCFrame =
                    antiGrabSavedCFrame
                    + (
                        moveDirection.Unit
                        * speed
                        * dt
                    )

                local horizontal =
                    Vector3.new(
                        moveDirection.X,
                        0,
                        moveDirection.Z
                    )

                if horizontal.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local position =
                        antiGrabSavedCFrame.Position

                    local look =
                        CFrame.lookAt(
                            position,
                            position
                                + horizontal.Unit
                        )

                    antiGrabSavedCFrame =
                        antiGrabSavedCFrame:Lerp(
                            look,
                            0.12
                        )
                end
            end

            H.resetVelocity(
                rootPart or torso
            )

            torso.CFrame =
                antiGrabSavedCFrame

            humanoid.Sit =
                antiKill

            task.spawn(
                H.ToggleCollisions,
                character,
                false
            )

            if not neck
                or not neck.Enabled
            then
                task.spawn(
                    H.RepositionLimbs,
                    character
                )
            end

            for _, track in ipairs(
                humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
            ) do
                local animation =
                    track.Animation

                if animation
                    and animation.AnimationId
                        == "rbxassetid://7047322890"
                then
                    track:Stop()
                end
            end

            if rootPart then
                local scream =
                    rootPart:FindFirstChild(
                        "Scream"
                    )

                if scream then
                    scream.Playing = false
                end
            end

            if not IsHeld.Value
                or not runtimeFlags.AntiGrab
            then
                state.States["Anti Grab"] =
                    false

                if antiKill then
                    task.delay(
                        0.1,
                        function()
                            if humanoid.Parent then
                                humanoid.Sit = false
                            end
                        end
                    )
                end

                humanoid.AutoRotate =
                    true

                if antiGrabConnection then
                    antiGrabConnection:Disconnect()

                    antiGrabConnection =
                        nil
                end
            end
        end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if runtimeFlags.AntiGrab then
            startAntiGrabIfNeeded()
        elseif state.States["Anti Grab"] then
            stopAntiGrab()
        end
    end
end)

local gucciWorkerGeneration = 0
local gucciActiveCleanup = nil

local function stopGucciAntiGrab()
    gucciWorkerGeneration += 1

    if gucciActiveCleanup then
        local cleanup =
            gucciActiveCleanup

        gucciActiveCleanup = nil
        pcall(cleanup)
    end
end

local function runGucciAntiGrab()
    gucciWorkerGeneration += 1

    local generation =
        gucciWorkerGeneration

    local vehicle = nil
    local seat = nil
    local nicePart = nil
    local vehicleType = nil
    local descendantConnection = nil
    local respawned = false

    local function cleanup()
        if descendantConnection then
            descendantConnection:Disconnect()

            descendantConnection = nil
        end

        if vehicle then
            H.dt(vehicle)
            vehicle = nil
        end

        seat = nil
        nicePart = nil
        vehicleType = nil
    end

    gucciActiveCleanup = cleanup

    local function waitTypeChange(expected)
        repeat
            task.wait()
        until settings.GucciType ~= expected
            or not runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab
            or generation ~= gucciWorkerGeneration
    end

    local function hideDescendant(descendant)
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
            descendant.Transparency = 1
            descendant.CanCollide = false
        elseif descendant:IsA("Decal")
            or descendant:IsA("Texture")
        then
            descendant.Transparency = 1
        elseif descendant.Name
            == "ItemSpawnSound"
        then
            descendant:Destroy()
        end
    end

    while runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab
        and generation == gucciWorkerGeneration
        and task.wait()
    do
        local character, rootPart, humanoid =
            H.charp()

        if not humanoid
            or humanoid.Health == 0
        then
            LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()

            respawned = true

            task.delay(
                1,
                function()
                    respawned = false
                end
            )

            task.wait(0.16)
            continue
        end

        local ragdolled =
            humanoid:FindFirstChild(
                "Ragdolled"
            )

        if not rootPart
            or not ragdolled
            or IsHeld.Value
            or ragdolled.Value
            or humanoid.Health <= 0
        then
            continue
        end

        if humanoid.Sit then
            humanoid.Sit = false
        end

        local currentType =
            settings.GucciType

        local reusable = false

        if vehicle
            and vehicle.Parent
            and seat
            and seat.Parent
            and vehicleType == currentType
        then
            local occupant =
                seat.Occupant

            if occupant == nil
                or occupant == humanoid
            then
                reusable = true
            end
        end

        if not reusable then
            cleanup()

            if currentType == "Train" then
                local map =
                    Workspace:FindFirstChild(
                        "Map"
                    )

                local always =
                    map
                    and map:FindFirstChild(
                        "AlwaysHereTweenedObjects"
                    )

                local train =
                    always
                    and always:FindFirstChild(
                        "Train"
                    )

                local object =
                    train
                    and train:FindFirstChild(
                        "Object"
                    )

                local objectModel =
                    object
                    and object:FindFirstChild(
                        "ObjectModel"
                    )

                if not objectModel then
                    notify(
                        "Train doesn't exist!"
                    )

                    waitTypeChange(
                        currentType
                    )

                    continue
                end

                vehicle = objectModel

                seat =
                    vehicle:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
                        "Seat"
                    )

                nicePart = nil
                vehicleType = currentType

                if not seat then
                    notify(
                        "Train doesn't have seats!"
                    )

                    waitTypeChange(
                        currentType
                    )

                    cleanup()
                    continue
                end
            else
                vehicle =
                    H.st(
                        currentType,
                        CFrame.new(
                            100000,
                            500000,
                            0
                        )
                    )

                seat =
                    vehicle
                    and vehicle:WaitForChild(
                        "VehicleSeat",
                        1.25
                    )

                nicePart =
                    seat
                    and H.findNicePart(
                        vehicle
                    )
                    or nil

                vehicleType =
                    currentType

                if vehicle then
                    for _, descendant in pairs(
                        vehicle:GetDescendants()
                    ) do
                        hideDescendant(
                            descendant
                        )
                    end

                    descendantConnection =
                        vehicle.DescendantAdded:Connect(
                            hideDescendant
                        )
                end

                if not seat then
                    cleanup()
                    continue
                end
            end
        end

        local currentVehicle = vehicle
        local currentSeat = seat
        local currentNicePart = nicePart

        local active = true

        if currentNicePart then
            local oldBP =
                currentNicePart:FindFirstChild(
                    "GucciBP"
                )

            if oldBP then
                oldBP:Destroy()
            end
        end

        currentSeat.Transparency = 1
        currentSeat.CanCollide = false

        local seatWeldBroken = false
        local savedCFrame = rootPart.CFrame
        local started = tick()

        local seatWeldConnection
        seatWeldConnection = currentSeat.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "SeatWeld" then
                child.Enabled = false
                child:Destroy()
                seatWeldBroken = true
                seatWeldConnection:Disconnect()
            end
        end)

        local ragdollLoopStarted = false

        refreshRemotes()

        task.defer(function()
            while humanoid
                and humanoid.Health > 0
                and active
                and runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab
                and generation == gucciWorkerGeneration
                and rootPart.Parent
                and task.wait()
            do
                if not runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab or generation ~= gucciWorkerGeneration then break end
                ragdollLoopStarted = true

                if remotes.RagdollRemote then
                    fireRemote(
                        remotes.RagdollRemote,
                        rootPart,
                        0
                    )
                end
            end

            ragdollLoopStarted = true
        end)

        repeat
            task.wait()
        until ragdollLoopStarted
            or generation
                ~= gucciWorkerGeneration

        if generation ~= gucciWorkerGeneration or not runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab then
            active = false
            seatWeldConnection:Disconnect()
            break
        end

        currentSeat:Sit(humanoid)

        while true do
            task.wait()

            if humanoid.Health <= 0
                or not rootPart.Parent
                or not currentVehicle.Parent
                or not currentSeat.Parent
            then
                break
            end

            if not runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab
                or generation ~= gucciWorkerGeneration
                or IsHeld.Value
                or ragdolled.Value
                or seatWeldBroken
                or tick() - started > 0.75
            then
                break
            end
        end

        seatWeldConnection:Disconnect()
        if not runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab or generation ~= gucciWorkerGeneration then
            active = false
            break
        end

        if seatWeldBroken then
            humanoid.Sit = false

            task.delay(
                0.15,
                function()
                    active = false
                    task.wait()

                    if runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab
                        and generation == gucciWorkerGeneration
                        and remotes.RagdollRemote
                        and rootPart.Parent
                    then
                        fireRemote(
                            remotes.RagdollRemote,
                            rootPart,
                            0.15
                        )
                    end
                end
            )

            if respawned then
                H.teleport(
                    rootPart,
                    savedCFrame
                )
            end

            if settings.GucciType ~= "Train"
                and currentNicePart
            then
                local plotItems =
                    Workspace:FindFirstChild(
                        "PlotItems"
                    )

                local inPlotItems =
                    plotItems
                    and currentNicePart:IsDescendantOf(
                        plotItems
                    )

                if inPlotItems
                    or H.isnetworkowner(
                        currentNicePart
                    )
                    or H.sno(
                        currentNicePart
                    )
                then
                    local position =
                        Vector3.new(
                            math.random(
                                -100000,
                                100000
                            ),
                            100000,
                            math.random(
                                -100000,
                                100000
                            )
                        )

                    local bodyPosition =
                        Instance.new(
                            "BodyPosition"
                        )

                    bodyPosition.Name =
                        "GucciBP"

                    bodyPosition.D = 200

                    bodyPosition.MaxForce =
                        Vector3.new(
                            math.huge,
                            math.huge,
                            math.huge
                        )

                    bodyPosition.Position =
                        position

                    currentVehicle.Name =
                        "GucciRSC"

                    bodyPosition.Parent =
                        currentNicePart
                end
            end

            task.delay(
                0.75,
                function()
                    if not currentSeat
                        or not currentSeat.Parent
                        or not currentVehicle
                        or not currentVehicle.Parent
                    then
                        return
                    end

                    currentSeat.Parent =
                        ReplicatedStorage

                    task.wait()

                    if humanoid
                        and humanoid.Parent
                    then
                        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(
                                Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping,
                                true
                            )
                    end

                    if currentVehicle
                        and currentVehicle.Parent
                        and currentSeat
                    then
                        currentSeat.Parent =
                            currentVehicle
                    end
                end
            )

            while currentVehicle.Parent
                and currentSeat.Parent
                and humanoid.Parent
                and humanoid.Health > 0
                and not IsHeld.Value
                and not ragdolled.Value
                and (
                    currentSeat.Occupant == nil
                    or currentSeat.Occupant
                        == humanoid
                )
                and runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab
                and settings.GucciType
                    == currentType
                and generation
                    == gucciWorkerGeneration
            do
                task.wait()
            end

            local occupant =
                currentSeat.Occupant

            if occupant
                and occupant ~= humanoid
                and occupant.Parent
            then
                local player =
                    Players:FindFirstChild(
                        occupant.Parent.Name
                    )

                if player then
                    notify(
                        player.DisplayName
                            .. " sat on your gucci!"
                    )
                end
            end

            if humanoid
                and humanoid.Parent
                and humanoid.Health > 0
            then
                local occupiedSeat =
                    humanoid.SeatPart

                if occupiedSeat
                    and occupiedSeat
                        ~= currentSeat
                then
                    notify(
                        "You can't sit on other Vehicles while in Gucci!",
                        "Invincibility"
                    )
                end

                humanoid.Sit = true
                task.wait(0.05)
                humanoid.Sit = false
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end

        if descendantConnection then
            descendantConnection:Disconnect()

            descendantConnection = nil
        end

        if currentVehicle
            and currentVehicle.Parent
        then
            H.dt(currentVehicle)
        end

        vehicle = nil
        seat = nil
        nicePart = nil
        vehicleType = nil
        active = false

        task.wait(0.05)
    end

    cleanup()

    if gucciActiveCleanup == cleanup then
        gucciActiveCleanup = nil
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    local savedRootAttachment = nil
    local dummyRootAttachment = nil
    while task.wait() do
        local _, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp()
        if not rootPart or not humanoid then
            if savedRootAttachment and savedRootAttachment.Parent then
                savedRootAttachment:Destroy()
            end
            savedRootAttachment = nil
        elseif runtimeFlags.AntiBlobman then
            if (not savedRootAttachment or not savedRootAttachment.Parent) and rootPart:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment") then
                task.wait(0.5)
                local attachment = rootPart:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")
                if attachment and remoteRoot then
                    savedRootAttachment = attachment
                    savedRootAttachment.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
                    dummyRootAttachment = Instance.new("BoolValue")
                    dummyRootAttachment.Name = "RootAttachment"
                    dummyRootAttachment.Parent = rootPart
                end
            end
            rootPart.Massless = false
        else
            if savedRootAttachment then
                savedRootAttachment.Parent = rootPart
                savedRootAttachment = nil
            end
            if dummyRootAttachment then
                dummyRootAttachment:Destroy()
                dummyRootAttachment = nil
            end
        end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local antiInputToy = nil
    local endGrabEarly = nil
    local pendingInvokes = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function invokeOnce(remote, toy, ...)
        if pendingInvokes[remote] then return end
        local args = table.pack(...)
        pendingInvokes[remote] = true
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                if runtimeFlags.AntiInputLag and toy == antiInputToy and toy.Parent then
                    remote:InvokeServer(toy, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
                end
            end)
            pendingInvokes[remote] = nil
        end)
    end
    while true do
        task.wait(settings.InputLgDelay)
        if runtimeFlags.AntiInputLag then
            refreshRemotes()
            local grabEvents = remoteRoot and remoteRoot:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
            if grabEvents and not endGrabEarly then
                local existing = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("EndGrabEarly")
                if existing then
                    existing:Destroy()
                end
                endGrabEarly = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
                endGrabEarly.Name = "EndGrabEarly"
                endGrabEarly.Parent = grabEvents
            end

            local character, _, humanoid = H.charp()
            if antiInputToy and not antiInputToy.Parent then
                antiInputToy = nil
            end
            if not antiInputToy then
                antiInputToy = H.st(settings.AntiInputType, CFrame.new(0, 1e20, 0))
            end
            if antiInputToy and antiInputToy.Parent then
                local holdPart = antiInputToy:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 2)
                local holdRemote = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 2)
                local dropRemote = holdRemote and holdPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 2)
                local rigid = holdPart and holdPart:FindFirstChild("RigidConstraint")

                for _, descendant in ipairs(antiInputToy:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                        descendant.CanCollide = false
                        if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant.Name == "Part" then
                            descendant.Transparency = settings.AILTransparency
                        end
                    elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") then
                        descendant.Transparency = settings.AILTransparency
                    end
                end

                if character and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and holdPart and holdRemote and dropRemote then
                    invokeOnce(dropRemote, antiInputToy, CFrame.new(0, 1e20, 0), Vector3.new())
                    invokeOnce(holdRemote, antiInputToy, character)
                    if rigid then
                        local leftArm = character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
                        local rightArm = character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                        local leftGrip = leftArm and leftArm:FindFirstChild("LeftGripAttachment")
                        local rightGrip = rightArm and rightArm:FindFirstChild("RightGripAttachment")
                        local a1 = rigid.Attachment1
                        if a1 and a1 ~= leftGrip and a1 ~= rightGrip then
                            H.dt(antiInputToy)
                            antiInputToy = nil
                        end
                    end
                else
                    H.dt(antiInputToy)
                    antiInputToy = nil
                end
            end
        else
            if antiInputToy then
                H.dt(antiInputToy)
                antiInputToy = nil
            end
            if endGrabEarly then
                endGrabEarly:Destroy()
                endGrabEarly = nil
            end
        end
    end
end)

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child.Name ~= "Part" or not runtimeFlags.AntiExplosion or not child:IsA("BasePart") then
        return
    end
    local character, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp()
    if not character or not rootPart or not humanoid or (child.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude > 20 then
        return
    end
    rootPart.Anchored = true
    task.wait(0.02)
    if not humanoid.SeatPart then
        humanoid.Sit = false
    end
    local ragdollParts = nil

    pcall(function()
        ragdollParts =
            character:QueryDescendants(
                "#RagdollLimbPart"
            )
    end)

    if ragdollParts then
        for _, part in pairs(ragdollParts) do
            part.CanCollide = false
        end
    else
        for _, descendant in ipairs(
            character:GetDescendants()
        ) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart")
                and (
                    descendant.Name
                        == "RagdollLimbPart"
                    or descendant:GetAttribute(
                            "RagdollLimbPart"
                        )
                )
            then
                descendant.CanCollide =
                    false
            end
        end
    end
    H.resetVelocity(rootPart)
    rootPart.Anchored = false
end)

local function bindAntiBurn(character)
    local rootPart = character and character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)
    if not rootPart then
        return
    end
    rootPart.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name ~= "FireParticleEmitter" or not runtimeFlags.AntiBurn then
            return
        end
        local map = getMap()
        local hole = map and map:FindFirstChild("Hole")
        local smallHole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")
        local extinguish = smallHole and smallHole:FindFirstChild("ExtinguishPart")
        if not extinguish or not extinguish:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end
        local original = extinguish.CFrame
        local fireLight = rootPart:FindFirstChild("FireLight")
        if fireLight then
            fireLight:Destroy()
        end
        if child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            child.Enabled = false
        end
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        local overlay = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("ScreenOverlayGui")
        local fireFade = overlay and overlay:FindFirstChild("FireFade")
        if fireFade and fireFade:IsA("GuiObject") then
            fireFade.Visible = false
        end
        while child and child.Parent and rootPart.Parent do
            extinguish.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame
            task.wait()
        end
        if extinguish.Parent then
            extinguish.CFrame = original
        end
    end)
end

do
    local map = getMap()
    local hole = map and map:FindFirstChild("Hole")
    local smallHole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")
    local extinguish = smallHole and smallHole:FindFirstChild("ExtinguishPart")
    if extinguish and extinguish:IsA("BasePart") then
        local tex = extinguish:FindFirstChild("Tex")
        if tex then
            tex:Destroy()
        end
        extinguish.Transparency = 1
    end
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(bindAntiBurn)
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.defer(bindAntiBurn, LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if runtimeFlags.AntiVoid then
            local _, rootPart = H.charp()
            if rootPart and (rootPart.Position.Y >= 10000 or rootPart.Position.Y <= -500) then
                H.teleport(rootPart, CFrame.new(0, 15, 0))
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function syncRecoveredProperties(instance, desired)
    for key, value in next, desired do
        if instance[key] ~= value then
            local current = instance[key]

            if type(current) ~= "number" then
                instance[key] = value
            elseif type(value) ~= "number" then
                instance[key] = value
            elseif math.abs(current - value) > 1e-5 then
                instance[key] = value
            end
        end
    end
end

local antiKickShuriken = nil

local function destroyAntiKickShuriken()
    if antiKickShuriken then
        H.dt(antiKickShuriken)
        antiKickShuriken = nil
    end
end

local function getAntiKickParts()
    if not antiKickShuriken
        or not antiKickShuriken.Parent
    then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local stickyPart =
        antiKickShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")

    if not stickyPart then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local stickyWeld =
        stickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")

    return stickyPart, stickyWeld
end

local function antiKickAttachedTo(hitbox)
    if not antiKickShuriken
        or not antiKickShuriken.Parent
        or antiKickShuriken.Name ~= "ShurikenRSC"
    then
        return false
    end

    local stickyPart, stickyWeld = getAntiKickParts()

    if not stickyPart or not stickyWeld then
        return false
    end

    return stickyWeld.Part1 == hitbox
end

local function applyAntiKickAppearance()
    if not antiKickShuriken
        or not antiKickShuriken.Parent
    then
        return
    end

    local highlight =
        antiKickShuriken:FindFirstChild("ShurHighlight")

    if not highlight then
        highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "ShurHighlight"
        highlight.Adornee = antiKickShuriken
        highlight.Parent = antiKickShuriken
    end

    syncRecoveredProperties(
        highlight,
        {
            Enabled = state.AntiKickSettings.Highlight,
            FillColor = state.AntiKickSettings.HighlightFill,
            OutlineColor = state.AntiKickSettings.HighlightOutl,
            FillTransparency = state.AntiKickSettings.HighlightFT,
            OutlineTransparency = state.AntiKickSettings.HighlightOT,
        }
    )

    local transparency =
        state.AntiKickSettings.Transparency

    for _, child in ipairs(
        antiKickShuriken:GetChildren()
    ) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            if child.Transparency ~= transparency then
                if child:IsA("MeshPart")
                    or child.Name == "Part"
                then
                    syncRecoveredProperties(
                        child,
                        {
                            Transparency = transparency,
                        }
                    )
                end
            end

            child.CanCollide = false
            child.CanQuery = false
        end
    end
end

local function attachAntiKickShuriken(hitbox)
    if not antiKickShuriken
        or not antiKickShuriken.Parent
    then
        if antiKickShuriken then
            H.dt(antiKickShuriken)
        end

        antiKickShuriken = H.st(
            "NinjaShuriken",
            hitbox.CFrame
                + Vector3.new(0, 15, 0)
        )

        if not antiKickShuriken then
            return false
        end
    end

    local stickyPart =
        antiKickShuriken:WaitForChild(
            "StickyPart",
            1.5
        )

    if not stickyPart then
        destroyAntiKickShuriken()
        return false
    end

    local stickyWeld =
        stickyPart:WaitForChild(
            "StickyWeld",
            1.5
        )

    if not stickyWeld then
        destroyAntiKickShuriken()
        return false
    end

    if not H.sno(stickyPart) then
        destroyAntiKickShuriken()
        return false
    end

    antiKickShuriken.Name = "ShurikenRSC"

    task.spawn(function()
        local touchInterest =
            stickyPart:WaitForChild(
                "TouchInterest",
                2.5
            )

        if touchInterest then
            touchInterest:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    refreshRemotes()

    if remotes.spe then
        fireRemote(
            remotes.spe,
            stickyPart,
            hitbox,
            CFrame.new(0, -0.3, 0)
                * CFrame.Angles(
                    math.rad(90),
                    0,
                    0
                )
        )
    end

    local started = tick()

    while true do
        recoveredYield()

        if stickyWeld.Part1 == hitbox then
            break
        end

        if not stickyWeld.Parent
            or not hitbox.Parent
            or not stickyPart.Parent
            or not antiKickShuriken
            or not antiKickShuriken.Parent
        then
            break
        end

        local _, _, humanoid = H.charp()

        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health == 0 then
            break
        end

        if tick() - started > 0.5 then
            break
        end
    end

    return stickyWeld.Parent ~= nil
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if runtimeFlags.AntiKick then
            local _, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp()

            if rootPart and humanoid then
                local hitbox =
                    rootPart:FindFirstChild(
                        "RagdollTouchedHitbox"
                    )

                if hitbox and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    local inPlot =
                        LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

                    local inOwnedPlot =
                        LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")

                    if inPlot and inPlot.Value then
                        while inPlot.Value
                            and (
                                not inOwnedPlot
                                or not inOwnedPlot.Value
                            )
                        do
                            task.wait()
                        end
                    end

                    if state.AKType == "Shuriken" then
                        local ready =
                            antiKickAttachedTo(hitbox)

                        if not ready then
                            ready =
                                attachAntiKickShuriken(
                                    hitbox
                                )
                        end

                        if ready
                            and antiKickShuriken
                            and antiKickShuriken.Parent
                        then
                            applyAntiKickAppearance()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            destroyAntiKickShuriken()
        end
    end
end)

local function runPCLDKillLoop(humanoid, finished)
    while true do
        humanoid.Health = 0

        pcall(function()
            humanoid:ChangeState(
                Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
            )
        end)

        recoveredYield()

        if finished() then
            break
        end
    end
end

local function runPCLDBreakSequence(humanoid)
    local firstCharacter = nil

    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        notify(
            "Detected death, breaking PCLD again...",
            "PCLD Breaker"
        )
    else
        notify(
            "Starting PCLD Break...",
            "PCLD Breaker"
        )

        task.spawn(
            runPCLDKillLoop,
            humanoid,
            function()
                return firstCharacter ~= nil
            end
        )
    end

    firstCharacter = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()

    local firstHumanoid =
        firstCharacter:WaitForChild(
            "Humanoid",
            5
        )

    if not firstHumanoid then
        return
    end

    local secondCharacter = nil

    task.spawn(
        runPCLDKillLoop,
        firstHumanoid,
        function()
            return secondCharacter ~= nil
        end
    )

    secondCharacter = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()

    local secondHumanoid =
        secondCharacter:WaitForChild(
            "Humanoid",
            5
        )

    if not secondHumanoid then
        return
    end

    secondHumanoid:SetAttribute(
        "BrokenPCLD",
        true
    )

    notify("Done!", "PCLD Breaker")
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.2) do
        if runtimeFlags.AntiKick2 then
            local _, _, humanoid = H.charp()

            if humanoid then
                local broken =
                    humanoid:GetAttribute(
                        "BrokenPCLD"
                    )

                if not broken
                    or humanoid.Health <= 0
                then
                    runPCLDBreakSequence(humanoid)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local antiStickyGeneration = 0
local function startAntiStickyLoop()
    antiStickyGeneration += 1
    local generation = antiStickyGeneration
    task.spawn(function()
        while runtimeFlags.AntiSticky and generation == antiStickyGeneration and task.wait(0.1) do
            local character = H.charp()
            local seenSticky, seenGrab = {}, {}
            if character then
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        for _, connected in ipairs(part:GetConnectedParts()) do
                            if connected.Name == "StickyPart" and not seenSticky[connected]
                                and not isLocallySpawnedToy(connected) then
                                seenSticky[connected] = true
                                local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                                if not plotItems or not connected:IsDescendantOf(plotItems) or getInOwnedPlotValue() then
                                    for _, child in ipairs(connected:GetChildren()) do
                                        if child:IsA("TouchInterest") then
                                            child:Destroy()
                                        end
                                    end
                                    local nice = H.findNicePart(connected.Parent)
                                    if nice and not seenGrab[nice] then
                                        seenGrab[nice] = true
                                        H.grab(nice)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.5)

    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if runtimeFlags.AntiSnowball then
            if runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab then
                repeat
                    task.wait()
                until not runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab

                task.wait(1)
            end

            if runtimeFlags.AntiBanana then
                local character = H.charp()
                local rightLeg =
                    character
                    and character:FindFirstChild(
                        "Right Leg"
                    )

                while rightLeg do
                    task.wait()
                    character = H.charp()

                    if not character then
                        rightLeg = nil
                    else
                        rightLeg =
                            character:FindFirstChild(
                                "Right Leg"
                            )
                    end
                end
            end

            local character, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp()

            if rootPart
                and humanoid
                and humanoid.Health ~= 0
            then
                local weldHRP =
                    rootPart:FindFirstChild(
                        "WeldHRP"
                    )

                if weldHRP then
                    refreshRemotes()

                    if remotes.RagdollRemote then
                        fireRemote(
                            remotes.RagdollRemote,
                            rootPart,
                            0.5
                        )
                    end

                    if not humanoid.SeatPart then
                        humanoid.Sit = false
                    end

                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true

                    if weldHRP.Enabled then
                        ContextActionService:UnbindAction(
                            "JumpRemover"
                        )

                        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(
                            Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping,
                            true
                        )

                        H.RepositionLimbs(character)

                        H.ToggleCollisions(
                            character,
                            false,
                            false,
                            true
                        )
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2)

    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if runtimeFlags.AntiBanana then
            local character, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp()
            local rightLeg =
                character
                and character:FindFirstChild(
                    "Right Leg"
                )

            if character
                and rightLeg
                and rootPart
                and humanoid
            then
                if humanoid.Health == 0 then
                    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    task.wait(0.5)
                elseif H.isnetworkowner(rootPart) then
                    local gucciWasActive =
                        runtimeFlags.GucciAntiGrab

                    if gucciWasActive then
                        local gucciToggle =
                            Toggles.GucciAntiGrab

                        if gucciToggle then
                            gucciToggle:SetValue(false)
                        end

                        task.wait(0.25)
                    end

                    humanoid.Sit = false
                    humanoid.HipHeight = 2

                    H.breaklimb(
                        LocalPlayer,
                        "Legs"
                    )

                    local bananaConnection = nil

                    bananaConnection =
                        humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
                            "HipHeight"
                        ):Connect(function()
                            if runtimeFlags.AntiBanana then
                                humanoid.HipHeight = 2
                            elseif bananaConnection then
                                bananaConnection:Disconnect()
                            end
                        end)

                    local started = tick()

                    while true do
                        task.wait()

                        if not rightLeg.Parent then
                            break
                        end

                        if humanoid.Health == 0 then
                            break
                        end

                        if tick() - started > 0.75 then
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if gucciWasActive then
                        local gucciToggle =
                            Toggles.GucciAntiGrab

                        if gucciToggle then
                            gucciToggle:SetValue(true)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local paintConnections = {}
local paintDescendantConnection = nil
state.PaintGeneration = 0
local function applyPaintPart(part, enabled)
    if not part:IsA("BasePart") or part.Name ~= "PaintPlayerPart" then
        return
    end
    part.CanTouch = not enabled
    if enabled and not paintConnections[part] then
        paintConnections[part] = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanTouch"):Connect(function()
            if runtimeFlags.AntiPaint and part.Parent then
                part.CanTouch = false
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function applyAntiPaint(enabled)
    for part, connection in pairs(paintConnections) do
        connection:Disconnect()
        paintConnections[part] = nil
        if part.Parent then
            part.CanTouch = true
        end
    end
    if paintDescendantConnection then
        paintDescendantConnection:Disconnect()
        paintDescendantConnection = nil
    end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        applyPaintPart(descendant, enabled)
    end
    if enabled then
        paintDescendantConnection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            applyPaintPart(descendant, true)
        end)
    end
end

local function applyAntiPoison(enabled)
    local map = getMap()
    local hole = map and map:FindFirstChild("Hole")
    if not hole then
        return
    end
    local poisonHoles = {hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonBigHole"), hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")}
    for _, poisonHole in ipairs(poisonHoles) do
        if poisonHole then
            local poisonHurtPart = poisonHole:FindFirstChild("PoisonHurtPart")
            local paintPlayerPart = poisonHole:FindFirstChild("PaintPlayerPart")
            if poisonHurtPart and poisonHurtPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                poisonHurtPart.CanTouch = not enabled
            end
            if paintPlayerPart and paintPlayerPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                paintPlayerPart.CanTouch = not enabled
            end
        end
    end
end

local function applyAntiLag(enabled)
    runtimeFlags.AntiLag = enabled
    local playerScripts = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
    local characterAndBeamMove = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("CharacterAndBeamMove")
    local stickyPartsTouchDetection = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("StickyPartsTouchDetection")
    if characterAndBeamMove then
        characterAndBeamMove.Enabled = not enabled
    end
    if stickyPartsTouchDetection then
        stickyPartsTouchDetection.Enabled = not enabled
    end
    if enabled then
        task.delay(1.5, function()
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local character = player.Character
                if character then
                    for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
                            child:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

local lastAutoLagCheck = 0
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not runtimeFlags.AutoAntiLag or os.clock() - lastAutoLagCheck < 2.5 or dt <= 0 then
        return
    end
    lastAutoLagCheck = os.clock()
    local fps = math.floor(1 / dt)
    if fps < settings.AntiLagSens and Toggles.AntiLag and not Toggles.AntiLag.Value then
        notify("Anti Lag Enabled || Too low fps", "Auto Anti Lag")
        Toggles.AntiLag:SetValue(true)
    end
end)

local antiInvisGeneration = 0
local function startAntiInvisLoop()
    antiInvisGeneration += 1
    local generation = antiInvisGeneration
    task.spawn(function()
        while runtimeFlags.AntiInvis and generation == antiInvisGeneration and task.wait(0.1) do
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local _, rootPart, humanoid = H.charp(player)
                    if rootPart then
                        rootPart.Massless = false
                    end
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Sit and humanoid.SeatPart then
                        local seatWeld = humanoid.SeatPart:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")
                        if seatWeld and not seatWeld.Enabled then
                            seatWeld.Enabled = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local autoResetConnection = nil
local function onPossibleKickFlag(firstArg)
    if firstArg ~= "Flying" then
        return
    end
    local timestamp = tick()
    if timestamp - state.LastKickFlagTime <= 1 then
        state.KickFlags += 1
    else
        state.LastKickFlagTime = timestamp
        state.KickFlags = 1
    end
    if not runtimeFlags.AutoReset then
        return
    end
    if state.KickFlags < 4 then
        local _, _, humanoid = H.charp()
        if humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
            end)
            if state.KickFlags == 1 then
                notify("Reset due to kick flag", "Auto Reset")
            end
        end
    else
        LocalPlayer:Kick("Resonance (Auto Reset)\nAvoided Kick")
    end
end

local function bindAutoResetRemote()
    refreshRemotes()
    local correctionEvents = remoteRoot and remoteRoot:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionEvents")
    local correctionNotify = correctionEvents and correctionEvents:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionsNotify")
    if not correctionNotify or not correctionNotify:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        return false
    end
    if autoResetConnection then
        autoResetConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    autoResetConnection = correctionNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(onPossibleKickFlag)
    return true
end

if not bindAutoResetRemote() then
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            if bindAutoResetRemote() then
                break
            end
        end
    end)
end

local loopTpGeneration = 0
local function startLoopTP()
    loopTpGeneration += 1
    local generation = loopTpGeneration
    task.spawn(function()
        local character, rootPart = H.charp()
        local original = rootPart and rootPart.CFrame or CFrame.new(0, -5, 0)
        while runtimeFlags.LoopTP and generation == loopTpGeneration and task.wait(0.04) do
            character = H.charp()
            if character then
                character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), -7.35, math.random(-1000, 1000)))
            end
        end
        character = H.charp()
        if character then
            character:PivotTo(original)
        end
    end)
end

local function applyDisableVoid(enabled)

    runtimeFlags.DestroyVoid = enabled
    if enabled then
        Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0 / 0
    else
        Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
    end
end

local function applyWaterWalk(enabled)
    runtimeFlags.WaterWalk = enabled
    local map = getMap()
    local always = map and map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
    local ocean = always and always:FindFirstChild("Ocean")
    local object = ocean and ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")
    local objectModel = object and object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
    if not objectModel then
        return
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(objectModel:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            if enabled then
                child.CanCollide = true
                if child.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    child.Transparency = 0.5
                end
            else
                child.CanCollide = false
                if child.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    child.Transparency = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function applyDeleteBarriers(enabled)
    runtimeFlags.DeleteBarriers = enabled
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        return
    end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local barrier = plot:FindFirstChild("Barrier")
        if barrier then
            for _, part in ipairs(barrier:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == "PlotBarrier" then
                    part.CanCollide = not enabled
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local otherInvGeneration = 0
local function startOtherInvCollisions()
    otherInvGeneration += 1
    local generation = otherInvGeneration
    task.spawn(function()
        while runtimeFlags.OtherInvCollisions and generation == otherInvGeneration and task.wait(0.1) do
            local character = H.charp()
            if character then
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            PhysicsService:SetPartCollisionGroup(part, "PlotPlayers")
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local character = H.charp()
        if character then
            local collisionGroup = getInOwnedPlotValue() and "PlotPlayers" or "Players"
            for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function()
                        PhysicsService:SetPartCollisionGroup(part, collisionGroup)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Connect the requested Grab Kick to Resonance's existing complete network-kick
-- implementation, including its ragdoll and sticky-removal subroutines.
do
    local bridge = LeviusShared.ResonanceTarget
    if bridge then
        settings.NKOffset = 15
        settings.NKDelayMode = "Faster but worse"
        runtimeFlags.RagdollInNK = true
        runtimeFlags.DeleteShurInNK = true
        bridge.KickPass = function(player, index, count)
            return performNetworkKickPass(player, index, count, true)
        end
        bridge.Cleanup = function(player)
            if player and player.Parent then
                pcall(cleanupNetworkKickPlayer, player)
            end
        end
    end
end

applyDisableVoid(true)
end

-- End imported Resonance Invincibility
