function getToysFolder()
    return workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
end

function SpawnShuriken(name)
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    local CanSpawnToy = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
    local InPlot = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

    while true do
        task.wait()
        if CanSpawnToy and CanSpawnToy.Value and InPlot and not InPlot.Value then break end
    end

    SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 20))

    local toysFolder = getToysFolder()
    if not toysFolder then return end

    local shuriken = toysFolder:WaitForChild("NinjaShuriken", 1)
    if not shuriken then return end

    local soundPart = shuriken:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 1)
    if not soundPart then DestroyT(shuriken); return end

    local timeout = tick()
    while true do
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
        local ok = (not soundPart) or (not hrp) or ((hrp.Position - soundPart.Position).Magnitude > 20)
        if not ok then
            ok = soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                 and soundPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name
        end
        if ok or tick() - timeout > 1 then break end
    end

    if soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
       and soundPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
        if not soundPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
            local bp    = Instance.new("BodyPosition", soundPart)
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            bp.D        = 100
            bp.Position = Vector3.new(math.random(-100000, 100000), 100000, math.random(-100000, 100000))
        end
        shuriken.Name = name
        return shuriken
    end

    DestroyT(shuriken)
end

local States = LeviusShared.States
local ServerState = States.Server
local AntiState = States.Anti
local KickNotifyState = States.KickNotify

local MapPath = Workspace:WaitForChild("Map", 5)
local AlwaysTweened = MapPath and MapPath:WaitForChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects", 5)

local AllWhitelisted = States.General and States.General.AllWhitelisted or {}

function GetPlot()
    local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
    if not plotItems or not plotItems:FindFirstChild("PlayersInPlots") or not plotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name) then
        return false
    end
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return false end

    for _, v in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local sign = v:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        local thisPlotsOwners = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
        if thisPlotsOwners then
            for _, owner in ipairs(thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
                if owner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
                    local plotItem = plotItems:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
                    if plotItem then
                        return true, plotItem
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function NewToy(name, cf)
    if not name or not cf then return nil end
    local canSpawn = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
    while not (canSpawn and canSpawn.Value) do
        task.wait()
    end

    local start = tick()
    local toy = nil

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.spawn(function()
                if SpawnToyRemoteFunction then
                    SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(name, cf, Vector3.zero)
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if toy or (tick() - start > 1.5) then break end
        end
    end)

    local inPlot, plotFolder = GetPlot()
    if inPlot and plotFolder then
        toy = plotFolder:WaitForChild(name, 1.5)
        return toy
    end
    local tf = getToysFolder()
    if tf then
        toy = tf:WaitForChild(name, 1.5)
        return toy
    end
    return nil
end

function UnGrab(part)
    if DestroyGrabLine and part then
        DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(part)
    end
end

function GetCharPart(character, partName)
    if character and partName then
        return character:WaitForChild(partName, 1)
    end
    return nil
end

function CountSize(arg)
    local size = 0
    if type(arg) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(arg) do
            if type(v) == "string" then
                size = size + string.len(v)
            elseif type(v) == "table" then
                size = size + CountSize(v)
            end
        end
    elseif type(arg) == "string" then
        size = string.len(arg)
    end
    return size
end

local INFO = Tabs.Server:AddLeftGroupbox("Server Info", "badge-info")
local PC = INFO:AddLabel("Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/25")
local KC = INFO:AddLabel("Total Kicks: " .. (KickNotifyState and KickNotifyState.KickCount or 0))

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
    pcall(function() PC:SetText("Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/25") end)
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function() PC:SetText("Players: " .. #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/25") end)
    end)
end)

INFO:AddButton("FindCheaters", {
    Text = "Find Cheaters",
    Func = function()
        local found = false
        for _, v in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if v ~= LocalPlayer and Workspace:FindFirstChild(v.Name .. "SpawnedInToys") then
                local toys = Workspace:FindFirstChild(v.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                for _, toy in ipairs(toys:GetChildren()) do
                    local stickyWeld = toy:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld", true)
                    if stickyWeld and stickyWeld.Part1 and stickyWeld.Part1.Name == "FirePlayerPart" then
                        found = true
                        Library:Notify({
                            Title = "LEVIUS",
                            Description = "Cheater Found: " .. v.DisplayName .. " [" .. v.Name .. "]",
                            Time = 3,
                            BigIcon = "triangle-alert",
                        })
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not found then
            Library:Notify({
                Title = "LEVIUS",
                Description = "No cheaters detected",
                Time = 2,
                BigIcon = "info",
            })
        end
    end,
})

INFO:AddButton("RejoinServer", {
    Text = "Rejoin",
    Func = function()
        if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
            LocalPlayer:Kick()
            task.wait(0.25)
            TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
        else
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
        end
    end,
})

local SL = Tabs.Server:AddLeftGroupbox("Slots", "coins")
local AutoSlots = false
local SlotsTimeLeft = SL:AddLabel("Time Left: 15:00")

task.spawn(function()
    local slotsFolder = Workspace:WaitForChild("Slots", 5)
    local slot = slotsFolder and slotsFolder:FindFirstChild("Slots")
    local screen = slot and slot:FindFirstChild("Screen")
    local slotGui = screen and screen:FindFirstChild("SlotGui")
    local timeLeftFrame = slotGui and slotGui:FindFirstChild("TimeLeftFrame")
    local slotsTimeText = timeLeftFrame and timeLeftFrame:FindFirstChild("TimeText")
    if slotsTimeText then
        SlotsTimeLeft:SetText("Time Left: " .. slotsTimeText.Text)
        slotsTimeText:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            SlotsTimeLeft:SetText("Time Left: " .. slotsTimeText.Text)
        end)
    end
end)

SL:AddToggle("AutoSlots", {
    Text = "Auto Spin Slots",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        AutoSlots = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while AutoSlots and task.wait() do
                    local slotsFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Slots")
                    local slot = slotsFolder and slotsFolder:FindFirstChild("Slots")
                    local screen = slot and slot:FindFirstChild("Screen")
                    local slotGui = screen and screen:FindFirstChild("SlotGui")
                    local timeLeftFrame = slotGui and slotGui:FindFirstChild("TimeLeftFrame")
                    local slotsTimeText = timeLeftFrame and timeLeftFrame:FindFirstChild("TimeText")

                    if slotsTimeText and slotsTimeText.Text == "0:00" and AutoSlots and slotsFolder then
                        for _, s in ipairs(slotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                            local _, hrp, hum = GetHRH(char)
                            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                                local savedCF = hrp.CFrame
                                for i = 1, 10 do
                                    if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and slotsTimeText.Text == "0:00" and AutoSlots and s:FindFirstChild("Spin2") and s:FindFirstChild("SlotHandle") and s.SlotHandle:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
                                        Teleport(hrp, s.Spin2.CFrame, CFrame.new(0, -6, -7))
                                        Ownerboat(s.SlotHandle.Handle)
                                        task.wait(0.05)
                                    end
                                end
                                if hrp then
                                    Teleport(hrp, savedCF, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local BR = Tabs.Server:AddLeftGroupbox("Plots", "brick-wall-shield")
local BarrierStatus = BR:AddLabel("Barrier Status: Working 🔴")
local BreakBarrierEnabled = false

BR:AddToggle("BreakBarrierToggle", {
    Text = "Break Barrier",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        BreakBarrierEnabled = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while BreakBarrierEnabled and task.wait() do
                    if char then
                        local _, hrp, hum = GetHRH(char)
                        if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            local burger = NewToy("FoodHamburger", CFrame.new(0, 1000, 0))
                            if burger then
                                local holdPart = burger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 1)
                                if holdPart then
                                    local holdItemRF = holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 1)
                                    local rigid = holdPart:WaitForChild("RigidConstraint", 1)

                                    if holdItemRF and rigid and not rigid.Attachment1 and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                                        local savedCF = hrp.CFrame
                                        Teleport(hrp, CFrame.new(400, -8, 535), CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                                        task.wait(0.15)
                                        local now = tick()
                                        while true do
                                            task.wait()
                                            pcall(function() holdItemRF:InvokeServer(burger, char) end)
                                            if not (BreakBarrierEnabled and hum and hum.Health > 0 and rigid and rigid.Parent and not rigid.Attachment1 and (tick() - now <= 1.5)) then
                                                break
                                            end
                                        end

                                        if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and rigid and rigid.Parent and rigid.Attachment1 and rigid.Attachment1.Parent and rigid.Attachment1.Parent.Parent and rigid.Attachment1.Parent.Parent.Name == LocalPlayer.Name then
                                            DestroyT(burger)
                                            task.wait(0.2)
                                            if hrp then Teleport(hrp, savedCF, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)) end

                                            local tf = getToysFolder()
                                            if tf then
                                                for _, t in ipairs(tf:GetChildren()) do
                                                    if t.Name == "PlantPottedTree" then
                                                        DestroyT(t)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end

                                            if NewToy("PlantPottedTree", CFrame.new(400, -8, 535)) then
                                                local now3 = tick()
                                                while true do
                                                    task.wait()
                                                    local tf2 = getToysFolder()
                                                    if not (BreakBarrierEnabled and tf2 and tf2:FindFirstChild("PlantPottedTree") and (tick() - now3 <= 1.5)) then
                                                        break
                                                    end
                                                end

                                                local tf3 = getToysFolder()
                                                if tf3 and tf3:FindFirstChild("PlantPottedTree") then
                                                    DestroyT(tf3.PlantPottedTree)
                                                    BarrierStatus:SetText("Barrier Status: Broken 🟢")
                                                    local bbToggle = (Toggles and Toggles.BreakBarrierToggle) or (Options and Options.BreakBarrierToggle)
                                                    if bbToggle then
                                                        bbToggle:SetValue(false)
                                                        if bbToggle.SetDisabled then bbToggle:SetDisabled(true) end
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        else
                                            DestroyT(burger)
                                        end
                                    else
                                        DestroyT(burger)
                                    end
                                else
                                    DestroyT(burger)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

BR:AddDivider()
local PlotsToBreakTable = {}

function SpawnKunaiToBreakPlotArea(arg)
    if AntiState and AntiState.AntiKick and not AntiState.AntiKickSticked then return end
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local canSpawn = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
    local inPlot = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

    if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and canSpawn and canSpawn.Value and inPlot and not inPlot.Value then
        SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 20))
        local tf = getToysFolder()
        local ninjaShuriken = tf and tf:WaitForChild("NinjaShuriken", 1)

        if ninjaShuriken then
            ninjaShuriken.Name = "Plot[" .. arg .. "]Breaker"
            local stickyPart = ninjaShuriken:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 1)
            local soundPart = ninjaShuriken:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 1)

            if stickyPart and soundPart then
                while true do
                    task.wait()
                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
                    local ok = soundPart and soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and soundPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name
                    if ok or not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then break end
                end

                local ok = soundPart and soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and soundPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name
                if ok and Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. arg) and Workspace.Plots["Plot" .. arg]:FindFirstChild("PlotArea") then
                    StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, Workspace.Plots["Plot" .. arg].PlotArea, CFrame.new(2.3e9, 2.3e9, 2.3e9))
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

BR:AddDropdown("PlotsToBreak", {
    Text = "Select Plots",
    Values = { "Plot1", "Plot2", "Plot3", "Plot4", "Plot5" },
    Default = nil,
    Multi = true,
    Callback = function(arg)
        table.clear(PlotsToBreakTable)
        for k, v in pairs(arg) do
            if v then table.insert(PlotsToBreakTable, k) end
        end
    end,
})

local BreakPlotAreaBoolean = false

BR:AddToggle("BreakPlotAreaToggle", {
    Text = "Break Area",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        BreakPlotAreaBoolean = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while BreakPlotAreaBoolean and task.wait() do
                    local tf = getToysFolder()
                    if tf then
                        for _, plotName in ipairs(PlotsToBreakTable) do
                            local plotNum = plotName:match("%d+")
                            if plotNum and not tf:FindFirstChild("Plot[" .. plotNum .. "]Breaker") then
                                SpawnKunaiToBreakPlotArea(plotNum)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local SRVINTTB = Tabs.Server:AddLeftTabbox("Interaction")
local SRVFNB = SRVINTTB:AddTab("Non Blobman", "bot-off")
local SRVFB = SRVINTTB:AddTab("Blobman", "bot")

local LoopAllNoBlobmanMethod = "Bring"
local LoopAllNoBlobmanEnabled = false
local PositionToBring = nil

SRVFNB:AddDropdown("LoopAllNoBlobmanMethods", {
    Text = "Methods",
    Values = { "Bring", "Void" },
    Default = "Bring",
    Multi = false,
    Callback = function(arg) LoopAllNoBlobmanMethod = arg end,
})

SRVFNB:AddToggle("LoopAllNoBlobmanToggle", {
    Text = "Loop All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        LoopAllNoBlobmanEnabled = arg
        if arg then
            if char then
                local hrp = GetCharPart(char, "HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then PositionToBring = hrp.CFrame end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                local own
                while LoopAllNoBlobmanEnabled and task.wait() do
                    if char then
                        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            for _, targetPlr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if targetPlr ~= LocalPlayer and not table.find(AllWhitelisted, targetPlr) and targetPlr.Character and targetPlr:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and not targetPlr.InPlot.Value and LoopAllNoBlobmanEnabled then
                                    local head = targetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                    local hum2 = targetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                    if head and hum2 and hum2.Health > 0 then
                                        if not (LoopAllNoBlobmanMethod == "Void" and head.CFrame.Position.Y <= -100) then
                                            while true do
                                                task.wait()
                                                Teleport(hrp, head.CFrame, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 1.5))
                                                own = Ownerboat(head)
                                                if not LoopAllNoBlobmanEnabled or (targetPlr:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and targetPlr.InPlot.Value) or not head or not hum2 or hum2.Health <= 0 or not hrp or own then
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            end

                                            if own then
                                                if LoopAllNoBlobmanMethod == "Bring" and PositionToBring then
                                                    Teleport(head, PositionToBring, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                                                elseif LoopAllNoBlobmanMethod == "Void" then
                                                    head.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -100000000, 0)
                                                end
                                                UnGrab(head)
                                            end

                                            if hrp and PositionToBring then
                                                Teleport(hrp, PositionToBring, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local LoopAllBlobmanMethod = "Bring"
local LoopAllBlobmanEnabled = false

SRVFB:AddDropdown("LoopAllBlobmanMethods", {
    Text = "Methods",
    Values = { "Bring", "Kill" },
    Default = "Bring",
    Multi = false,
    Callback = function(arg) LoopAllBlobmanMethod = arg end,
})

SRVFB:AddToggle("LoopAllBlobmanToggle", {
    Text = "Loop All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        LoopAllBlobmanEnabled = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while LoopAllBlobmanEnabled and task.wait() do
                    for _, targetPlr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if targetPlr ~= LocalPlayer and not table.find(AllWhitelisted, targetPlr) and targetPlr.Character then
                            local _, hrp, hum = GetHRH(char)
                            local _, targetHRP, targetHum = GetHRH(targetPlr.Character)

                            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and targetHRP and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 and not targetHum.Sit and LoopAllBlobmanEnabled then
                                if hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent then
                                    local blob = hum.SeatPart.Parent
                                    local blobScript = blob:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                                    local blobHRP = blob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    local leftDetector = blob:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")

                                    if blobScript and blobHRP and leftDetector then
                                        local creatureGrab = blobScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
                                        local creatureRelease = blobScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease")
                                        local leftWeld = leftDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")

                                        if creatureGrab and creatureRelease and leftWeld then
                                            local now = tick()
                                            local savedCF = hrp.CFrame

                                            while true do
                                                task.wait(0.01)
                                                Teleport(hrp, targetHRP.CFrame, CFrame.new(0, 7, 3))
                                                creatureGrab:FireServer(leftDetector, targetHRP, leftWeld)
                                                if not LoopAllBlobmanEnabled or not hum or not hum.SeatPart or not hum.SeatPart.Parent or not targetHRP or not targetHum or targetHum.Health <= 0 or (leftWeld.Attachment0 and leftWeld.Attachment0.Parent == targetHRP) or (tick() - now > 1) then
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            end

                                            if hum and hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent and targetHRP and leftWeld and leftWeld.Attachment0 and leftWeld.Attachment0.Parent == targetHRP then
                                                if LoopAllBlobmanMethod == "Bring" then
                                                    creatureRelease:FireServer(leftWeld)
                                                    local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", targetHRP)
                                                    bp.Position = savedCF.Position
                                                    bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                                    bp.D = 100
                                                    task.spawn(function()
                                                        task.wait(0.25)
                                                        if bp then bp:Destroy() end
                                                    end)
                                                elseif LoopAllBlobmanMethod == "Kill" then
                                                    creatureRelease:FireServer(leftWeld)
                                                    targetHum:ChangeState("Dead")
                                                end
                                            end

                                            if hrp then Teleport(hrp, savedCF, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)) end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

SRVFB:AddButton("KickAll", {
    Text = "Kick All",
    Tooltip = "With Blobman",
    Func = function()
        if char then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.SeatPart then
                local blob = hum.SeatPart.Parent
                if blob and blob.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                    local blobHRP = blob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local blobScript = blob:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                    if blobScript and blobHRP then
                        local KABlobGrab = blobScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
                        local KABlobRelease = blobScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease")
                        local KABlobLDetector = blob:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                        local KABlobLWeld = KABlobLDetector and KABlobLDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")

                        if KABlobGrab and KABlobRelease and KABlobLDetector and KABlobLWeld then
                            for _, v in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if v ~= LocalPlayer and not table.find(AllWhitelisted, v) and v.Character then
                                    local targetHRP = v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    local targetHum = v.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if targetHRP and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent == blob then
                                        for i = 1, 30 do
                                            hrp.CFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 3)
                                            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                            hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                            KABlobGrab:FireServer(KABlobLDetector, targetHRP, KABlobLWeld)
                                            KABlobRelease:FireServer(KABlobLWeld)
                                            task.wait()
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent == blob then
                                blobHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 250, 0)
                                blobHRP.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                blobHRP.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", blobHRP)
                                bp.Position = Vector3.new(0, 250, 0)
                                bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                bp.D = 100
                                Instance.new("BodyGyro", blobHRP).MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)

                                local validTargets = {}
                                for _, v in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                    if v ~= LocalPlayer and not table.find(AllWhitelisted, v) and v.Character then
                                        table.insert(validTargets, v)
                                    end
                                end

                                if #validTargets > 0 then
                                    for idx, v in ipairs(validTargets) do
                                        local targetHRP = v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        local targetHum = v.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                        if targetHRP and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                                            local angle = idx * 6.2831853071795862 / #validTargets
                                            local posX = math.cos(angle) * 20
                                            local posZ = math.sin(angle) * 20
                                            local tbp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", targetHRP)
                                            tbp.Position = Vector3.new(posX, 250, posZ)
                                            tbp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                            tbp.D = 100
                                            Instance.new("BodyGyro", targetHRP).MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    task.wait(0.25)

                                    for _, v in ipairs(validTargets) do
                                        local targetHRP = v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        local targetHum = v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                        if targetHRP and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent == blob then
                                            task.spawn(function()
                                                for i = 1, 5 do
                                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(targetHRP, targetHRP.CFrame)
                                                    if DestroyGrabLine then DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(targetHRP) end
                                                    task.wait()
                                                    KABlobGrab:FireServer(KABlobLDetector, targetHRP, KABlobLWeld)
                                                end
                                            end)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        task.wait(3)
                                        for _, v in ipairs(validTargets) do
                                            if v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                                local th = v.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                                                if th:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then th.BodyPosition:Destroy() end
                                                if th:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then th.BodyGyro:Destroy() end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end)

                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    local tf = getToysFolder()
                                    if blob and tf and blob.Parent == tf then DestroyT(blob) end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                else
                    Library:Notify({ Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Sit On Blob", Time = 3, BigIcon = "triangle-alert" })
                end
            else
                Library:Notify({ Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Sit On Blob", Time = 3, BigIcon = "triangle-alert" })
            end
        end
    end,
})

local SRVF = Tabs.Server:AddLeftGroupbox("Interaction", "bug-play")
local StealAllHoldablesMode = "Break"
local StealAllHoldablesEnabled = false

SRVF:AddDropdown("StealAllModeDropdown", {
    Text = "Steal Mode",
    Values = { "Hold", "Use", "Break", "Delete" },
    Default = "Break",
    Multi = false,
    Callback = function(arg) StealAllHoldablesMode = arg end,
})

function ProcessStealHoldable(item)
    if not item or item.Name == "Broken" or not item:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") then return end
    local holdPart = item.HoldPart
    local holdItemRF = holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 1)
    local dropItemRF = holdPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 1)
    local rigid = holdPart:WaitForChild("RigidConstraint", 1)

    if holdItemRF and dropItemRF and rigid then
        task.spawn(function()
            local now = tick()
            while true do
                task.wait()
                pcall(function() holdItemRF:InvokeServer(item, char) end)
                if not StealAllHoldablesEnabled or not rigid or not rigid.Parent or rigid.Attachment1 or (tick() - now > 1) then
                    break
                end
            end

            if rigid and rigid.Attachment1 and rigid.Attachment1.Parent and rigid.Attachment1.Parent.Parent and rigid.Attachment1.Parent.Parent.Name == LocalPlayer.Name then
                if StealAllHoldablesMode == "Hold" then
                    item.Name = "Broken"
                elseif StealAllHoldablesMode == "Use" then
                    item.Name = "Broken"
                    pcall(function()
                        if HoldEvents and HoldEvents:FindFirstChild("Use") then
                            HoldEvents.Use:FireServer(item)
                        end
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    pcall(function() dropItemRF:InvokeServer(item) end)
                elseif StealAllHoldablesMode == "Break" then
                    item.Name = "Broken"
                    pcall(function() dropItemRF:InvokeServer(item) end)
                elseif StealAllHoldablesMode == "Delete" then
                    pcall(function() dropItemRF:InvokeServer(item, CFrame.new(0, -500, 0), Vector3.zero) end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

SRVF:AddToggle("StealAllHoldablesToggle", {
    Text = "Steal All Holdables",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        StealAllHoldablesEnabled = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while StealAllHoldablesEnabled and task.wait(0.1) do
                    if char then
                        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                                local tf = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                                if tf then
                                    for _, toy in ipairs(tf:GetChildren()) do
                                        ProcessStealHoldable(toy)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait(0.05)
                        local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                        if plotItems then
                            for _, plotFolder in ipairs(plotItems:GetChildren()) do
                                for _, toy in ipairs(plotFolder:GetChildren()) do
                                    ProcessStealHoldable(toy)
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait(0.05)
                        local foodFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Food")
                        if foodFolder then
                            for _, food in ipairs(foodFolder:GetChildren()) do
                                ProcessStealHoldable(food)
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait(0.05)
                        local notNew = Workspace:FindFirstChild("NOT New")
                        if notNew then
                            for _, item in ipairs(notNew:GetChildren()) do
                                ProcessStealHoldable(item)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local RAI = false
SRVF:AddToggle("RAI", {
    Text = "Sync All",
    Tooltip = "Anti Invisible Gucci",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        RAI = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while RAI and task.wait(0.1) do
                    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Massless then
                            plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Massless = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local BananaAllESPColor = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255)
local BananaAllBool = false

SRVF:AddToggle("BananaAll", {
    Text = "Banana All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        BananaAllBool = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while BananaAllBool and RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() do
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local baToggle = (Toggles and Toggles.BananaAll) or (Options and Options.BananaAll)
                        if baToggle and baToggle.SetDisabled then baToggle:SetDisabled(true) end

                        local canSpawn = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
                        while not (canSpawn and canSpawn.Value) and BananaAllBool do
                            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                        end

                        SpawnToy("FoodBanana", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 20))
                        local inPlot, plotFolder = GetPlot()
                        local bananAll
                        if inPlot and plotFolder then
                            bananAll = plotFolder:WaitForChild("FoodBanana", 1)
                        else
                            local tf = getToysFolder()
                            bananAll = tf and tf:WaitForChild("FoodBanana", 1)
                        end

                        if bananAll then
                            bananAll.Name = "BananaAll67"
                            local holdPart = bananAll:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 1)
                            local holdItemRF = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 1)
                            local dropItemRF = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 1)
                            local soundPart = bananAll:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 1)
                            local ediblePart = bananAll:WaitForChild("EdiblePart", 1)

                            if holdPart and soundPart and holdItemRF and dropItemRF then
                                if ediblePart then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        holdItemRF:InvokeServer(bananAll, char)
                                        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                        if HoldEvents and HoldEvents:FindFirstChild("Use") then
                                            HoldEvents.Use:FireServer(bananAll)
                                        end
                                    end)

                                    while BananaAllBool and bananAll and bananAll:FindFirstChild("EdiblePart") and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 do
                                        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                    end

                                    RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                    pcall(function() dropItemRF:InvokeServer(bananAll) end)

                                    if bananAll and holdPart and soundPart and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                                        while BananaAllBool and soundPart and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 do
                                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
                                            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                            local partOwner = soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                            if partOwner and partOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then break end
                                        end

                                        local partOwner = soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                        if partOwner and partOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name and DestroyGrabLine then
                                            DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(soundPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                soundPart.CanCollide = false
                                local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight", bananAll)
                                highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                highlight.OutlineColor = BananaAllESPColor

                                if baToggle and baToggle.SetDisabled then baToggle:SetDisabled(false) end

                                while RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() and soundPart and BananaAllBool do
                                    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and not table.find(AllWhitelisted, plr) and plr.Character and BananaAllBool then
                                            local tHRP = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            local lHRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if tHRP and lHRP then
                                                soundPart = bananAll:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                                if soundPart then
                                                    soundPart.CFrame = tHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2, 0)
                                                    soundPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
                                                    task.wait(0.15)
                                                end
                                            elseif lHRP and BananaAllBool then
                                                soundPart = bananAll:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                                if soundPart then
                                                    soundPart.CFrame = lHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 9, 0)
                                                    soundPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                end
                                            end
                                        elseif BananaAllBool then
                                            local lHRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if lHRP then
                                                soundPart = bananAll:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                                if soundPart then
                                                    soundPart.CFrame = lHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 9, 0)
                                                    soundPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if soundPart then
                                    task.wait(0.01)
                                    soundPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -100000, 0)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            local tf = getToysFolder()
            if tf then
                for _, v in ipairs(tf:GetChildren()) do
                    if v.Name == "BananaAll67" or v.Name == "FoodBanana" then
                        DestroyT(v)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end,
}):AddColorPicker("BananaAllESPColorPicker", {
    Default = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255),
    Transparency = 0,
    Callback = function(arg) BananaAllESPColor = arg end,
})

local OvenESPColor = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255)
local AEXP = false

SRVF:AddToggle("AutoExplode", {
    Text = "Auto Explode Bombs",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        AEXP = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while AEXP and task.wait(0.1) do
                    if char then
                        local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 1)
                        local canSpawn = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
                        local inPlot = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

                        if hrp and canSpawn and canSpawn.Value and inPlot and not inPlot.Value then
                            SpawnToy("OvenDarkGray", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 20))
                            local tf = getToysFolder()
                            local oven = tf and tf:WaitForChild("OvenDarkGray", 1)
                            if oven then
                                oven.Name = "AutoExplode"
                                local buttonOven = oven:WaitForChild("ButtonOven", 1)
                                local paintPlayerPart = oven:WaitForChild("PaintPlayerPart", 1)
                                local glassWindow = oven:WaitForChild("GlassWindow", 1)

                                if buttonOven and paintPlayerPart and glassWindow then
                                    while AEXP and oven and hrp do
                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(buttonOven, buttonOven.CFrame)
                                        local partOwner = buttonOven:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                        if partOwner and partOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then break end
                                        task.wait(0.25)
                                    end

                                    local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", buttonOven)
                                    bp.Position = Vector3.new(115, 300, 332)
                                    bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                    bp.D = 100
                                    paintPlayerPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(115, 300, 332)
                                    glassWindow.CFrame = CFrame.new(115, 297, 332)
                                    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight", oven)
                                    highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                    highlight.OutlineColor = OvenESPColor

                                    while AEXP and oven:FindFirstChild("ButtonOven") and task.wait(0.05) do
                                        local bOven = oven:FindFirstChild("ButtonOven")
                                        local pPart = oven:FindFirstChild("PaintPlayerPart")
                                        local gWin = oven:FindFirstChild("GlassWindow")
                                        if not (bOven and pPart and gWin) then break end

                                        function checkAndDetonate(bomb)
                                            if not bomb then return end
                                            local part = bomb:FindFirstChild("Part") or bomb:FindFirstChild("PartHitDetector") or bomb:FindFirstChild("Balloon")
                                            if part and pPart and gWin then
                                                pPart.CFrame = part.CFrame
                                                gWin.CFrame = part.CFrame
                                                task.wait(0.025)
                                                pPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(115, 300, 332)
                                                pPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                pPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                                gWin.CFrame = CFrame.new(115, 297, 332)
                                                gWin.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                gWin.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                            end
                                        end

                                        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                                                local pToys = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                                                if pToys then
                                                    checkAndDetonate(pToys:FindFirstChild("BombMissile"))
                                                    checkAndDetonate(pToys:FindFirstChild("BombDarkMatter"))
                                                    checkAndDetonate(pToys:FindFirstChild("BombBalloon"))
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end

                                        task.wait(0.05)
                                        local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
                                        if plotItems then
                                            for _, pFolder in ipairs(plotItems:GetChildren()) do
                                                for _, bomb in ipairs(pFolder:GetChildren()) do
                                                    if bomb.Name == "BombMissile" or bomb.Name == "BombDarkMatter" or bomb.Name == "BombBalloon" then
                                                        checkAndDetonate(bomb)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            local tf = getToysFolder()
            if tf and tf:FindFirstChild("AutoExplode") then
                DestroyT(tf.AutoExplode)
            end
        end
    end,
}):AddColorPicker("OvenESPColorPicker", {
    Default = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255),
    Transparency = 0,
    Callback = function(arg) OvenESPColor = arg end,
})

SRVF:AddButton("DestroyTrain", {
    Text = "Delete Train",
    Func = function()
        local train = AlwaysTweened and AlwaysTweened:FindFirstChild("Train")
        local trainObj = train and train:FindFirstChild("Object")
        local objectModel = trainObj and trainObj:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

        if objectModel and objectModel:FindFirstChild("Seat") and char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and char.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
            local avToggle = (Toggles and Toggles.Levius_AntiVoid) or (Options and Options.Levius_AntiVoid)
            if avToggle then avToggle:SetValue(true) else AntiState.AntiVoid = true end

            local savedCF = char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            while true do
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                if objectModel:FindFirstChild("Seat") and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                    objectModel.Seat:Sit(char.Humanoid)
                end
                local seated = objectModel:FindFirstChild("Seat") and objectModel.Seat.Occupant == char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if seated or not char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or char.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then break end
            end

            local canSpawn = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
            while not (canSpawn and canSpawn.Value) do
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end

            SpawnToy("FoodHamburger", CFrame.new(0, 100, 0))
            local tf = getToysFolder()
            local burger = tf and tf:WaitForChild("FoodHamburger", 1)
            if burger then
                local holdPart = burger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 1)
                local holdItemRF = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 1)
                if holdItemRF and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and char.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    pcall(function() holdItemRF:InvokeServer(burger, char) end)
                    task.wait(0.25)
                    char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -300, 0)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    char.Humanoid.Jump = true
                    RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                    char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = savedCF
                    char.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    char.HumanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                DestroyT(burger)
            end
        else
            Library:Notify({ Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Train is Deleted or Not Found", Time = 1.5, BigIcon = "info" })
        end
    end,
})

local LineLagDetector = true
local LineLagDetectorTimeOut = false
local LastLineLagger = nil

if CreateGrabLine and CreateGrabLine:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
    CreateGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg, arg2, arg3)
        if LineLagDetector and not LineLagDetectorTimeOut and typeof(arg3) == "CFrame" and Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation") and (Workspace.SpawnLocation.Position - arg3.Position).Magnitude > 100000 and Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg)) then
            LineLagDetectorTimeOut = true
            local p = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg))
            if p and LastLineLagger ~= p then
                LastLineLagger = p
                Library:Notify({
                    Title = "LEVIUS",
                    Description = "Line Lag Detected\nPlayer: " .. p.DisplayName .. " (" .. p.Name .. ")\nPart: " .. tostring(arg2) .. "\nCFrame: (" .. math.round(arg3.Position.X) .. ", " .. math.round(arg3.Position.Y) .. ", " .. math.round(arg3.Position.Z) .. ")",
                    Time = 3,
                    BigIcon = "triangle-alert",
                })
            end
            task.wait(3.3)
            LineLagDetectorTimeOut = false
            LastLineLagger = nil
        end
    end)
end

SRVL:AddToggle("DetectLineLagToggle", {
    Text = "Detect Line Lag",
    Default = true,
    Callback = function(arg) LineLagDetector = arg end,
})

SRVL:AddDivider()

local KeepShurikens = false
local ShurikensCount = 10
local ShurikensNow = {}

function StickAll()
    local ksToggle = (Toggles and Toggles.KeepShurikensToggle) or (Options and Options.KeepShurikensToggle)
    if ksToggle then ksToggle:SetValue(false) end
    task.wait(0.01)

    local tf = getToysFolder()
    if tf then
        for _, v in ipairs(tf:GetChildren()) do
            if v.Name == "ShurikensToKeep" then
                v.Name = "Sticked"
                local stickyPart = v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                if stickyPart and Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation") then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        stickyPart.Parent = nil
                        StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, Workspace.SpawnLocation, CFrame.new(2.3e9, math.random(0, 111), 2.3e9))
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

SRVL:AddSlider("ShurikensCountSlider", {
    Text = "Shurikens Count",
    Default = 10,
    Min = 1,
    Max = 40,
    Rounding = 0,
    Compact = true,
    Callback = function(arg) ShurikensCount = arg end,
})

SRVL:AddToggle("KeepShurikensToggle", {
    Text = "Keep Shurikens",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        KeepShurikens = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while KeepShurikens and task.wait() do
                    table.clear(ShurikensNow)
                    local tf = getToysFolder()
                    if tf then
                        for _, v in ipairs(tf:GetChildren()) do
                            if v.Name == "ShurikensToKeep" then
                                table.insert(ShurikensNow, v)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if #ShurikensNow < ShurikensCount then
                        SpawnShuriken("ShurikensToKeep")
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            table.clear(ShurikensNow)
        end
    end,
})

    Text = "Stick All [Lag]",
    Func = function()
        Library:Notify({ Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Sticked All", Time = 1.5, BigIcon = "info" })
        StickAll()
    end,
})

local AntiDetectPackets = false
local PacketLagg = false
local PacketMultipler = 50000
local PacketInterval = 1
local PacketText = "sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 "
local PacketDetector = false
local PacketsNotifyTimeOut = false
local LastPacketsSource = nil
local PacketInfo

if ExtendGrabLine and ExtendGrabLine:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
    ExtendGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg, arg2)
        if PacketDetector and not PacketsNotifyTimeOut and arg and Players:FindFirstChild(arg.Name) then
            local p = Players:FindFirstChild(arg.Name)
            if p == LocalPlayer or LastPacketsSource == p then return end

            if type(arg2) == "string" then
                LastPacketsSource = p
                PacketsNotifyTimeOut = true
                Library:Notify({
                    Title = "LEVIUS",
                    Time = 3,
                    BigIcon = "triangle-alert",
                    Description = "Packets Detected\nPlayer: " .. p.DisplayName .. " (" .. p.Name .. ")\nPacket Size: " .. tostring(math.round(string.len(arg2) / 1048576 * 1000) / 1000) .. " MB\nPacket Args: " .. tostring(arg2):sub(1, 40) .. "...",
                })
            elseif type(arg2) == "table" then
                LastPacketsSource = p
                PacketsNotifyTimeOut = true
                local totalSize = CountSize(arg2)
                Library:Notify({
                    Title = "LEVIUS",
                    Time = 3,
                    BigIcon = "triangle-alert",
                    Description = "Packets Detected\nPlayer: " .. p.DisplayName .. " (" .. p.Name .. ")\nPacket Size: " .. tostring(math.round(totalSize / 1048576 * 1000) / 1000) .. " MB",
                })
            end

            task.wait(3.3)
            LastPacketsSource = nil
            PacketsNotifyTimeOut = false
        end
    end)
end

SRVP:AddInput("PacketMSG", {
    Numeric = false,
    Finished = false,
    ClearTextOnFocus = false,
    Text = "Packet Args",
    Tooltip = "Send Packet With Custom Args",
    Placeholder = "Message Here...",
    Default = "sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 sex 💖 ",
    Callback = function(arg)
        PacketText = arg
        if PacketInfo then
            PacketInfo:SetText("Packet Size: " .. math.round(string.len(PacketText) * PacketMultipler / 1048576 * 1000) / 1000 .. " MB")
        end
    end,
})

SRVP:AddSlider("PacketStrength", {
    Text = "Packet Amount",
    Default = 50000,
    Min = 1,
    Max = 100000,
    Rounding = 0,
    Compact = true,
    Callback = function(arg)
        PacketMultipler = arg
        if PacketInfo then
            PacketInfo:SetText("Packet Size: " .. math.round(string.len(PacketText) * PacketMultipler / 1048576 * 1000) / 1000 .. " MB")
        end
    end,
})

SRVP:AddSlider("PacketsInterval", {
    Text = "Send Interval",
    Default = 1,
    Min = 0.01,
    Max = 1,
    Rounding = 2,
    Compact = true,
    Callback = function(arg) PacketInterval = arg end,
})

PacketInfo = SRVP:AddLabel("Packet Size: " .. math.round(string.len(PacketText) * PacketMultipler / 1048576 * 1000) / 1000 .. " MB")

SRVP:AddCheckbox("AntiDetectToggle", {
    Text = "Anti Detect",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg) AntiDetectPackets = arg end,
})

SRVP:AddToggle("PacketLag", {
    Text = "Send Packets",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        PacketLagg = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while PacketLagg and task.wait(PacketInterval) do
                    if ExtendGrabLine then
                        if AntiDetectPackets then
                            ExtendGrabLine:FireServer({ pitux = { string.rep(PacketText, PacketMultipler) } })
                        else
                            ExtendGrabLine:FireServer(string.rep(PacketText, PacketMultipler))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
}):AddKeyPicker("TogglePacketLag", {
    Default = "None",
    Callback = function()
        local plToggle = (Toggles and Toggles.PacketLag) or (Options and Options.PacketLag)
        if plToggle then
            plToggle:SetValue(not PacketLagg)
            Library:Notify({ Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Packet Lag " .. (PacketLagg and "On" or "Off"), Time = 1.5, BigIcon = "info" })
        end
    end,
})

SRVP:AddButton("SendPacketMessage", {
    Text = "Send Packet",
    Func = function()
        if ExtendGrabLine then
            if AntiDetectPackets then
                ExtendGrabLine:FireServer({ pitux = { string.rep(PacketText, PacketMultipler) } })
            else
                ExtendGrabLine:FireServer(string.rep(PacketText, PacketMultipler))
            end
        end
    end,
})

SRVP:AddDivider()

    Text = "Detect Packets",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg) PacketDetector = arg end,
})

local SelectedChatSounds = {}
