MSGSPAM:AddToggle("SoundSpamToggle", {
    Text = "Sound Spam",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        SoundSpamEnabled = arg
        if SoundSpamEnabled then
            pcall(function()
                local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
                local expChat = coreGui:FindFirstChild("ExperienceChat")
                local appLayout = expChat and expChat:FindFirstChild("appLayout")
                local chatWin = appLayout and appLayout:FindFirstChild("chatWindow")
                local contentFrame = chatWin and chatWin:FindFirstChild("contentFrame")
                local scrollingView = contentFrame and contentFrame:FindFirstChild("scrollingView")
                local bottomLocked = scrollingView and scrollingView:FindFirstChild("bottomLockedScrollView")
                local scrollView = bottomLocked and bottomLocked:FindFirstChild("scrollView")
                if scrollView then
                    ClearChatErrors = scrollView.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                        if string.find(child.Name, "0-{", 1, true) then
                            child.Visible = false
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                while SoundSpamEnabled and task.wait() do
                    for soundKey, active in pairs(SelectedChatSounds) do
                        if active then
                            task.spawn(function()
                                pcall(function()
                                    if TextChatService and TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels") and TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral") then
                                        TextChatService.TextChannels.RBXGeneral:SendAsync("/w ' " .. soundKey)
                                    end
                                end)
                            end)
                            task.wait(SoundSpamInterval)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if ClearChatErrors then
                ClearChatErrors:Disconnect()
                ClearChatErrors = nil
            end
        end
    end,
})

-- Initialize optional map tools separately so missing map objects never block LEVIUS startup.
task.defer(function()
    local ok, err = xpcall(function()
        local MO = Tabs.Server:AddRightGroupbox("Map Objects", "file-box")

local SelectedMapParts           = States.Server.SelectedMapParts
local ShurikenBreakers           = States.Server.ShurikenBreakers
local WatchForGrabParts          = nil
local BreakMapPartsMode          = "Noclip"
local SelectedBreakMapPartsColor = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255)
local BreakObjectShurikens       = States.Server.BreakObjectShurikens
local TsunamiShurikensNow        = States.Server.TsunamiShurikensNow

local TsunamiButton
local BreakObjectButton

MapPath       = MapPath or Workspace:WaitForChild("Map", 8)
if not MapPath then error("Map not found (optional Map Objects skipped)") end
AlwaysTweened = AlwaysTweened or MapPath:WaitForChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects", 8)
if not AlwaysTweened then error("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects not found (optional Map Objects skipped)") end
local caveCart = AlwaysTweened:WaitForChild("CaveCart", 8)
if not caveCart then error("CaveCart not found (optional Map Objects skipped)") end
local SelectedMapObject = caveCart:WaitForChild("Object", 8)
if not SelectedMapObject then error("CaveCart Object not found (optional Map Objects skipped)") end

local SelectedMapPartsLabel = MO:AddLabel("Selected Parts: 0")

MO:AddDropdown("BreakMapPartsModeDropdown", {
    Text    = "Break Mode",
    Values  = { "Noclip", "Kick" },
    Default = "Noclip",
    Multi   = false,
    Callback = function(value) BreakMapPartsMode = value end,
})

MO:AddLabel("Selected Parts Color"):AddColorPicker("BreakMapPartsColor", {
    Default      = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255),
    Transparency = 0,
    Callback     = function(value) SelectedBreakMapPartsColor = value end,
})

MO:AddButton({
    Text = "Break Part",
    Func = function()
        Library:Notify({
            Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Grab Part", Time = 1.5, BigIcon = "info",
        })

        if WatchForGrabParts then WatchForGrabParts:Disconnect() end

        WatchForGrabParts = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name ~= "GrabParts" then return end
            local part1 = child.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1
            if not (part1 and part1:IsA("Part") and part1.Anchored
                    and not part1:FindFirstChild("SelectedToBreak")) then
                return
            end

            local sb               = Instance.new("SelectionBox", part1)
            sb.Adornee             = part1
            sb.LineThickness       = 0
            sb.SurfaceColor3       = SelectedBreakMapPartsColor
            sb.SurfaceTransparency = 0.75
            sb.Name                = "SelectedToBreak"

            table.insert(SelectedMapParts, part1)
            SelectedMapPartsLabel:SetText("Selected Parts: " .. #SelectedMapParts)

            if WatchForGrabParts then
                WatchForGrabParts:Disconnect()
                WatchForGrabParts = nil
            end

            local shuriken = SpawnShuriken("BreakMapPartsShuriken")
            if not shuriken then return end

            if BreakMapPartsMode == "Noclip" then
                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(
                    shuriken.StickyPart, part1,
                    CFrame.new(2.3e12, 2.3e12, 2.3e12)
                )
            else
                StickyPartEvent:FireServer(
                    shuriken.StickyPart, part1,
                    CFrame.new(0, -2.3e9, 0)
                )
            end

            table.insert(ShurikenBreakers, shuriken)
            shuriken.Parent = nil
        end)
    end,
}):AddButton({
    Text = "Fix All",
    Func = function()
        for _, part in SelectedMapParts do
            if part:FindFirstChild("SelectedToBreak") then
                part.SelectedToBreak:Destroy()
            end
        end
        for _, sh in ShurikenBreakers do DestroyT(sh) end
        table.clear(SelectedMapParts)
        SelectedMapPartsLabel:SetText("Selected Parts: 0")
    end,
})

MO:AddDivider()

TsunamiButton = MO:AddButton({
    Text = "Flood",
    Func = function()
        if BreakObjectButton then BreakObjectButton:SetDisabled(true) end
        TsunamiButton:SetDisabled(true)
        Library:Notify({
            Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Wait For Shurikens Spawn",
            Time = 3, BigIcon = "info",
        })

        while #TsunamiShurikensNow < 10 and RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() do
            table.clear(TsunamiShurikensNow)
            local toysFolder = getToysFolder()
            if toysFolder then
                for _, v in toysFolder:GetChildren() do
                    if v.Name == "TsunamiShurikens" then
                        table.insert(TsunamiShurikensNow, v)
                    end
                end
            end
            if #TsunamiShurikensNow < 10 then
                SpawnShuriken("TsunamiShurikens")
            end
        end

        local oceanObject = AlwaysTweened:WaitForChild("Ocean"):WaitForChild("Object")
        local objectModel = oceanObject:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
        if objectModel then
            local target
            for _, p in objectModel:GetChildren() do
                if p.Size == Vector3.new(1680, 80, 1712) then
                    target = p; break
                end
            end

            local toysFolder = getToysFolder()
            if toysFolder and target then
                for _, v in toysFolder:GetChildren() do
                    if v.Name == "TsunamiShurikens" then
                        local sp = v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                        if sp then
                            sp.Parent = nil
                            StickyPartEvent:FireServer(
                                sp, target,
                                CFrame.new(-2.3e9, 2.3e9, -2.3e9)
                            )
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        table.clear(TsunamiShurikensNow)
        task.wait(3)
        TsunamiButton:SetDisabled(false)
        if BreakObjectButton then BreakObjectButton:SetDisabled(false) end
    end,
})

MO:AddDivider()

MO:AddDropdown("BreakMapObjectDropdown", {
    Text    = "Select Object",
    Values  = { "CaveCart", "InnerUFO", "OuterUFO", "LrgDebris", "LrgDebris2", "SmlDebris", "SmlDebris2" },
    Default = "CaveCart",
    Multi   = false,
    Callback = function(value)
        local obj = AlwaysTweened:FindFirstChild(value)
        if obj and obj:FindFirstChild("Object") then
            SelectedMapObject = obj.Object
        end
    end,
})

BreakObjectButton = MO:AddButton({
    Text = "Break Object",
    Func = function()
        if TsunamiButton then TsunamiButton:SetDisabled(true) end
        BreakObjectButton:SetDisabled(true)
        Library:Notify({
            Title = "LEVIUS", Description = "Wait For Shurikens Spawn",
            Time = 3, BigIcon = "info",
        })

        if not char then
            BreakObjectButton:SetDisabled(false)
            if TsunamiButton then TsunamiButton:SetDisabled(false) end
            return
        end

        if SelectedMapObject and SelectedMapObject:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel") then
            if SelectedMapObject:FindFirstChild("FollowThisPart") then
                SelectedMapObject.FollowThisPart:Destroy()
            end

            local objectModel = SelectedMapObject:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
            local _, hrp, hum = GetHRH(char)

            if hrp and hum then
                local target
                for _, p in objectModel:GetChildren() do
                    if (p:IsA("Part") or p:IsA("MeshPart")) and p.CanQuery then
                        target = p; break
                    end
                end

                if target then
                    local savedCF = hrp.CFrame
                    local timeout = tick()
                    local own

                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        Teleport(hrp, target.CFrame, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                        own = Ownerboat(target)
                        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not hrp or not target
                            or (hrp.Position - target.Position).Magnitude > 20
                            or tick() - timeout > 3 or own then
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if own then
                        Teleport(hrp, savedCF, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))

                        while #BreakObjectShurikens < 10 and task.wait() do
                            table.clear(BreakObjectShurikens)
                            local toysFolder = getToysFolder()
                            if toysFolder then
                                for _, v in toysFolder:GetChildren() do
                                    if v.Name == "BreakObjectShurikens" then
                                        table.insert(BreakObjectShurikens, v)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if #BreakObjectShurikens < 10 then
                                SpawnShuriken("BreakObjectShurikens")
                            end
                        end

                        local toysFolder = getToysFolder()
                        if toysFolder then
                            for _, v in toysFolder:GetChildren() do
                                if v.Name == "BreakObjectShurikens" then
                                    local sp = v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                                    if sp then
                                        StickyPartEvent:FireServer(sp, target, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        Teleport(hrp, savedCF, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(3)
        table.clear(BreakObjectShurikens)
        BreakObjectButton:SetDisabled(false)
        if TsunamiButton then TsunamiButton:SetDisabled(false) end
    end,
})

MO:AddLabel(
    "If objects don't fall, try kick all, and massless grab should also be enabled!",
    true
)

    end, debug.traceback)
    if not ok then
        warn("[LEVIUS Map Objects] Optional map UI failed: " .. tostring(err))
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- LEVIUS Visual: BlackHole / Camera / ESP Toys / Game Sounds
-- Integrated into the existing Obsidian window (no duplicate UI)
-- ============================================================
do
    local visualOK, visualError = xpcall(function()
    local Visual = Tabs.VISUAL
    local PlayersVisual = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunVisual = game:GetService("RunService")
    local TweenVisual = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local LightingVisual = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local MeVisual = PlayersVisual.LocalPlayer

    -- ============================================================
    -- LEVIUS VISUAL | PCLD View + Players ESP
    -- Replaces the previous Toggles group. Scoped to avoid global conflicts.
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local P = game:GetService("Players")
        local T = game:GetService("TweenService")
        local me = P.LocalPlayer
        local ESPModule = {
            Config = {
                PCLDEnabled = false,
                PCLDColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                PCLDTransparency = 0.75,
                PCLDSelectionBox = false,
                PCLDSelectionBoxColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                ESPName = false,
                ESPTrace = false,
                ESPPlayerColor = Color3.fromRGB(237, 198, 255),
                ESPTargetColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
                ESPWhitelistColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
            },
            Targets = {}, Whitelisted = {},
        }
        local pcldObjects, playerObjects, connections = {}, {}, {}
        local destroyed = false

        local function contains(list, value)
            for _, item in ipairs(list) do
                if item == value then return true end
            end
            return false
        end
        local function isTarget(plr)
            return contains(ESPModule.Targets, plr)
                or (States.Dragonic and States.Dragonic.SelectedPlayer == plr.Name)
        end
        local function getColor(plr)
            if isTarget(plr) then return ESPModule.Config.ESPTargetColor end
            if contains(ESPModule.Whitelisted, plr) then return ESPModule.Config.ESPWhitelistColor end
            return ESPModule.Config.ESPPlayerColor
        end
        local function cleanupPCLD(part)
            local data = pcldObjects[part]
            if not data then return end
            if data.moved then data.moved:Disconnect() end
            if data.ancestry then data.ancestry:Disconnect() end
            if data.tween then data.tween:Cancel() end
            if data.clone then data.clone:Destroy() end
            pcldObjects[part] = nil
        end
        local function updatePCLD(part)
            if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or part.Name ~= "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then return end
            local conf = ESPModule.Config
            if not conf.PCLDEnabled or not part.Parent then
                cleanupPCLD(part)
                return
            end
            local data = pcldObjects[part]
            if not data then
                local clone = part:Clone()
                clone:ClearAllChildren()
                clone.Name = "ClonedPCLD"
                clone.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                clone.Anchored = true
                clone.CanCollide = false
                clone.CanTouch = false
                clone.CanQuery = false
                clone.CFrame = part.CFrame
                clone.Parent = part
                data = {clone = clone}
                pcldObjects[part] = data
                data.moved = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame"):Connect(function()
                    if destroyed or not clone.Parent then return end
                    if data.tween then data.tween:Cancel() end
                    data.tween = T:Create(clone, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = part.CFrame})
                    data.tween:Play()
                end)
                data.ancestry = part.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                    if not parent then cleanupPCLD(part) end
                end)
            end
            local clone = data.clone
            clone.Color = conf.PCLDColor
            clone.Transparency = conf.PCLDTransparency
            local selection = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("SelectionBox")
            if conf.PCLDSelectionBox then
                if not selection then
                    selection = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                    selection.Name = "PCLDSelectionBox"
                    selection.Adornee = clone
                    selection.Parent = clone
                end
                selection.Color3 = conf.PCLDSelectionBoxColor
                selection.LineThickness = 0.01
            elseif selection then
                selection:Destroy()
            end
        end
        function ESPModule:UpdateAllPCLD()
            for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do updatePCLD(child) end
            for part in pairs(pcldObjects) do
                if not part.Parent or not self.Config.PCLDEnabled then cleanupPCLD(part) end
            end
        end
        function ESPModule:SetPCLDEnabled(v)
            self.Config.PCLDEnabled = v == true
            self:UpdateAllPCLD()
        end
        function ESPModule:SetPCLDColor(v) self.Config.PCLDColor = v; self:UpdateAllPCLD() end
        function ESPModule:SetPCLDTransparency(v) self.Config.PCLDTransparency = math.clamp(v, 0, 1); self:UpdateAllPCLD() end
        function ESPModule:SetPCLDSelectionBox(v) self.Config.PCLDSelectionBox = v == true; self:UpdateAllPCLD() end
        function ESPModule:SetPCLDSelectionBoxColor(v) self.Config.PCLDSelectionBoxColor = v; self:UpdateAllPCLD() end

        local function cleanupPlayer(plr)
            local data = playerObjects[plr]
            if not data then return end
            if data.name then data.name:Destroy() end
            if data.beam then data.beam:Destroy() end
            if data.attachment then data.attachment:Destroy() end
            playerObjects[plr] = nil
        end
        local function updatePlayer(plr)
            if plr == me then return end
            local c = ESPModule.Config
            local character = plr.Character
            local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local targetRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local myChar = me.Character
            local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not (c.ESPName or c.ESPTrace) or not character or not character.Parent then
                cleanupPlayer(plr)
                return
            end
            local data = playerObjects[plr]
            if data and data.character ~= character then cleanupPlayer(plr); data = nil end
            if not data then
                data = {character = character}
                playerObjects[plr] = data
            end
            if c.ESPName and head then
                if not data.name then
                    local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    gui.Name = "PlayersESPName"
                    gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    gui.Adornee = head
                    gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 30)
                    gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 2.75, 0)
                    gui.Parent = character
                    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    label.Name = "TextLabel"
                    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    label.TextScaled = true
                    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    label.Parent = gui
                    data.name = gui
                end
                local label = data.name:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                if label then
                    local suffix = isTarget(plr) and " [Target]" or (contains(ESPModule.Whitelisted, plr) and " [Whitelisted]" or "")
                    label.Text = plr.DisplayName .. " [" .. plr.Name .. "]" .. suffix
                    label.TextColor3 = getColor(plr)
                end
            elseif data.name then data.name:Destroy(); data.name = nil end

            if c.ESPTrace and targetRoot and myRoot then
                if not data.beam or not data.beam.Parent or not data.attachment or data.attachment.Parent ~= targetRoot then
                    if data.beam then data.beam:Destroy() end
                    if data.attachment then data.attachment:Destroy() end
                    local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
                    a.Name = "PlayersESPTraceAttachment"
                    a.Parent = targetRoot
                    local myAttachment = myRoot:FindFirstChild("LEVIUS_ESPOrigin")
                    if not myAttachment then
                        myAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                        myAttachment.Name = "LEVIUS_ESPOrigin"
                        myAttachment.Parent = myRoot
                    end
                    local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                    beam.Name = "PlayersESPTrace"
                    beam.Attachment0 = myAttachment
                    beam.Attachment1 = a
                    beam.Width0 = 0.05
                    beam.Width1 = 0.05
                    beam.FaceCamera = true
                    beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
                    beam.Texture = "rbxassetid://8933346550"
                    beam.Parent = targetRoot
                    data.beam, data.attachment = beam, a
                end
                local myAttachment = myRoot:FindFirstChild("LEVIUS_ESPOrigin")
                data.beam.Attachment0 = myAttachment
                data.beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(getColor(plr))
            else
                if data.beam then data.beam:Destroy(); data.beam = nil end
                if data.attachment then data.attachment:Destroy(); data.attachment = nil end
            end
        end
        function ESPModule:UpdatePlayers()
            for _, plr in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do updatePlayer(plr) end
        end
        function ESPModule:SetESPName(v) self.Config.ESPName = v == true; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:SetESPTrace(v) self.Config.ESPTrace = v == true; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:SetESPPlayerColor(v) self.Config.ESPPlayerColor = v; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:SetESPTargetColor(v) self.Config.ESPTargetColor = v; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:SetESPWhitelistColor(v) self.Config.ESPWhitelistColor = v; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:SetTargets(list) self.Targets = list or {}; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:SetWhitelisted(list) self.Whitelisted = list or {}; self:UpdatePlayers() end
        function ESPModule:AddTarget(v) if not contains(self.Targets, v) then table.insert(self.Targets, v) end end
        function ESPModule:AddWhitelisted(v) if not contains(self.Whitelisted, v) then table.insert(self.Whitelisted, v) end end
        function ESPModule:EnableAll()
            self:SetPCLDEnabled(true)
            self:SetESPName(true)
            self:SetESPTrace(true)
        end
        function ESPModule:DisableAll()
            self:SetPCLDEnabled(false)
            self:SetESPName(false)
            self:SetESPTrace(false)
        end
        function ESPModule:Destroy()
            destroyed = true
            self:DisableAll()
            for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
            for plr in pairs(playerObjects) do cleanupPlayer(plr) end
            for part in pairs(pcldObjects) do cleanupPCLD(part) end
            LeviusShared.ESPModule = nil
        end

        table.insert(connections, workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if ESPModule.Config.PCLDEnabled then updatePCLD(child) end
        end))
        table.insert(connections, P.PlayerRemoving:Connect(cleanupPlayer))
        table.insert(connections, P.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
            table.insert(connections, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if not destroyed then task.defer(function() ESPModule:UpdatePlayers() end) end
            end))
        end))
        for _, plr in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= me then
                table.insert(connections, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                    if not destroyed then task.defer(function() ESPModule:UpdatePlayers() end) end
                end))
            end
        end
        table.insert(connections, me.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            if not destroyed then task.defer(function() ESPModule:UpdatePlayers() end) end
        end))
        -- One bounded refresh loop, only active for Players ESP; no duplicate loops.
        task.spawn(function()
            while not destroyed do
                if ESPModule.Config.ESPName or ESPModule.Config.ESPTrace then
                    local ok, err = pcall(function() ESPModule:UpdatePlayers() end)
                    if not ok then warn("[LEVIUS ESP] " .. tostring(err)) end
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
        end)
        LeviusShared.ESPModule = ESPModule

        -- The old VISUAL Toggles tab was removed; these are the replacement controls.
        local ESPGroup = Tabs.VISUAL:AddLeftGroupbox("ESP", "eye")
        ESPGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_PCLDView", {
            Text = "PCLD View", Default = false,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetPCLDEnabled(v) end,
        }):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_PCLDColor", {
            Title = "PCLD Color", Default = ESPModule.Config.PCLDColor,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetPCLDColor(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddSlider("LEVIUS_PCLDTransparency", {
            Text = "PCLD Transparency", Default = 0.75,
            Min = 0, Max = 1, Rounding = 2,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetPCLDTransparency(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_PCLDSelectionBox", {
            Text = "PCLD Selection Box", Default = false,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetPCLDSelectionBox(v) end,
        }):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_PCLDSelectionBoxColor", {
            Title = "Selection Box Color", Default = ESPModule.Config.PCLDSelectionBoxColor,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetPCLDSelectionBoxColor(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddDivider()
        ESPGroup:AddLabel("Player Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_PlayerESPColor", {
            Default = ESPModule.Config.ESPPlayerColor,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetESPPlayerColor(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_ESPName", {
            Text = "ESP Name", Default = false,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetESPName(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_ESPTrace", {
            Text = "ESP Trace", Default = false,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetESPTrace(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddLabel("Target Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_ESPTargetColor", {
            Default = ESPModule.Config.ESPTargetColor,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetESPTargetColor(v) end,
        })
        ESPGroup:AddLabel("Whitelist Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_ESPWhitelistColor", {
            Default = ESPModule.Config.ESPWhitelistColor,
            Callback = function(v) ESPModule:SetESPWhitelistColor(v) end,
        })
    end


    local BlackHoleGroup = Visual:AddLeftGroupbox("BlackHole", "moon-star")
    local CameraGroup = Visual:AddLeftGroupbox("Camera", "camera")
    local ESPToysGroup = Visual:AddRightGroupbox("ESP Toys", "box")
    local GameSoundsGroup = Visual:AddRightGroupbox("Game Sounds", "volume-2")

    local soundIds = {
        ["No Sound"] = nil,
        ["Apple Pay"] = "rbxassetid://114973877940754",
        ["Steam notification"] = "rbxassetid://195868961",
        Resonance = "rbxassetid://97643101798871",
        ["CS:GO Kill"] = "rbxassetid://113740359043835",
        ["CS:GO HeadShot"] = "rbxassetid://5764885315",
        Bonk = "rbxassetid://137041944943141",
        ["The Worst"] = "rbxassetid://96867813755421",
    }
    local selectedSound, notifyTime, notifyEnabled = nil, 5, false
    local KickNotifyState = States.KickNotify
    KickNotifyState.Enabled = false
    KickNotifyState.SoundId = nil
    local showHoleHighlight, showHoleName, showHoleTrace = true, true, true
    local holeHighlightColor, holeNameColor, holeTraceColor = Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.new(1,1,1)
    local holeColor, holeTransparency = Color3.new(0,0,0), 0
    local customImageEnabled, holeImage = false, "rbxassetid://9920612764"
    local holeImageColor, holeImageTransparency = Color3.new(0,0,0), 0
    local stickyEnabled, blobmanEnabled = false, false
    local myStickyColor, otherStickyColor, houseStickyColor = Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.fromRGB(255,125,0), Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
    local myBlobColor, otherBlobColor, houseBlobColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,255,0), Color3.fromRGB(255,125,0), Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
    local myStickyAlpha, otherStickyAlpha, houseStickyAlpha = 0.75, 0.75, 0.75
    local myBlobAlpha, otherBlobAlpha, houseBlobAlpha = 0.75, 0.75, 0.75
    local explosionsVolume, screamsVolume = 1, 1
    local thirdPersonConnection, bodyPartsConnection, activeFovTween = nil, nil, nil
    local originalCameraMode, originalZoomMax, originalZoomMin = MeVisual.CameraMode, MeVisual.CameraMaxZoomDistance, MeVisual.CameraMinZoomDistance
    local originalCameraOffset = nil
    local trackedHighlights = { StickyItemsESP = {}, BlobmanESP = {} }

    local function visualNotify(data)
        pcall(function() Library:Notify(data) end)
    end
    local function pickerAlpha(id, fallback)
        local opt = Options and Options[id]
        return opt and opt.Transparency or fallback
    end

    -- Preview playback is separate from the startup "picon" sound.
    -- Sound permissions are controlled by Roblox; inaccessible asset IDs cannot be forced to play.
    local previewSound = nil
    local function playNotifyPreview(soundId)
        if previewSound then
            pcall(function() previewSound:Stop(); previewSound:Destroy() end)
            previewSound = nil
        end
        if not soundId then return end
        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.Name = "LEVIUS_NotifySoundPreview"
        sound.SoundId = soundId
        sound.Volume = 1
        sound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        previewSound = sound
        sound.Ended:Once(function()
            if previewSound == sound then previewSound = nil end
            sound:Destroy()
        end)
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 12)
        local ok, err = pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
        if not ok then
            warn("[LEVIUS] Test Sound could not start: " .. tostring(err))
            if previewSound == sound then previewSound = nil end
            sound:Destroy()
        end
    end

    BlackHoleGroup:AddDropdown("LEVIUS_V_NotifySound", {
        Values = { "No Sound", "Apple Pay", "Steam notification", "Resonance", "CS:GO Kill", "CS:GO HeadShot", "Bonk", "The Worst" },
        Default = "No Sound", Multi = false, Text = "Notify Sound",
        Callback = function(v)
            selectedSound = soundIds[v]
            KickNotifyState.SoundId = selectedSound
            playNotifyPreview(selectedSound) -- Play selected sound immediately (Test Sound)
        end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddSlider("LEVIUS_V_NotifyTime", {
        Text = "Notify Time", Default = 5, Min = 1, Max = 10, Rounding = 0, Compact = true,
        Callback = function(v) notifyTime = v; KickNotifyState.Time = v end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_KickNotify", {
        Text = "Kick Notify", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) notifyEnabled = v; KickNotifyState.Enabled = v end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddDivider()
    BlackHoleGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_HoleHighlight", {
        Text = "Blackhole Highlight", Default = true,
        Callback = function(v) showHoleHighlight = v end,
    }):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HoleHighlightColor", {
        Default = Color3.new(1,1,1), Callback = function(v) holeHighlightColor = v end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_HoleName", {
        Text = "Blackhole Name", Default = true,
        Callback = function(v) showHoleName = v end,
    }):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HoleNameColor", {
        Default = Color3.new(1,1,1), Callback = function(v) holeNameColor = v end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_HoleTrace", {
        Text = "Blackhole Trace", Default = true,
        Callback = function(v) showHoleTrace = v end,
    }):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HoleTraceColor", {
        Default = Color3.new(1,1,1), Callback = function(v) holeTraceColor = v end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddLabel("Hole Color And Transparency"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HoleColor", {
        Default = Color3.new(0,0,0), Transparency = 0,
        Callback = function(v) holeColor = v; holeTransparency = pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_HoleColor", holeTransparency) end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddDivider()
    BlackHoleGroup:AddInput("LEVIUS_V_HoleImageInput", {
        Text = "Blackhole Image", Placeholder = "Image Link Here...", Finished = true, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if type(v) ~= "string" or not v:match("^https://") then return end
            if type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(getcustomasset) ~= "function" then
                visualNotify({Title="LEVIUS",Description="Custom image file functions unavailable",Time=3})
                return
            end
            local filename = "levius_hole_" .. tostring(os.time()) .. ".png"
            local ok, asset = pcall(function()
                writefile(filename, game:HttpGet(v))
                return getcustomasset(filename)
            end)
            if type(isfile) == "function" and type(delfile) == "function" then
                pcall(function() if isfile(filename) then delfile(filename) end end)
            end
            if ok and asset then holeImage = asset end
        end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddLabel("Blackhole Image Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HoleImageColor", {
        Default = Color3.new(0,0,0), Transparency = 0,
        Callback = function(v) holeImageColor = v; holeImageTransparency = pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_HoleImageColor", holeImageTransparency) end,
    })
    BlackHoleGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_CustomHoleImage", {
        Text = "Enable Custom Image", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) customImageEnabled = v end,
    })

    local function handleBlackHole(child)
        if not notifyEnabled or child.Name ~= "BlackHoleKick" then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local hole = child:WaitForChild("Hole", 1)
            if not hole or not hole:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            hole.Color, hole.Transparency = holeColor, holeTransparency
            local gui = hole:FindFirstChild("BillboardGui")
            if gui then
                local small = gui:FindFirstChild("Small")
                if small then small:Destroy() end
                local large = gui:FindFirstChild("Large")
                if customImageEnabled and large and large:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                    large.Image, large.ImageColor3, large.ImageTransparency = holeImage, holeImageColor, holeImageTransparency
                end
            end
            if showHoleHighlight then
                local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
                h.FillTransparency, h.OutlineColor = 1, holeHighlightColor
                h.Parent = child
            end
            local myRoot = MeVisual.Character and MeVisual.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if showHoleTrace and myRoot then
                local myAttachment = myRoot:FindFirstChild("KickNotifyAttachment")
                if not myAttachment then
                    myAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                    myAttachment.Name = "KickNotifyAttachment"
                    myAttachment.Parent = myRoot
                end
                local holeAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                holeAttachment.Parent = hole
                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                beam.Name = "BlackHoleTrace"
                beam.Attachment0, beam.Attachment1 = holeAttachment, myAttachment
                beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(holeTraceColor)
                beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
                beam.Texture = "rbxassetid://8933346550"
                beam.FaceCamera, beam.Width0, beam.Width1 = true, 0.05, 0.05
                beam.Parent = child
            end
            for _, plr in ipairs(PlayersVisual:GetPlayers()) do
                local root = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if plr ~= MeVisual and root and root.Anchored and not root:GetAttribute("LEVIUS_KickNotified") then
                    root:SetAttribute("LEVIUS_KickNotified", true)
                    if showHoleName then
                        local nameGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                        nameGui.Name = "PlayersESPName"
                        nameGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        nameGui.Adornee = hole
                        nameGui.Size = UDim2.new(5,50,1,50)
                        nameGui.Parent = child
                        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
                        label.TextScaled = true
                        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        label.TextColor3 = holeNameColor
                        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                        label.Text = plr.DisplayName .. " [" .. plr.Name .. "]"
                        label.Parent = nameGui
                    end
                    local owner = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    owner = owner and owner:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                    local lastGrabber = owner and PlayersVisual:FindFirstChild(tostring(owner.Value))
                    local suffix = lastGrabber and ("\nLast Grabber: " .. lastGrabber.DisplayName .. " [" .. lastGrabber.Name .. "]") or "\nLast Grabber: Unknown"
                    -- Notification and selected sound are handled once by the shared KickNotify listener.
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    local holeConnection = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(handleBlackHole)

    local function ownToysFolder()
        return workspace:FindFirstChild(MeVisual.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    end
    local function applyToyHighlight(model, tag, color, transparency)
        if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then return end
        local highlight = model:FindFirstChild(tag)
        if not highlight then
            highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = tag
            highlight.Parent = model
        end
        highlight.OutlineColor, highlight.FillColor = color, color
        highlight.OutlineTransparency, highlight.FillTransparency = 0, transparency
        trackedHighlights[tag][highlight] = true
    end
    local function refreshToyESP(tag)
        for _, plr in ipairs(PlayersVisual:GetPlayers()) do
            local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            if folder then
                for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if tag == "StickyItemsESP" then
                        if model:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or model.Name == "PCL anti kik" then
                            local mine = folder == ownToysFolder() or model.Name == "PCL anti kik"
                            applyToyHighlight(model, tag, mine and myStickyColor or otherStickyColor, mine and myStickyAlpha or otherStickyAlpha)
                        end
                    elseif model:IsA("Model") and model.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                        local mine = folder == ownToysFolder()
                        applyToyHighlight(model, tag, mine and myBlobColor or otherBlobColor, mine and myBlobAlpha or otherBlobAlpha)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
        if plots then
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                for _, model in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
                    if tag == "StickyItemsESP" then
                        if model:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or model.Name == "PCL anti kik" then
                            applyToyHighlight(model, tag, houseStickyColor, houseStickyAlpha)
                        end
                    elseif model:IsA("Model") and model.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                        applyToyHighlight(model, tag, houseBlobColor, houseBlobAlpha)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function clearToyESP(tag)
        for highlight in pairs(trackedHighlights[tag]) do
            if highlight and highlight.Parent then highlight:Destroy() end
            trackedHighlights[tag][highlight] = nil
        end
    end
    local function runToyESP(tag, isEnabled)
        task.spawn(function()
            while isEnabled() do
                pcall(refreshToyESP, tag)
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            clearToyESP(tag)
        end)
    end
    ESPToysGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_StickyESP", {
        Text="Sticky Items ESP", Default=false,
        Callback=function(v) stickyEnabled=v; if v then runToyESP("StickyItemsESP", function() return stickyEnabled end) else clearToyESP("StickyItemsESP") end end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddLabel("Your Sticky Items Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_MyStickyColor", {
        Default=myStickyColor, Transparency=0.75,
        Callback=function(v) myStickyColor=v; myStickyAlpha=pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_MyStickyColor",myStickyAlpha) end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddLabel("Sticky Items Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_OtherStickyColor", {
        Default=otherStickyColor, Transparency=0.75,
        Callback=function(v) otherStickyColor=v; otherStickyAlpha=pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_OtherStickyColor",otherStickyAlpha) end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddLabel("House Sticky Items Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HouseStickyColor", {
        Default=houseStickyColor, Transparency=0.75,
        Callback=function(v) houseStickyColor=v; houseStickyAlpha=pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_HouseStickyColor",houseStickyAlpha) end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddDivider()
    ESPToysGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_BlobESP", {
        Text="Blobman ESP", Default=false,
        Callback=function(v) blobmanEnabled=v; if v then runToyESP("BlobmanESP", function() return blobmanEnabled end) else clearToyESP("BlobmanESP") end end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddLabel("Your Blobman ESP Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_MyBlobColor", {
        Default=myBlobColor, Transparency=0.75,
        Callback=function(v) myBlobColor=v; myBlobAlpha=pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_MyBlobColor",myBlobAlpha) end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddLabel("Blobman ESP Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_OtherBlobColor", {
        Default=otherBlobColor, Transparency=0.75,
        Callback=function(v) otherBlobColor=v; otherBlobAlpha=pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_OtherBlobColor",otherBlobAlpha) end,
    })
    ESPToysGroup:AddLabel("House Blobman ESP Color"):AddColorPicker("LEVIUS_V_HouseBlobColor", {
        Default=houseBlobColor, Transparency=0.75,
        Callback=function(v) houseBlobColor=v; houseBlobAlpha=pickerAlpha("LEVIUS_V_HouseBlobColor",houseBlobAlpha) end,
    })

    GameSoundsGroup:AddSlider("LEVIUS_V_Ambience", {
        Text="Ambience",Default=0.05,Min=0,Max=1,Rounding=2,Compact=false,
        Callback=function(v)
            local map=workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local noises=map and map:FindFirstChild("MapNoises")
            if noises then for _,part in ipairs(noises:GetChildren()) do
                local sound=part:FindFirstChild("Sound1")
                if sound and sound:IsA("Sound") then sound.Volume=v end
            end end
        end,
    })
    GameSoundsGroup:AddSlider("LEVIUS_V_Explosions", {
        Text="Explosions",Default=1,Min=0,Max=1,Rounding=2,Compact=false,
        Callback=function(v) explosionsVolume=v end,
    })
    local function setScreamVolumes()
        for _,plr in ipairs(PlayersVisual:GetPlayers()) do
            local root=plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local sound=root and root:FindFirstChild("Scream")
            if sound and sound:IsA("Sound") then sound.Volume=screamsVolume end
        end
    end
    GameSoundsGroup:AddSlider("LEVIUS_V_Screams", {
        Text="Screams",Default=1,Min=0,Max=1,Rounding=2,Compact=false,
        Callback=function(v) screamsVolume=v; setScreamVolumes() end,
    })
    local explosionConnection = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name=="Part" then
            for _,obj in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                if obj:IsA("Sound") then obj.Volume=explosionsVolume end
            end
        end
    end)
    local screamConnections = {}
    local function watchCharacter(character)
        task.spawn(function()
            local root=character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",5)
            local scream=root and root:WaitForChild("Scream",5)
            if scream and scream:IsA("Sound") then scream.Volume=screamsVolume end
        end)
    end
    local function watchPlayer(plr)
        if plr.Character then watchCharacter(plr.Character) end
        table.insert(screamConnections,plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchCharacter))
    end
    for _,plr in ipairs(PlayersVisual:GetPlayers()) do watchPlayer(plr) end
    table.insert(screamConnections,PlayersVisual.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayer))

    CameraGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_ThirdPerson", {
        Text="Unlock Third Person",Default=false,
        Callback=function(v)
            if thirdPersonConnection then thirdPersonConnection:Disconnect();thirdPersonConnection=nil end
            if v then
                MeVisual.CameraMode=Enum.CameraMode.Classic
                MeVisual.CameraMaxZoomDistance=500
                thirdPersonConnection=RunVisual.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    MeVisual.CameraMaxZoomDistance=500
                    MeVisual.CameraMode=Enum.CameraMode.Classic
                    local character=MeVisual.Character
                    if character then
                        for _,part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name~="HumanoidRootPart" and part.Name~="CamPart" then
                                part.LocalTransparencyModifier=0
                            elseif part:IsA("Accessory") and part.Name~="TypingKeyboardMyWorld" then
                                local handle=part:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                                if handle then handle.LocalTransparencyModifier=0 end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                MeVisual.CameraMaxZoomDistance=math.max(originalZoomMax,originalZoomMin)
                MeVisual.CameraMinZoomDistance=originalZoomMin
                MeVisual.CameraMode=originalCameraMode
            end
        end,
    })
    CameraGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_DisableBlur", {
        Text="Disable Ragdoll Blur",Default=false,
        Callback=function(v)
            local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
            local blur=(cam and cam:FindFirstChild("Blur")) or LightingVisual:FindFirstChild("Blur")
            if blur then blur.Enabled=not v end
        end,
    })
    CameraGroup:AddToggle("LEVIUS_V_FirstPersonBody", {
        Text="First Person Body Parts",Default=false,
        Callback=function(v)
            if bodyPartsConnection then bodyPartsConnection:Disconnect();bodyPartsConnection=nil end
            if v then
                bodyPartsConnection=RunVisual.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    local char=MeVisual.Character
                    if not char then return end
                    local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum then
                        if not originalCameraOffset then originalCameraOffset=hum.CameraOffset end
                        hum.CameraOffset=Vector3.new(0,-0.25,-1)
                    end
                    for _,part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and (part.Name=="Torso" or part.Name=="Right Arm" or part.Name=="Left Arm" or part.Name=="Right Leg" or part.Name=="Left Leg") then
                            part.LocalTransparencyModifier=0
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                local hum=MeVisual.Character and MeVisual.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and originalCameraOffset then hum.CameraOffset=originalCameraOffset end
                originalCameraOffset=nil
            end
        end,
    })
    CameraGroup:AddSlider("LEVIUS_V_FOV", {
        Text="FOV",Default=70,Min=70,Max=120,Rounding=0,Compact=false,
        Callback=function(v)
            if activeFovTween then activeFovTween:Cancel() end
            local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
            if camera then
                activeFovTween=TweenVisual:Create(camera,TweenInfo.new(1,Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{FieldOfView=v})
                activeFovTween:Play()
            end
        end,
    })

    -- Clean up listeners and ESP objects when Obsidian unloads.
    if type(Library.OnUnload) == "function" then
    Library:OnUnload(function()
        if LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter then LeviusShared.LoopKillCounter.Cleanup() end
        -- Restore the physics sender rate and disconnect Desync on unload.
        pcall(function() DesyncKill.Stop() end)
        stickyEnabled,blobmanEnabled,notifyEnabled=false,false,false
        KickNotifyState.Enabled = false
        if thirdPersonConnection then thirdPersonConnection:Disconnect() end
        if bodyPartsConnection then bodyPartsConnection:Disconnect() end
        if activeFovTween then activeFovTween:Cancel() end
        if holeConnection then holeConnection:Disconnect() end
        if explosionConnection then explosionConnection:Disconnect() end
        for _,connection in ipairs(screamConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        clearToyESP("StickyItemsESP")
        clearToyESP("BlobmanESP")
        MeVisual.CameraMode=originalCameraMode
        MeVisual.CameraMaxZoomDistance=math.max(originalZoomMax,originalZoomMin)
        MeVisual.CameraMinZoomDistance=originalZoomMin
    end)
    end
    end, debug.traceback)
    if not visualOK then
        warn("[LEVIUS Visual] Initialization failed: " .. tostring(visualError))
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "LEVIUS Visual error",
                Text = tostring(visualError):sub(1, 180),
                Duration = 12,
            })
        end)
    end
end
do
    local CHAT_MESSAGE = "LEVIUS👌😎"
    local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

    function tryTextChatService()
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
            if not channels then return false end
            local general = channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
                or channels:FindFirstChild("RBXSystem")
            if not general then return false end
            general:SendAsync(CHAT_MESSAGE)
            return true
        end)
        return ok
    end

    function tryLegacyChat()
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local defaultChat = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
            if not defaultChat then return false end
            local say = defaultChat:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
            if not say then return false end
            say:FireServer(CHAT_MESSAGE, "All")
            return true
        end)
        return ok
    end

    function tryCustomChatRemotes()
        local candidates = {}
        function scan(container, depth)
            if depth > 3 or not container then return end
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") or child:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    local n = child.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("chat") or n:find("say") or n:find("message") then
                        table.insert(candidates, child)
                    end
                elseif child:IsA("Folder") or child:IsA("Model") then
                    scan(child, depth + 1)
                end
            end
        end
        scan(ReplicatedStorage, 0)

        for _, remote in ipairs(candidates) do
            pcall(function()
                if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    remote:FireServer(CHAT_MESSAGE)
                    remote:FireServer(CHAT_MESSAGE, "All")
                    remote:FireServer("All", CHAT_MESSAGE)
                elseif remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    remote:InvokeServer(CHAT_MESSAGE)
                end
            end)
        end
        return #candidates > 0
    end

    task.delay(3, function()
        local sent = false

        if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
            sent = tryTextChatService()
        end
        if not sent then
            sent = tryLegacyChat()
        end
        if not sent then
            sent = tryCustomChatRemotes()
        end

        if Library and Library.Notify then
            Library:Notify({
                Title = "<b>LEVIUS</b>",
                Description = sent
                    and ("💬 Sent: " .. CHAT_MESSAGE)
                    or  "⚠️ Chat send blocked by game (server-side filter)",
                Time = 4,
            })
        end
    end)
end


if getgenv().LEVIUS_SettingsAutoload then
    pcall(getgenv().LEVIUS_SettingsAutoload)
end

task.delay(0.5, function()
    local antiKick2 = (Toggles and Toggles.Levius_AntiKick2) or (Options and Options.Levius_AntiKick2)
    if antiKick2 then
        antiKick2:SetValue(true)
    end
    local antiLag = (Toggles and Toggles.Levius_AntiLag) or (Options and Options.Levius_AntiLag)
    if antiLag then
        antiLag:SetValue(true)
    end
end)

LeviusShared.Prints.Credits(tick())
